The verse's *shughul* (occupation) cures world's *inshighāl* (preoccupation): world-preoccupied (anxieties: provision? status? health? — v. 44's *matāʿ*-clutching; v. 49's disputes) versus Garden-occupied (delights *absorbing* — no anxiety-possible: provision-guaranteed, v. 57; status-crowned, 76:21's bracelets; health-perfect, 56:33–34's unfailing). *"Rather than preoccupied by concerns of this world"* (source commentary): Garden-minds *freed* (no bills, bosses, illnesses, deadlines, fears) *for* (delight, devotion, discovery). The psychology inverts earthly: world-joys *interrupted* (phone-rings mid-feast, worry-shadows mid-laugh, satiety-ends mid-pleasure); Garden-joys *uninterrupted* (no suqūm-illness, 37:48's context; no laghw-vain-talk, 56:25; no taʾthīm-sin, 56:25). *Fākihūn* (rejoicing) is *ḥāḍir*-joy (present, undistracted): each delight *fully* tasted (fruit *savored*, v. 57; spouses *cherished*, v. 56; greeting *trembled-at*, v. 58). Mindfulness-masters (present-moment gurus) glimpse Garden-*shughul*: *total* absorption, *zero* distraction — Paradise as *flow*-eternal. Practices: *taste* world-joys Gardenly (eat thankfully-present, v. 35; love attentively; worship absorbedly — *iḥsān*-presence, Muslim 8) — presence-trained now, perfected then. *Release* preoccupations Gardenward (anxieties to *tawakkul*, v. 38's *taqdīr*-trust; grudges to forgiveness, v. 26's praying-while-stoned) — unburdened-now, unburdenable-then. *Shughul* — delighted. *Inshighāl* — discarded. *Fākihūn* — forever.

**Levels of Occupation: Delights Diversified**

Al-Qurṭubī's levels-principle (*different delights for different ranks*) unfolds Garden's *personalized* bliss: *physical* (feasts, v. 57; couches-shade, v. 56; spouses-pure, 2:25; rivers, 47:15; garments-silks, 76:21) — bodies perfected (strength-Mosaic, beauty-Josephite, age-thirty-three — al-Tirmidhī 2545, from Muʿādh; ḥasan — youth-eternal). *Social* (company-goodly, 4:69; conversations, 52:25; family-reunion, 52:21; markets-visited — Muslim 2833's Friday-market — fellowship-unbroken). *Intellectual* (knowledge-unveiled — *"We removed thy covering; sight piercing,"* 50:22; mysteries-answered; creation-toured). *Spiritual* (worship-voluntary-delight — *tasbīḥ* as breathing, Muslim 2835; nearness, 50:35's *mazīd*, More; pleasure-divine, 9:72's *riḍwān*-supreme; vision, 75:23 — *"absorbed in vision,"* al-Rāzī's highest-*shughul*). Each *aṣḥāb* (inhabitant) *shughul*-matched (tastes honored — v. 57's *"whatsoever they call for"* — callings-answered). No envy (ranks accepted-joyfully — *"no rancor in breasts,"* 7:43 — superiority un-resented, inferiority un-felt). The runner (vv. 20–27): *mukramīn*-ranked (v. 27: among-honored) — his *shughul*? (longing-fulfilled — *qawmī*-knowing? — converts-joining? — envision). *Thy* rank? — *determined* now (deeds, v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn*; intentions; sacrifices). *Occupy* now (Garden-earning busyness: 103:3's faith-righteousness-truth-patience) — *occupied* then (delight). *Shughul*-now → *shughul*-then. *Busy* — doubly.

**Busying for Rejoicing: Practices of the Soon-Rejoicing**

The verse converts Garden-*shughul* (occupation) into world-*shughul* (preparation). *Busy righteously*: fill hours Gardenward (prayers-five, Qur'an-portion, charity-daily, kin-weekly, counsel-continually — *shughul*-sanctified). *Idleness* (the Fire-bound's world-*shughul* — v. 49's disputes, v. 46's turnings) forfeits; *industry-for-God* inherits. The Prophet's ﷺ busyness-model: days-serving (family, community, worship), nights-praying — *shughul*-blessed. *Rejoice righteously now*: *fākihūn* (rejoicing) *previewed* — lawful joys (family, food, nature, friendship) enjoyed-thankfully (v. 35's *shukr*), worship-joys (prayer's sweetness, Qur'an's tears, charity's warmth, dawn's coolness) cultivated — world-*fākih* as Garden-*fākihūn* foretaste. *Sorrow* (trials, griefs) borne patiently (Garden-*fākihūn* as sorrow's *end* — *"no fear, no grief,"* 2:62's Garden-promise). *Envision occupationally*: picture *thy* Garden-*shughul* (what delights? — feasts? gardens? company? vision?) — envisioning *motivates* (sacrifice-now for delight-then — fasting's hunger for feast's joy, night-prayer's loss for vision's gain). The Companions *saw* (Bilāl: *"tomorrow we meet beloveds!"* — deathbed-joy; Ḥanẓalah's Uḥud-rush — wedding-night-to-martyrdom). For the verse's final mercy is its *inna* (truly): Garden-busyness *certain* (promised, v. 11's *ajr-karīm*; sworn, v. 2's Wise-Book). *Truly* — rejoicing-awaits. *Truly* — busyness-blessed. *Truly* — Day-comes (v. 54's *al-yawma*). *Busy* — now (faith-acting). *Rejoice* — then (*fākihūn*). *Truly.*

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:56

> **They and their spouses reclining upon couches in the shade.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Hum Wa Azwājuhum: Companionship Crowned**

*"They and their spouses"* — *hum wa azwājuhum* — *hum* (they): Garden's inhabitants (v. 55's *aṣḥāb*). *Az-wāj* (spouses/pairs): v. 36's *azwāj* (pairs) *perfected* — earthly-pairing (male-female, v. 36) ripened into eternal-companionship. The Qur'an's spousal-promise: *"they and their spouses"* (43:70 — *"enter the Garden, you and your spouses, rejoicing"*); *"spouses made pure"* (2:25; 3:15; 4:57 — *muṭahharah*: sinless, ageless, rancorless, defectless). *Which* spouses? — commentators' layers (all-true): *worldly* spouses (believing couples reunited — 52:21's family-joining; 13:23's *"their parents, spouses, offspring"* entering-together), *paradisal* companions (*ḥūr ʿīn*, wide-eyed — 44:54; 52:20; 56:22 — created-for-Garden, 56:35–37's *"virgin, loving, age-matched"*), or both (reunion *plus* gifting). Loneliness (v. 36's pairing-promise) *abolished*: no widows (reunion/heavenly-pairing), no bachelors (paired-perfectly), no strife (7:43's rancor-removed). The runner (v. 20): earthly-unknown (wife? — unmentioned), eternally-paired (v. 27's *mukramīn* — honored *with* spouses?). Marriage-then images marriage-now's *purpose*: companionship (*sakan*, 30:21's tranquillity) *practiced*-now, *perfected*-then. *Married-now?* — love Gardenward (kindness as *azwāj*-rehearsal). *Unmarried-now?* — hope (Pairer pairs, v. 36; Garden completes). *Hum-wa-azwājuhum* — togetherness, forever.

**Muttakiʾūn: Reclining — Rest's Posture**

*"Reclining"* — *muttakiʾūn* — *ittikāʾ* (reclining, leaning-back, lounging): utter-rest's posture — work-ended, worry-absent, comfort-total. The Qur'an's reclining-gallery: *"reclining upon couches"* (18:31; 55:54; 76:13 — Garden's signature-pose); *"reclining upon thrones"* (15:47's *surur*, thrones — *"brethren, facing"*). *"Indicating content and peace"* (al-Rāzī on 18:31; 55:54): reclining = arrival (travelers *sit* mid-journey, *recline* home). *"Some view couches as thrones, indicating high rank"* (source commentary): *arāʾik* (couches/thrones — canopied-seats) as *royalty* — Garden-dwellers *kings* (76:20: *"when thou seest, thou seest bliss and great kingdom"* — *mulk-kabīr*, great-sovereignty — each-believer crowned, 76:21's bracelets-silk). Reclining contrasts: world-postures (standing-laboring, sitting-anxious, prostrating-worshiping — all *effort*) and Fire-postures (v. 64's *burn*-writhing, 14:50's shackled-faces-covered — all *torment*). *Muttakiʾūn* is *effortlessness*: worship-then voluntary-delight (v. 55's *shughul*-joyful), needs-then served (76:15–16's circulated-vessels, 76:19's immortal-youths). The Prophet ﷺ *reclined* (worldly-foretaste —pillow-leaning, companions-gathered) yet *never* reclined-eating-arrogantly (*"I eat as servants eat,"* al-Tirmidhī — humility-now, thrones-then). *Recline-now?* — rest *in* worship (Friday-rest, night-sleep, v. 37) — restlessness (anxiety, greed) forfeits foretaste. *Recline-then* — promised (*muttakiʾūn*). *Labor-now* (v. 54's *taʿmalūn*); *lounge-then*. *Earned-rest* — sweetest.

**ʿAlā al-Arāʾiki Fī al-Ẓilāl: Couches in Shade**

*"Upon couches in the shade"* — *ʿalā al-arāʾiki fī al-ẓilāl* — *arāʾik* (couches: curtained-canopied beds/thrones — luxury-privacy). *Ẓilāl* (shades/shadows): Garden-shade — *"extending shade"* (56:30 — *ẓill-mamdūd*, shade *stretched*-eternal; cf. 56:30c's connotations: coolness, shelter, proximity-to-Mercy). Shade's Qur'anic resonance: Throne-shade (v. 11's seven-shaded, al-Bukhārī 660 — *this*-shade's Day-preview), tree-shade (Lot-tree's, 53:14–16), mercy-shade (*"shade of His mercy"*). Shade contrasts: world's sun-scorch (76:13: *"they see therein neither sun nor biting-cold"* — climate-perfected) and Fire's *ẓill*-mockery (*"shade of black smoke,"* 56:43 — *ẓill* as torment — neither-cool-nor-generous, 77:31). *Fī al-ẓilāl* (in the shades): *dwelling*-within (not visiting — *residence*). Couches-*in*-shade: double-shelter (furniture-comfort *within* climate-comfort). The imagery's *desert*-power: Arabia's noonday-furnace (travelers shade-desperate) — Garden as *eternal-oasis* (no scorch-ever). Modernity's shade-hunger (burnout-furnaces, anxiety-heat) likewise: Garden-shade as soul's *coolness* (tranquillity, *sakīnah*-eternal). *Seek shade-now* (Throne-shade deeds, v. 11: unseen-fear, secret-charity, just-rule — seven-categories) — *dwell shade-then* (*fī-al-ẓilāl*). *Shade* — mercy's climate. *Couches* — mercy's furniture. *Recline* — mercy's posture.

**Spousal Shade: Marriage Perfected**

The verse *combines* (spouses + reclining + shade): marriage's *ideal* imaged — beloved-beside, comfort-beneath, coolness-around. World-marriage strains (provision-anxieties, in-law-frictions, health-failures, rancor-accumulations — 7:43's дозу) versus Garden-marriage ease (provision-served, v. 57; company-pure, 2:25; rancor-removed, 7:43; youth-eternal, 56:37). The verse *ministers*: *married-struggling?* — persevere (struggle-now, shade-then — *"with hardship comes ease,"* 94:5–6 — ease-eternal). *Married-blissful?* — thank (bliss as *ẓilāl*-foretaste — gratitude preserves, 14:7's increase). *Marriage-seeking?* — choose Garden-compatibly (faith-first — *"marry the religious, thou prosperest,"* al-Bukhārī 5090 — world-spouse as Garden-spouse-candidate, 52:21's joining-conditioned-on-faith). *Marriage-less?* (widowed, divorced, single) — hope (Garden-pairs, v. 36's Pairer; worldly-loss compensated-eternally — *"no soul knows hidden-delights,"* 32:17). The Prophet ﷺ modeled spousal-shade (Khadījah-comforted, ʿĀʾishah-raced, Fāṭimah-cherished — home as *ẓilāl*-patch) — imitate: shade-thy-spouse (shelter-from-harshness, coolness-in-anger, rest-in-weariness). *Spouses* — Garden-together (pray-together, fast-together, forgive-together — *azwāj*-training). *Reclining-together* — then. *Striving-together* — now.

**Reclining Toward Shade: Practices of the Soon-Shaded**

The verse's repose becomes a regimen. *Earn couches*: *arāʾik* (thrones) *earned* (v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn* — deeds-currency). Throne-deeds: faith (foundation), prayer (pillars — *"coolness of my eye in prayer,"* al-Nasāʾī 3939 — prayer-rest as reclining-rehearsal), charity (v. 47's *anfiqū* — giving-now, gifted-then), character (kindness — *"heaviest on Scale,"* Abū Dāwūd 4799). *Seek shade-deeds*: seven-shaded acts (al-Bukhārī 660: just-ruler, youth-grown-worshiping, mosque-hearted, God-loving-pair, beauty-refusing-God-fearer, secret-giver, solitude-weeper) — *practice* (justice-in-miniature, worship-in-youth, mosque-love, fellowship, chastity, secrecy, tears). *Shade* (Day's) *earned*-now, *enjoyed*-then. *Rest rhythmically*: honor rest (Sabbath-principle — Friday-ease, night-sleep, v. 37; vacations-refreshing — bodies-trusts, *"thy body has right,"* al-Bukhārī 5199) — restlessness (burnout, anxiety) un-Gardenly. *Recline* (Sundays-with-family, evenings-with-Qur'an, moments-with-spouse — *ẓilāl*-sips). *Marry/mercy*: perfect spousal-shade (shelter, coolness, rest-giving — *azwāj*-as-*ẓilāl*) — and relieve all (shade-the-shadeless: orphans, poor, strangers — *"shade"* as charity-metaphor). For the verse's final mercy is its *plural* (*hum*, they): shade *shared* (spouses, company, 4:69) — solitude-unknown. *Shared*-shade now (family, fellowship, hospitality — homes as mini-*ẓilāl*) previews *shared*-shade then. *They-and-spouses* — reclining. *Shade* — eternal. *Couches* — ready. *Recline* — prepared (deeds-cushioned). *Rest* — earned. *Together* — forever.

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:57

> **Therein they have fruit and whatsoever they call for.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Lahum Fīhā Fākihah: Fruit — Paradise's Signature**

*"Therein they have fruit"* — *lahum fīhā fākihatun* — *lahum* (for them): *theirs* (possession-secured — no scarcity, no price, no season). *Fīhā* (therein): Garden-located (vv. 55–56's Garden). *Fākihah* (fruit): delight-produce — *"far superior to this world's"* (source commentary): the Prophet ﷺ reached (in prayer-vision) — *"I was shown the Garden and wanted a bunch; had I taken, you would eat while world remains"* (aḥādīth-cited) — inexhaustible-cluster! Garden-fruit's Qur'anic gallery: *"neither cut-off nor forbidden"* (56:33 — supply/permission-infinite); *"its shade eternal, its fruit"* (13:35); *"near, hanging-low"* (69:23: *quṭūfuhā-dāniyah* — reach-unstretching); *"two Gardens' fruit"* (55:52: *fākihatayn*-paired). Earth-fruit (vv. 33–35: grain, palms-grapes — toil-grown, season-bound, rot-prone) versus Garden-fruit (gift-grown, eternity-ripe, decayless). *Fākihah* puns *fākihūn* (v. 55's rejoicing): fruit *is* joy made-edible; joy, fruit made-conscious. Eaters-then: bodies-needless (no hunger-pain) yet pleasure-full (taste-perfected) — eating-as-delight (not fuel). The runner (v. 20): world-hungry? (outskirts-poor?) — Garden-sated (*mukramīn*, v. 27 — honored *feasted*). *Thy* fruit? — planted-now (v. 12's *ṣadaqah-jāriyah* as orchards; v. 35's thanks as seeds) — harvested-then (*fākihah*). *Plant* — taste-then.

**Wa Lahum Mā Yaddaʿūn: Whatsoever They Call For**

*"And whatsoever they call for"* — *wa lahum mā yaddaʿūn* — *yaddaʿūn* (call-for, claim, summon-demand): *asking*-receives. *Mā* (whatsoever): *unlimited* (any-thing, every-thing). The Qur'an's desire-gallery: *"whatsoever they call for"* (41:31; 44:55); *"what they will/desire"* (16:31; 25:16; 39:34; 41:31; 42:22; 43:71; 50:35; 52:22; 56:20–24; 77:41–44) — will-fulfilled, desire-crowned. Against: disbelievers' *"barrier between them and desired"* (34:54) — desire-denied (thirst-unslaked, 14:16–17's pus-water; escape-ungranted, 35:36). *Yaddaʿūn* (call-for) implies *vocal* requesting (utter-and-receive — servants-attending, 76:15–19; creation-obeying — *"Be!"*-like responsiveness, v. 82-echoed). Desires-then *purified* (no sinful-callings — hearts-cleansed, 7:43; no harmful — wisdom-perfected): callings *worthy* (wholesome, nobler-than-world's). Novelty-infinite (32:17's *hidden*-delights — unimagined-callings answered-unasked!). *Mā-yaddaʿūn* is *duʿāʾ*-perfected: world-*duʿāʾ* (answered-sometimes, delayed-often, wisdom-filtered) versus Garden-*iddaʿāʾ* (answered-always, instantly, superabundantly). Train calling-now (*duʿāʾ*-habitual — askers-then were askers-now): *"call upon Me, I answer"* (40:60) — practiced-callers, perfected-answered. *Call* — now-and-then.

**Desire's Redemption: From Deniers' Barrier to Believers' Bounty**

The verse's *mā-yaddaʿūn* (whatsoever-called) redeems *desire* (world-suspect: cravings condemned — 45:23's desire-god; 79:40's desire-feared-restrained). Garden-desire *crowned*: restraint-then rewarded-indulgence-now (fasters feasted — *"eat-drink for what you forwarded,"* 69:24; chaste paired — 56:35–37; misers' opposites gifted-limitlessly). The economics: desire *deferred* (world) → desire *multiplied* (Garden); desire *indulged-sinfully* (world) → desire *barred* (34:54's barrier; Fire's 14:16-thirst). The Qur'an's desire-logic: *"paradise surrounded by disliked"* (al-Bukhārī 6487 — restraint-path), *"Fire by desires"* (indulgence-path) — paths-inverted-ends: restraint-road → desire-crown; indulgence-road → desire-barrier. Deniers (v. 47's withholders, v. 48's mockers) indulged (withholding-wealth-desire, mocking-ego-desire) — barred (34:54). Believers (v. 11's fearers, v. 20's runner) restrained (feared-unseen — desire-checked; ran-truthward — comfort-foregone) — crowned (*mā-yaddaʿūn*). *Desire* is capital: *invested* (restraint) → dividends-eternal (*whatsoever*); *squandered* (sin) → bankruptcy (barrier). *Audit* desires (each-craving: invest-or-squander?) — direct (lawful-indulged-thankfully, v. 35; unlawful-restrained-hopefully, v. 57). *Redeemed*-desire — Garden's currency. *Spend* wisely.

**Fruit-and-Call: Provision's Two Modes**

The verse pairs *fākihah* (fruit — *provided*-unasked, staple-given) with *mā-yaddaʿūn* (called-for — *requested*-granted, desire-answered): provision *automatic* + provision *responsive*. Automatic (fruit-abundant — no asking-needed — grace-initiated): God's *ibtidāʾ* (origination-giving — life, Garden, fruit-before-call). Responsive (callings-answered — asking-honored): God's *ijābah* (answering — 40:60's *call-answer*). World mirrors: automatic-provision (air, rain, v. 33's grain-unasked-by-infants) + responsive (duʿāʾ-answered, effort-rewarded — v. 35's hands-worked-honored). Garden *perfects* both: automatic-superabundant (fruit-beyond-imagining, 32:17) + responsive-instant (call-receive). Gratitude's double-duty: thank automatic (unasked-gifts — *ḥamd* for being-Gardened) + thank responsive (asked-granted — *shukr* per-calling). The pairing also *honors agency*: Garden-dwellers not passive-recipients (fruit-spoonfed) but active-callers (*yaddaʿūn* — summoning!) — personality *preserved* (tastes, preferences, curiosities — callings *differ* per soul). Eternity *unboring*: callings-infinite (explore, taste, meet, learn — *mazīd*, More, 50:35 — ever-more). *Practice* both-modes now: receive-automatic thankfully (daily-provisions — *ḥamd*), request-responsive hopefully (*duʿāʾ* — ask-boldly). *Provided* — thanked. *Calling* — answered. *Forever.*

**Calling Fruitfully: Practices of the Soon-Answered**

The verse trains *calling* (duʿāʾ) as Garden-rehearsal. *Call presently*: *duʿāʾ*-habitual (morning-evening *adhkār*, pre-prayer, post-prayer, night-third — v. 37's tryst; need-moments — ask *specifically*, hopefully, persistently — *"call, I answer,"* 40:60). *Answered*-now (sometimes — gratitude) and *stored*-now (unanswered-stored as Garden-*mā-yaddaʿūn* — *"duʿāʾ stored-or-answered-or-harm-averted,"* al-Tirmidhī 3573 — nothing-wasted). *Call purely*: purify callings (lawful-requests — provision-halal, guidance, forgiveness, Garden — not sin-aids; others-included — *duʿāʾ*-for-brethren answered-doubly — *"angels: and-for-thee-like,"* Muslim 2732). *Impure*-callings (harm-wishing, sin-seeking) un-Gardenly — cleanse. *Fruit presently*: enjoy world-*fākihah* (fruits — dates-grapes, v. 34 — thankfully, v. 35; share — v. 47's *anfiqū*; plant — v. 34's orchards-ongoing). *Taste* as *tadhkirah* (reminder): each fruit — *Garden's?* (superior-awaiting — restraint-sweetened). *Fast* (hunger-now for feast-then — *"fasters' joy at breaking... and at meeting,"* al-Bukhārī 1904). For the verse's final mercy is its *lahum* (for-them — twice!): fruit-*theirs*, callings-*theirs*. *Theirs* — secured (no eviction — *"abiding eternally,"* 98:8; no expiry — v. 44's *ilā-ḥīn* ended, eternity-begun). *Secure* now (faith-deeds as title-deeds, v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn*). *Theirs* — soon (Death-to-Garden swifter-than-imagined — *"blink,"* 16:77). *Call* — answered. *Eat* — eternally. *Theirs.*

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:58

> **"Peace!" a word from a Lord most Merciful.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Salāmun Qawlan: "Peace!" — A Word**

*"Peace!"* — *salāmun* — *salām* (peace, safety, wholeness, greeting): Garden's *supreme* utterance — one word (*qawlan*, a word/saying) outweighing feasts (v. 57), couches (v. 56), rejoicing (v. 55). *Salām* as God's Name (*al-Salām*, 59:23 — Peace/Source-of-peace): *"God Himself is peace upon the Garden's people"* (Ibn ʿAbbās, Ibn Kathīr) — Speaker *is* Speech (Peace *speaking* peace). The greeting parallels: *"their greeting on meeting Him will be 'Peace'"* (33:44 — *taḥiyyatuhum... salām*); angels' *"peace upon you for patience"* (13:24); Garden's *"no vain-talk, only 'Peace! Peace!'"* (56:25–26 — *salām*-atmosphere). *Qawlan* (a word): utterance counted-singular — *one* word's weight (against volumes-worldly): God's *salām* outweighs libraries. The ḥadīth unveils the scene: *"While Garden-people bliss, light shines; they lift heads — God beholding from above — 'Peace be upon you, O Garden-people!' — that is His saying 'Peace! a word from Merciful Lord.' He looks, they look — undistracted by bliss while beholding — until unveiled, Light-and-Blessing remaining in abodes"* (Ibn Kathīr, al-Qurṭubī — from Jābir/others). *Salām* + *ruʾyah* (vision): greeting *with* beholding — word *and* Face (75:23's gazing). *One* word; *eternal* echo. *Peace!* — spoken. *Peace!* — abiding.

**Min Rabbin Raḥīm: From a Lord Most Merciful**

*"From a Lord most Merciful"* — *min rabbin raḥīm* — *Rabb* (Lord: Originator, v. 22's *faṭara*; Owner; Nurturer): Speaker-as-Lord (authority-tender). *Raḥīm* (most-Merciful: constant, intimate-mercy — v. 5's *Raḥīm*-paired-with-Mighty): Speaker-as-Merciful (tenderness-climactic). The genitive-chain (*min...* — from): *salām* sourced (not angels' — though angels greet, 13:24; not prophets' — though prophets welcome; but *Lord's-direct*). Directness *is* the honour: kings send emissaries; the King *speaks*. *Raḥīm* (not *Jabbār*, Compeller; not *Muntaqim*, Avenger — true Names, unseasonal-here): Garden-address in mercy's Name (wrath-Names sheathed — Fire-addressed separately, vv. 59–64's *"O guilty!"*). The pairing (*Rabb* + *Raḥīm*) completes the sūrah's Lord-portrait: Mighty-Merciful Revealer (v. 5), Compassionate Promiser (v. 52), Knowing Measurer (v. 38), Carrying Preserver (v. 41), Peace-Speaking Welcomer (here). *Rabb-Raḥīm* also answers deniers' *Raḥmān*-misuse (v. 15's "Compassionate-sent-nothing" — *Raḥīm*-speaks-*salām*!): the Denied-Compassionate *compensates* believers-compassionately. *From* (min): provenance-eternal (peace *stored*-heavenly, dispensed-personally). *Lord* — speaking. *Merciful* — peace-ing. *Hear* — trembling-joyful. *Abide* — peacefully. *Forever.*

**Peace as Garden's Essence and Sum**

*Salām* (peace) *sums* Garden (all prior delights *aspects* of peace): bodies-peaceful (no illness, 56:33–34's unfailing; no death — *"no death after first,"* 44:56), minds-peaceful (no anxiety — *"no fear, no grief,"* 2:62; no rancor, 7:43), hearts-peaceful (no envy, no boredom — 32:17's ever-new), relations-peaceful (spouses-pure, 2:25; company-goodly, 4:69; speech-*salām*-only, 56:26), souls-peaceful (Lord-pleased, 9:72's *riḍwān*; vision-granted, 75:23). *Salām* contrasts: world- Contention (v. 49's *yakhiṣṣimūn*, disputing; v. 77's *khaṣīm*, adversary) and Fire-torment (vv. 63–64's burning; 14:16's pus-drinking). The *salām*-word *effects* what it *utters* (divine-speech efficacious — v. 82's *"Be!"*-like): spoken-peace *makes*-peaceful (hearts calmed-eternally, wounds healed-absolutely). *"Peace!"* is thus Garden's *sacrament*: audible-grace (heard), visible-grace (Light-shining, ḥadīth), abiding-grace (*"Light-and-Blessing remain,"* ḥadīth). Al-Rāzī's hierarchy (v. 55: vision-highest-*shughul*) peaks here: *salām*-with-vision (ḥadīth's looking-while-greeted) as bliss-summit — beholding-Beauty, hearing-Peace, abiding-Light. *Below*: fruits (v. 57), couches (v. 56), rejoicing (v. 55) — *scaffolding* to *salām*. *Above*: nothing (Lord-Himself the summit — *"thy Lord's Face abides,"* 55:27). *Climb* scaffolding-now (deeds, v. 54) — *hear* summit-then (*Salām!*). *Peace!*

**The Beatific Encounter: Theology of Greeting-and-Vision**

The ḥadīth's scene (light, lifting-heads, beholding, greeting, gazing-undistracted, unveiling-abiding) encodes *ruʾyah* (vision-of-God) theology: *affirmed* (Sunnī-consensus — 75:22–23: *"faces that Day radiant, gazing at Lord"*; 83:15's veiled-deniers implying unveiled-believers; *"you will see your Lord as you see full-moon, no difficulty,"* al-Bukhārī 554; Muslim 633, from Jarīr), *modality-unasked* (*bilā-kayf* — without-how — transcendent-seeing, 42:11's *"naught like Him"* preserved), *supreme-bliss* (*"no bliss dearer than gazing,"* Muslim 181's *ziyādah*-ḥadīth — *extra* = gaze). The *salām* (greeting) *accompanies* vision (word-with-Face): God *speaks* (relational — not mute-Object) *and* shows (generous — not hidden-Subject). Undistracted-gazing (*"not distracted by bliss while looking,"* ḥadīth) reveals vision's supremacy (Garden's lesser-blisses *pale* — moon-stars at sunrise). *Unveiling-abiding* (*"Light-and-Blessing remain in abodes"*): encounter *transforms* (dwellings-irradiated, faces-luminous — 75:22's *nāḍirah*, radiant). Deniers (vv. 59–64) veiled (83:15) — vision's *absence* as Fire's essence (beyond burning: banishment). *Long* for encounter (believers' *shawq*, yearning — *"whoever loves meeting God...,"* v. 51's ḥadīth). *Prepare* eyes (lowered-now from unlawful — 24:30 — raised-then to Lawful-Beauty; wept-now in awe, v. 11 — dried-then in joy). *Greeted* — envisioned. *Peace!* — Face!

**Greeting Peace Now, Hearing Peace Then**

The verse's *salām* becomes a practice-bridge. *Spread salām now*: the Prophet's ﷺ Garden-greeting rehearsed — *"spread peace; feed; join kin; pray at night; enter Paradise in peace"* (al-Tirmidhī 2485; Ibn Mājah 1334, from ʿAbd Allāh ibn Salām; graded ṣaḥīḥ) — *salām*-spreading as Garden-entry-cause. Greet (*as-salāmu-ʿalaykum*) widely (known-unknown — al-Bukhārī 6236), warmly (smiling — charity, al-Tirmidhī 1956), firstly (initiator-superior — Abū Dāwūd 5197). *Be salām*: embody peace (tongue-harmless — *"Muslim is whom Muslims safe from tongue-hand,"* al-Bukhārī 10; heart-rancorless — 7:43-previewed; home-peaceful — *salām*-upon-entering, 24:61). *Peacemake* (49:9–10 — feuders-reconciled as *salām*-service). *Yearn for the Word*: picture *salām*-moment (light, lifting, beholding, *Salām!*) — yearning *fuels* (night-prayers for dawn-Greeting, fasts for feast-with-Greeter, tears for smiling-Meeting). The Prophet ﷺ yearned (*"O God, I ask the delight of gazing at Thy Face,"* al-Nasāʾī 1305 — graded ṣaḥīḥ) — pray likewise. For the verse's final mercy is its *qawlan* (a-word): *one* word — memorizable (even children), repeatable (each prayer-ending: *"O God, Thou art Peace, from Thee is peace,"* Muslim 592), dyable-upon (*salām*-last-word — deathbed-*salām*-hoped). *One* word — eternity's password. *Speak* it (now, to all). *Be* it (now, for all). *Hear* it (then, from All-Merciful). *Salāmun* — *qawlan* — *min Rabbin Raḥīm*. *Peace!*

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:59

> **Stand apart this Day, O guilty ones!**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wamtāzū al-Yawma: The Command of Separation**

*"Stand apart this Day"* — *wamtāzū al-yawma* — *imtāzū* (separate, stand-apart, be-distinguished): imperative of *segregation*. *Al-yawma* (this Day): Judgment-Day (v. 54's *al-yawma*, v. 55's Garden-Day — now *sorting*-Day). The Speaker: God (to the arraigned, vv. 32, 53's *muḥḍarūn*). The verse *"alludes to the separation of believers and disbelievers on Judgment Day"* (source commentary): the *jamīʿ* (all-together, v. 53) *divided*. The Day's separation-Names: *Yawm al-Faṣl* (Day of Division/Decision — 37:21; 44:40; 77:13–14, 38; 78:17); *yawm al-furqān* (Day of Distinction, 8:41 — Badr-as-preview). The mechanics: *"We shall separate them"* (10:28: *fazyayalnā-baynahum*); *"on that Day they shall be divided"* (30:14: *yatafarraqūn*); right-left sorting (56:8–9; 69:19, 25 — books-distributed post-sorting). *Wamtāzū* is *self*-separation commanded (move-yourselves-apart!): the guilty *walk* to wretchedness (feet testifying-then, v. 65, marching-now). No ushers-needed (guilt *knows* its station — faces-darkened, 3:106: *"faces blackened"* self-sorting). *Stand-apart* — marched. *This-Day* — sorted. *Wamtāzū!* — obeyed (compelled). *Division* — eternal (vv. 55–58 versus 59–64 — Garden/Fire, never-reuniting).

**Ayyuhā al-Mujrimūn: "O Guilty Ones" — Addressed as Criminals**

*"O guilty ones!"* — *ayyuhā al-mujrimūn* — *mujrimūn* (guilty, criminals, sinners-persistent — *ijrām*: crime, cutting-off-good): judgment's *label*. The address contrasts Garden's (v. 58: *"O Garden-people!"* — ḥadīth's *yā-ahla-al-jannah* — honour-titled) with Fire's (*yā-mujrimūn* — crime-titled). *Mujrim* in Qur'anic usage: persistent-deniers (7:40's arrogant-rejecters; 25:22's disbelieving-faces; 77:18's destroyed-generations — v. 31's *qurūn* as *mujrimīn*). The label *fits* (v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn* — deeds-named): mockers (v. 30), threateners (v. 18), stoners (v. 26-tradition), withholders (v. 47), disputers (v. 49), resurrection-deniers (v. 78) — *crimes* catalogued, *criminals* addressed. *Ayyuhā* (O): direct-vocative (no intermediaries — Judge-to-defendants). The Qur'an's *mujrim*-scenes: *"the guilty shall be known by marks, seized by forelocks-and-feet"* (55:41 — marked-dragged); *"hadst thou seen when guilty hang heads before Lord"* (32:12 — head-hung — v. 8's chin-up *reversed*!); *"the guilty desire ransoming with sons... all-earth — no rescue"* (70:11–14 — ransom-refused). *Mujrimūn* — marked (faces), marched (forelocks), managed (Fire-ward, v. 64's *burn*). *Label* — earned (*ijrām*-lifestyle, v. 54's *kuntum*, used-to). *Avoid* label-now (repent — *mujrim*-to-*muʾmin* convertible-pre-death, 25:70's replacement) — label-then *sealed* (*wamtāzū*-fixed). *Guilty?* — repent (now). *Separated?* — then (forever).

**Separation's Justice and Mercy**

*Wamtāzū* (separate!) is *just* (criminals-distinguished from righteous — conflation-unjust: victims-beside-victimizers eternally? — separation-rights) and *merciful* (to righteous — Garden unpolluted: no mockers (v. 30) sneering, no threateners (v. 18) stoning, no disputers (v. 49) wrangling — *"no vain-talk, no sin"* (56:25; 52:23) — peace-by-separation). The Qur'an's separation-parables: sheep-goats (40:17-context), wheat-chaff (8:37: *"that God may separate vile from good, pile vile together, cast into Hell"* — *piling*-imagery: rubbish-heaped), companions-sorted (4:69's goodly-company *gathered*; 37:22–23's *"gather wrongdoers, their mates, what they worshiped"* — evil-company *bundled*). World-mixing (believers-among-deniers — runner-among-town, v. 20; Abū-Bakr-among-scoffers, v. 47) was *trial* (patience-proving, invitation-needing); Day-separation is *verdict* (trial-graded, company-finalized). *Mujrimūn*-separated *from* (righteous) *to* (Fire-company — 37:22's bundled-damned). Al-Rāzī notes separation's *timing* (post-arraignment, vv. 32, 53; pre-sentencing, vv. 60–64's charges): sorted-then-sentenced (charges-addressed to sorted — vv. 60–64's *"O Children of Adam... this is Hell"* to *mujrimūn*-assembled). *Orderly* justice: produce (v. 53) → sort (here) → charge (vv. 60–62) → sentence (vv. 63–64) → execute (v. 64's *burn*). *Court* — procedural. *Wamtāzū* — procedure's pivot.

**The World's Pre-Separation: Sorting Before Sorting**

The Day's *wamtāzū* (separate!) is previewed by world's *voluntary* separations — and the previews *prepare*. *Moral* separation (righteous-distinguished-by-conduct — v. 11's Reminder-followers *vs.* v. 10's warned-in-vain; v. 21's followed-guided *vs.* v. 15's deniers): conduct *sorts* pre-Day (prayers, charity, honesty marking). *Social* separation (company-chosen — *"man is upon his friend's religion,"* Abū Dāwūd 4833, from Abū Hurayrah; ḥasan — friends *pre*-sorting: righteous-companions Gardenward, 4:69; evil-companions Fireward, 37:22–23). *Ritual* separation (congregations — mosque-gathered *vs.* heedless-scattered; ḥajj-assembled; Friday-distinguished). *Eschatological* separation-preview (death: souls-sorted — *"garden-or-pit"* grave, al-Tirmidhī 2460; martyrs-honored, v. 26 — grave *pre*-*wamtāzū*). The Prophet's ﷺ community *practiced* pre-separation (Madinan-distinction: believers-brotherhooded, hypocrites-suspected, deniers-treatied — social-furqān). Yet world-separation is *incomplete* (hypocrites-hidden, fates-unknown — no *wamtāzū*-pronouncing over living!): final-sorting God's-alone (v. 53's *ladaynā*, before-Us). *Practice*: separate *morally* (conduct-distinct — visibly-righteous), *socially* (company-righteous — friends-Gardenward), *compassionately* (invite-unsorted — runner-ran *into* town, v. 20 — separation-from-sin, never-from-sinners'-invitation). *Pre*-sort (thyself, now) — *post*-sorted (then, eternally). *Wamtāzū* — rehearsed.

**Standing Distinct Now, Standing Apart Then**

The verse's imperative (*wamtāzū*, stand-apart!) becomes a present summons. *Stand-apart morally now*: distinguish (pray-where-neglected, give-where-withheld, v. 47; speak-truth-where-mocked, v. 30; forgive-where-venged, v. 26) — distinction *costs* (runner stoned, v. 26-tradition; Bilāl tortured) yet *crowns* (v. 27's *mukramīn*, honored). *Never* blend (sin-normalized — *"do not follow majority-astray,"* 6:116 — crowds *mujrim*-bound, v. 7's *akthar*, most). *Stand-apart devotionally*: separations-of-worship (dawn-mosque while sleepers-sleep, night-prayer while heedless-snore, fasting while indulgers-indulge — *wamtāzū*-in-miniature). Distinction-delights (worship-sweetness *in* solitude — v. 11's unseen-fearers). *Stand-apart compassionately*: distinct-*for* (invitation), never distinct-*from* (contempt) — runner *O-my-people* (v. 20) while distinct; prophets *among* (v. 3's *mina*, among-messengers — sent *to*, distinct *for*). Invite the *mujrim*-drifting (warn, v. 6; counsel, v. 45; feed, v. 47) — today's *mujrim* tomorrow's *muʾmin* (ʿUmar-converted, Khālid, Hind — *mujrim*-labels *lifted* pre-death!). For the verse's final mercy is its *al-yawma* (this-Day): separation *then* (Day), invitation *now* (world). *Now*: mixed (warn-able). *Then*: parted (unjoinable — *"between them barrier,"* 57:13 — wall-with-door-shut). *Part* from sins-now (repentance-*wamtāzū*); *party* with righteous-now (company-*wamtāzū*); *plead* with straying-now (invitation). *Then*: *wamtāzū* — stood. *Apart* — eternally. *Stand* — rightly (now).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:60

> **Did I not enjoin upon you, O Children of Adam, that you not worship Satan—truly he is a manifest enemy unto you—**

**Expanded Commentary**

**A-Lam Aʿhad Ilaykum: "Did I Not Enjoin Upon You?"**

*"Did I not enjoin upon you"* — *a-lam aʿhad ilaykum* — *a-lam* (did-not?): interrogative-rebuke (v. 31's *a-lam-yaraw*, have-not-considered — rebuke-questions punctuating). *Aʿhad* (enjoin/covenant — *ʿahd*: vow, pact, covenant): divine *covenanting*. *"Enjoin translates aʿhad, from the same root as ʿahd, meaning vow, pact, or covenant"* (source commentary): the charge is *covenant-violation* (not mere rule-breaking — *treaty*-breach). *Which* covenant? Layers: *primordial* (7:172's *mīthāq* — *"am I not your Lord? — Yea!"* — pre-world testimony; v. 1's Qushayrī-reading as *yawm-al-mīthāq* — sūrah *opened* at Covenant-Day, now *closes*-toward it); *scriptural* (every revelation's *aʿhad* — *"worship God, associate naught,"* 4:36; *"Satan is enemy, take as enemy,"* 35:6); *prophetic* (each messenger's *aʿhad*-renewal — v. 14's declarations, v. 17's proclamation, vv. 20–25's plea); *rational* (*fiṭrah*-covenant, 30:30 — nature's *aʿhad*). *Ilaykum* (upon-you): covenant *addressed* (all-Adamites — v. 59's *mujrimūn* subset). The question *tries*: defendants (*muḥḍarūn*, vv. 32, 53; sorted-*mujrimūn*, v. 59) asked *pre*-sentence (vv. 63–64's *burn*): *did We not enjoin?* — prosecution's opening (covenant-exhibit). Answer-possible? — none (covenants *multiple*, witnesses *limbs*, v. 65). Silence-convicts (mouths-sealed, v. 65 — sealing *answers*). *Aʿhad* — enjoined. *Violated* — sentenced. *Covenant* — courtroom's foundation.

**Yā Banī Ādam: "O Children of Adam" — Universal Address**

*"O Children of Adam"* — *yā banī ādam* — *banī* (children/sons): descent-from-Adam (all-mankind — no tribe-excepted, no era-excluded). The Qur'an's *banī-ādam* addresses (7:26–27, 31, 35; 17:70; 19:58-contexts): dignity-and-warning (honored-carried, 17:70 — yet Satan-warned, 7:27: *"let not Satan tempt you as he expelled parents"*). *Addressing* sorted-*mujrimūn* (v. 59) as *banī-ādam* (not *yā-mujrimūn*-repeated): dignity-within-rebuke (criminals-yet-Adamites — creation-honoured, 17:70, though conduct-dishonoured). The address *recalls* Eden (parents warned-*there*: *"dwell... eat... approach-not-this-tree,"* 2:35; Satan vowed-*there*: *"I shall mislead all save sincere,"* 15:39–40; 38:82–83): children's trial *repeats* parents' (same tempter, same tree-principle — forbidden-fruit = forbidden-*shirk*/sin). Adam fell-*then*-repented (2:37: *"words... relented"* — paradigm!); *mujrimūn* fell-*and*-persisted (v. 7's Word-due) — paradigm-spurned. *Banī-ādam* also *equalizes* (kings/outcasts — one-father): judgment-fatherhood (no lineage-shields, 23:101's kinship-cut). *Hear* as Adamite (thou! — this verse *addresses*): covenant-bound (7:172's *balā*, yea-testified), Satan-warned (here), prophet-guided (v. 3's sent). *Adamite* — honoured (17:70). *Adamite-warned* — responsible. *Adamite-persisting* — sentenced (vv. 63–64). *Which* Adamite? — *now*-decided.

**Lā Taʿbudū al-Shayṭān: "That You Not Worship Satan"**

*"That you not worship Satan"* — *lā taʿbudū al-shayṭān* — *lā-taʿbudū* (not-worship): covenant's *negative*-clause (v. 61's *wa-ani-uʿbudūnī*, worship-Me — positive). *Al-shayṭān* (Satan: adversary, 19:44's *ʿaṣā*, rebelled; 2:34's refused-prostrated): Iblīs-and-host (devils-human-and-jinn, 6:112's *shayāṭīn*; 114:4–6's whisperers). *Worship* (*ʿibādah*): obedience-as-worship (following-Satan = worshiping — *"hast thou seen who takes desire as god?"* 45:23 — desire-obeyed = deified; *"they took rabbis/monks as lords,"* 9:31 — obeyed-absolutely = worshiped). Satan-worship's forms: *explicit* (Satanists, sorcerers-pacting — rare), *implicit* (sin-obeying — *"Satan's footsteps,"* 2:168, 208: *"follow not Satan's footsteps"* — each sin-step a prostration-direction), *systemic* (idolatries — v. 23's gods, v. 74's taken-gods — Satan's *proxies*: idols-silent, Satan-speaking-through). The town (vv. 13–29): Satan-worshiped (messengers-threatened, v. 18 — Satan's *awliyāʾ*, allies, 7:27; runner-stoned — Satan-applauded). The *mujrimūn* (v. 59): Satan-worshiped (denial, mockery, withholding — all *khuṭuwāt*, footsteps). Covenant victim-blaming-none: *warned* (here), *reminded* (35:6: *"Satan is enemy — take as enemy"*), *protected-if-seeking* (*istiʿādhah*, 16:98: *"seek refuge"*). *Worshiped-Satan* — self-chose (v. 7's *fa-hum*, for-they). *Sentenced* — self-earned (v. 54).

**Innahu Lakum ʿaduwwun Mubīn: Manifest Enemy**

*"Truly he is a manifest enemy unto you"* — *innahu lakum ʿaduwwun mubīn* — *ʿaduww* (enemy): *ʿadāwah* (enmity — active-hostility, not dislike-passive). *Mubīn* (manifest: evident *and* evidencing — v. 24's *ḍalāl-mubīn* grammar): enmity *obvious* (declared vows — *"I shall mislead,"* 15:39; *"I shall lie in wait on straight-path,"* 7:16 — enemy *announced*!) and *evidenced* (Eden-expulsion, 7:27; Cain's-murder, 5:30-whispered; every-war, every-addiction, every-despair — fingerprints-manifest). *"For references to Satan as manifest enemy, see 2:168; 2:208; 6:142; 7:22; 12:5; 17:53; 28:15; 43:62"* (source commentary) — *eight*-fold Qur'anic labelling! Enmity's *lakum* (unto-you): *personal* (not abstract-evil — *thy*-foe, whispering *thy*-name, 114:5's *yuwaswisu-fī-ṣudūri-al-nās*). Manifestness *convicts* befrienders: befriending-declared-enemy = treason (against soul, Lord, Adam-father). *Mujrimūn* befriended (obeyed-whispers, followed-footsteps, worshiped-proxies) — *manifest*-enemy embraced, *manifest*-Lord (v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*) abandoned. Inversion-insane — yet *common* (each sin a handshake-with-foe). *Recognize* (enemy-manifest — label-trusted), *resist* (*istiʿādhah*, vigilance, company-righteous), *attack-back* (obedience — each prayer a blow, each charity a wound to foe — *"Satan weeps at prostration,"* Muslim 81). *Manifest* — unmasked. *Enemy* — fought. *Victory* — promised (*"Satan's plot is weak,"* 4:76 — *if* resisted, 16:99–100: *no-authority over believers-trusting*).

**Honouring the Covenant, Fighting the Enemy**

The verse's *aʿhad* (covenant) becomes a loyalty-program. *Renew covenant daily*: morning-evening *adhkār* (covenant-renewals — *"O God, Thou art my Lord... I uphold Thy covenant as best I can"* — *sayyid-al-istighfār*, al-Bukhārī 6306 — *covenant*-worded!). Prayers as *aʿhad*-reaffirmations (*iyyāka-naʿbudu*, 1:5 — Thee-alone-worship = *lā-shayṭān + uʿbudūnī*, vv. 60–61 — recited 17×-daily!). *Study enemy-intel*: Satan's tactics (whispering, 114:4; beautifying, 15:39; despairing, 12:87's *"despair not"* — despair *is* Satanic; hastening-anger, disputes, v. 49; miserliness-threatening, 2:268) — know-foe (Sun-Tzu-sanctioned, revelation-commanded). *Warfare*: *istiʿādhah* (seeking-refuge — pre-Qur'an, 16:98; pre-prayer; in-anger — *"I seek refuge,"* al-Bukhārī 6115), *dhikr*-armour (*āyat-al-kursī* — Satan-flees, al-Bukhārī 2311; *muʿawwidhatayn* — 113–114, nightly-shield), company-fortress (righteous — wolves-take-loners, Abū Dāwūd 5478), repentance-recovery (falls *stood-from* — *"the one who repents is like sinless,"* Ibn Mājah 4250). For the verse's final mercy is its *question-form* (*a-lam*, did-not?): asked *then* (sentencing) what *warns* now (covenant-reminded!). *Then*-asked (defendants-dumb, v. 65's sealed) — *now*-asked (readers-answering: *balā! — Yea, Thou didst enjoin! — and I heed!*). *Answer* now (obedience). *Escape* then (Fire-avoided, vv. 63–64). *Aʿhad* — honoured. *Shayṭān* — fought. *Manifest-enemy* — defeated (*if* God-sought). *Covenant-keeper* — Garden-welcomed (v. 58's *Salām!*).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:61

> **And that you worship Me? This is a straight path.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Ani-uʿbudūnī: The Covenant's Positive Clause**

*"And that you worship Me?"* — *wa ani-uʿbudūnī* — *wa* (and): covenant's second-clause (v. 60's *lā-taʿbudū-shayṭān*, not-worship-Satan — negative; here *uʿbudūnī*, worship-Me — positive). *Uʿbudūnī* (worship-Me): *ʿibādah* (worship: love-plus-awe-plus-obedience — heart's devotion, tongue's praise, limbs' service). *Nī* (Me): worship's *exclusive*-Object (Allāh-alone — *tawḥīd-al-ulūhiyyah*, Oneness-of-worship — v. 22's runner-logic: Originator-deserves; v. 23's idols-undeserving). The couplet (60–61) *is* the *shahādah* (testimony) expanded: *lā-ilāha* (no-god — v. 60's Satan/idols-negated) *illa-Allāh* (save-God — v. 61's Me-affirmed). Covenant-complete: *abandon*-false (Satan/proxies), *adhere*-True (Lord). The defendants (*mujrimūn*, v. 59) violated *both* (worshiped-Satan, abandoned-Lord — double-breach). The interrogative (*a-lam*, did-not — governing both verses): *did We not enjoin... and that you worship Me?* — prosecution's two-count indictment (count-one: Satan-followed; count-two: Lord-abandoned). Either-count-alone convicts (Satan-service = treason; Lord-neglect = ingratitude, v. 35's *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn*); both-together damn (vv. 63–64's *burn*). *Uʿbudūnī* (worship-Me) also *invites* (beyond indicts): worship-offered (mercy-door — 39:53's *"despair not"* — worshipers-welcomed). *Indictment*-then (defendants), *invitation*-now (readers): *worship-Me* — heeded-now (Garden, vv. 55–58) or regretted-then (Fire, vv. 63–64). *Worship* — covenant's crown. *Me* — covenant's King.

**Hādhā Ṣirāṭun Mustaqīm: "This Is a Straight Path"**

*"This is a straight path"* — *hādhā ṣirāṭun mustaqīm* — *hādhā* (this): covenant-keeping (Satan-abandoned + Lord-worshiped) *demonstrated* (behold-it!). *Ṣirāṭ-mustaqīm* (straight-path): v. 4's Prophet-path (*ʿalā-ṣirāṭin-mustaqīm*, upon-straight — he-walked) + 1:6's prayed-path (*ihdinā*, guide-us — we-beg) = here *defined* (covenant = path). *"For straight path, see 1:6c"* (source commentary): the Fātiḥah's petition *answered*-here (path *described*: worship-Me-alone). Path's *straightness*: shortest (v. 4: no-detours — Satan's paths *crooked*, 6:153's *subul*, diverging-paths — *"follow not paths, they scatter you"*); safest (v. 60's enemy-avoided — straight-path *guarded*, 7:16's Satan-lying-in-wait *thwarted*-by-seekers); surest (destination-Garden, v. 58's *salām* — arrival-guaranteed, 19:71–72's *wurūd*-then-rescue). The defendants *left* it (crooked-wandered — v. 24's *ḍalāl*, straying; v. 62's *aḍalla*, led-astray): *hādhā* (this — pointed-at, shown-clearly) *abandoned* for *tilka* (those — Satan's subul, distant-doomed). Al-Rāzī's path-theology: straightness *demonstrated* (prophets-walked-it-visibly — v. 4's *ʿalā*, upon; runners-ran-it, v. 20), *demanded* (covenant-commanded, here), *rewarded* (Garden-ended, vv. 55–58). *This* — shown. *Straight* — walked-by-best. *Path* — open (till-death, 39:53; till-Cry, v. 49). *Walk* — now.

**Two Worships, Two Paths, Two Ends**

Verses 60–61 stage worship's *binary*: Satan-worshiped (v. 60 — path-crooked, end-Fire, vv. 63–64) versus Lord-worshiped (v. 61 — path-straight, end-Garden, vv. 55–58). No third-worship (neutrality-impossible — *"whoever is not with Me is against"* spirit — 10:32: *"what is after truth but error?"*): refraining-Lord-worship *is* Satan-serving (footsteps-followed, 2:168 — vacuum-filled-by-foe). The binary's Qur'anic refrains: *"Satan's party"* vs *"God's party"* (58:19, 22 — *ḥizb*-divided); *"companions-of-Fire"* vs *"companions-of-Garden"* (59:20 — *lā-yastawūn*, unequal); *"right"* vs *"left"* (56:8–9, 27, 41 — *aṣḥāb*-sorted). The *mujrimūn* (v. 59): Satan's-party (sorted-*wamtāzū*). The *aṣḥāb-al-jannah* (v. 55): God's-party (welcomed-*salām*, v. 58). Sorting-criterion: *worshiped-whom?* (vv. 60–61's two-*uʿbud*). Evidence: limbs (v. 65's testifying — worship-postures recorded: foreheads-prostrated-where? hands-given-to-whom? feet-walked-toward-what?). *Thy* party? — *now*-evidenced (prayer-niche *vs.* heedlessness-couch; charity-hand *vs.* miser-fist, v. 47; Qur'an-tongue *vs.* mockery-mouth, v. 30). Parties *visible*-now (conduct-badged), *separated*-then (*wamtāzū*). *Enlist* rightly (God's-party — worship-Me). *Desert* wrongly (Satan's — repent-out). *Binary* — bridgeless. *Choose* — now.

**The Straight Path as Covenant-Lived**

*Hādhā-ṣirāṭun-mustaqīm* (this-straight-path) *defines* Fātiḥah's petition (1:6) *operationally*: straight-path = covenant-lived (Satan-shunned + Lord-served). Operationally: *shun* (temptations-refused — gaze-lowered, 24:30; anger-swallowed — *"strong is anger-controller,"* al-Bukhārī 6114; whispers-dismissed — *istiʿādhah*, v. 60; company-evil-quit — *"loneliness better than evil-companion,"* al-Ḥākim); *serve* (obligations-rendered — five-prayers, zakāh, fasting, ḥajj-if-able; supererogations-added — night-prayers, v. 37; charity-habits, v. 47; counsel, vv. 20–25). The Prophet's ﷺ path-demonstration: *drew line* (straight) + *lines* (diverging) — *"this is God's path, straight — these are paths, each with devil-calling"* (Aḥmad 1:435, from Ibn Masʿūd; graded ṣaḥīḥ — *ṣirāṭ*-diagrammed!). Walkers *stumble* (sins) yet *remain* (direction-kept — v. 4's *ʿalā*, upon — stumblers-returning, 3:135's *"remembered-then-sought-forgiveness"*). Strayers *leave* (direction-abandoned — v. 62's *aḍalla-jibillan*, multitudes-led — exit-taken). Difference: *return* (stumblers) vs *persist* (strayers, v. 7's Word-due-upon-persisters). *Stumbled?* — return (*tawbah* — path-rejoined). *Straying?* — U-turn (*hādhā*-pointed — this!-not-that!). *Path* — straight. *Walkers* — welcomed (*salām*, v. 58). *Wanderers* — warned (*burn*, v. 64). *Walk.*

**Walking Straight Now, Welcomed Straight Then**

The verse's path becomes a walking-plan. *Map daily*: morning-resolve (today's straight-steps: prayers-timely, tongue-truthful, hands-helpful, eyes-guarded — *ṣirāṭ*-scheduled); evening-review (steps-kept? stumbled-where? — *muḥāsabah*, v. 32 — path-audited). *Walk together*: companions-straight (righteous-friends — path-fellowship; family-praying-together — household-caravan; mosque-congregations — *ṣirāṭ*-crowds). Solo-walkers stray-easier (wolves-take-loners); caravans arrive (4:69's goodly-company). *Mark straightness*: signs (*istiqāmah*-evidences — consistency: *"say 'I believe' then be steadfast,"* Muslim 38, from Sufyān — steadfastness as straightness-maintained; growth: knowledge-deepening, character-softening, worship-sweetening). *Repair deviations*: stumbles-repented (immediate — *"follow evil with good, it wipes,"* al-Tirmidhī 1987), strayings-U-turned (major — conditions-four, v. 45), doubts-studied (scholars-consulted, v. 11's Reminder-followed). For the verse's final mercy is its *hādhā* (this!): path *shown* (prophets-walked, v. 4; Book-mapped, v. 2; runners-ran, v. 20) — *this!* (not hidden-maze, not secret-society). *Shown* — walkable. *Straight* — arrivable. *This* — thine (if taken). *Take* (covenant-kept: Satan-shunned, v. 60; Lord-served, here). *Walk* (steadfastly). *Arrive* (*salām!*, v. 58). *Hādhā-ṣirāṭun-mustaqīm* — *this!*

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:62

> **For indeed he has led many among you astray. Did you not understand?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Laqad Aḍalla Minkum Jibillan Kathīran: Multitudes Misled**

*"For indeed he has led many among you astray"* — *wa laqad aḍalla minkum jibillan kathīran* — *laqad* (indeed-certainly): fact-emphasized (history-attested). *Aḍalla* (led-astray): Satan's *iḍlāl* (misleading — v. 60's enemy *acting*). *Minkum* (among-you): victims *from* defendants (Adamites, v. 60 — *thy* ranks thinned!). *Jibillan-kathīran* (many-multitudes, great-creation-masses — *jibill*: multitude, generation-heap): *vast*-numbers (not dozens — *civilizations*!). The Qur'an's misled-multitudes: Noah's drowned-majority (11:40's *"few"*-believed — multitudes-otherwise), ʿĀd/Thamūd (erased-powers, v. 31), Pharaoh's host (drowned-regiments), town's stoners (v. 18 — cityful), Quraysh's Badr-dead (chiefs-heaped). Satan's *kill*-count (spiritual): *jibillan* (heaps) per-century. His *vow* (7:16–17: *"I shall come front-behind-right-left — Thou wilt find most unthankful"*) *fulfilled* (v. 7's *akthar*, most-Word-due; v. 35's *most*-unthankful, 2:243). *Minkum* (among-you) *particularizes*: defendants *see* (fellow-*mujrimūn*, v. 59 — heap-members!). *Jibillan* (heaps) *quantifies*: misled-massive (success-rate Satanic-high). *Aḍalla* (led) *verbalizes*: misleading *active* (lures-cast, doubts-sown, sins-beautified, 15:39). *Laqad* (indeed) *certifies*: undeniable (ruins, v. 31; books, v. 12; limbs, v. 65 — evidences-converging). *Heaps* — misled. *Thou* — among-heaps? (*minkum* — from-you!). *Escape* heaps-now (few-believing, 11:40's Noah-few; runner-one, v. 20 — fewness-as-safety!). *Many* — misled. *Few* — guided (34:13's *"few thankful"*). *Be* few.

**A-Fa-Lam Takū Taʿqilūn: "Did You Not Understand?"**

*"Did you not understand?"* — *a-fa-lam takū taʿqilūn* — *a-fa-lam* (did-then-not?): rebuke-interrogative (vv. 31, 60's *a-lam*-series — questions-prosecuting). *Taʿqilūn* (understand/reason — *ʿaql*: intellect, restraint — reason *restrains*): defendants *unreasoning*-charged. *"Did you not understand the fundamental difference between worshipping God and worshipping Satan?"* (Ibn Kathīr — source commentary): *the* difference (Creator-vs-creature, vv. 22–23; Benefactor-vs-enemy, v. 60; straight-vs-crooked, v. 61; Garden-vs-Fire, vv. 55–64). *Taʿqilūn* (reason) implies reason *sufficed* (proofs-plain: *fiṭrah*, 30:30; signs, vv. 33–44; warners, vv. 14–25; ruins, v. 31 — intellect *equipped*!). Failure thus *culpable* (not incapacity — *misuse*: intellects *spent* (commerce-cunning, poetry-skill, dispute-sharpness, v. 49's *yakhiṣṣimūn*) yet *unspent* (on *the*-difference). The Qur'an's unreasoning-indictments: *"they have hearts understanding-not"* (7:179 — faculties-unused); *"deaf, dumb, blind — they understand not"* (2:171 — senses-unreasoned); *"most understand not"* (29:63; 49:4 — majorities-mindless). *Taʿqilūn*-failure *distinct* from *taʿlamūn* (knowing): defendants *knew* (warned! — v. 6) yet *understood-not* (weighed-not, acted-not — knowledge-unreasoned). *ʿAql* (reason) as *ʿiqāl* (hobble — camel-tie): reason *restrains* (from cliffs) — unrestrained (Satan-followed) = unreasoned (*lā-yaʿqilūn*). *Reason* — restraining. *Unrestrained* — unreasonable. *Heap-members* (misled) — *un*-reasoned (though clever!). *Understand* — now (difference-pondered, path-chosen). *Then*-asked (*a-fa-lam?*) — now-answered (*balā! — Yea, I understand — and obey!*).

**Multitudes-Misled as Warning Against Majorities**

*Jibillan-kathīran* (many-multitudes) *weaponizes* against majority-following: *many* = misled (Satan's harvest-majority). The Qur'an's majority-verdicts: *"most do not believe"* (12:103 — v. 7's *akthar*-echoed); *"most are unthankful"* (2:243 — v. 35); *"most understand not"* (above); *"if thou obeyest most on earth, they mislead from God's path"* (6:116 — majority-obedience = misguidance!). The town (vv. 13–29): majority-stoned (cityful-threatened, v. 18), minority-believed (runner-one, v. 20) — majority *Cried* (v. 29), minority *crowned* (vv. 26–27). Quraysh: majority-mocked (v. 30), minority-believed (early-few) — majority Badr-felled, minority history-crowned. *Jibillan* (heaps) thus *inverts* social-proof (crowds-followed = cliffs-followed): popularity-as-peril (viral-misguidance, fashionable-sin, consensus-unbelief). *Kathīran* (many) *quantifies* peril (not edge-cases — *mainstream*-misled!). Safety: *qillah* (fewness) — Noah's-few (11:40), cave-youth-few (18:22's debated-number — fewness-certain), Badr-313 (few-victorious), runner-one (v. 20). *"Islām began strange, returns strange — glad-tidings to strangers"* (Muslim 145, from Abū Hurayrah — *ghurabāʾ*-praised!). *Strange* (minority-righteous) > *jibillan* (multitude-misled). *Count* differently: heaven-counts (one-runner outweighs city, v. 20–29), earth-miscounts (city outweighs runner — stones-prevail-momentarily). *Join* few-now (prayers-while-sleepers, charity-while-hoarders, truth-while-mockers) — *crowds*-then (Garden-multitudes, 56:39–40's *"many from later"* — righteousness-majority-eternal!). *Few*-now; *many*-then. *Jibillan*-warned; *ghurabāʾ*-crowned.

**Understanding as Salvation's Hinge**

*A-fa-lam-taʿqilūn* (did-you-not-understand) makes *ʿaql* (understanding) salvation's *hinge*: understood (difference-grasped + acted) = saved; understood-not = sentenced (vv. 63–64). Understanding's Qur'anic *content*: *signs*-understood (29:43's parables — *"none understands save knowers"*; v. 46's signs-faced-not-fled), *warnings*-understood (63:3's hearts-sealed-understanding-not — unsealed-understand), *Hereafter*-understood (29:64's *"Hereafter is life, if they knew"* — eternity-weighed). Understanding's *proof*: *action* (understood = obeyed — *"those who listen then follow best,"* 39:18 — listening-plus-following = understanding!). Defendants *heard* (warners!) yet *acted-not* (persisted!) — hearing-minus-acting = understanding-minus (2:171's *"deaf"* — ears-unacted). The sūrah *tutors* understanding throughout: parables (vv. 13–30 — *iḍrib*, strike-parable — understanding-via-narrative), signs (vv. 33–44 — nature-understood), questions (vv. 31, 60, 62, 68 — *a-lam?*, have-not — understanding-provoked), contrasts (vv. 55–64 — Garden/Fire — understanding-clarified). *Taʿqilūn* (understand) *now*: ponder-difference (God-vs-Satan, v. 60–61 — worship-compared), weigh-multitudes (heaps-misled — majority-distrusted), choose-few (runner-joined, v. 20). *Understanding* — acted. *Acted* — saved. *Saved* — *salām* (v. 58). *Understand!*

**Fleeing Heaps, Joining Few: Practices of the Understanding**

The verse's *jibillan* (heaps) becomes an exodus-call. *Audit memberships*: each crowd (ideological, social, digital — followers, factions, fandoms): *heap-or-few?* (majority-doctrine vs revelation — 6:116-test; crowd-conduct vs *taqwā* — v. 45-test; leaders vs messengers — v. 21-test: unpaid? guided?). *Exit* heaps (misleading-majorities — quietly-if-weak, loudly-if-strong — runner *ran-in*, then stood-out, vv. 20–25). *Join few*: seek righteous-minorities (mosque-dawn-few, study-circles, charity-crews — *ghurabāʾ*-gatherings). *Value* fewness (small-congregations, lonely-stands — *"strangers"*-glad-tidings, Muslim 145). *Understand deliberately*: study-difference (God-vs-Satan — Names-contrasted: Creator-vs-whisperer, Benefactor-vs-begruder, Straight-vs-crooked; ends-compared: Garden-vs-Fire, vv. 55–64 — *tafakkur*-sessions). Teach-difference (children-catechized: *who-deserves-worship? — the-Originator!* v. 22; *who-schemes? — manifest-enemy!* v. 60). *Act* understanding (worship-shifted: Satan-steps-quit, Lord-steps-kept — v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*-operationalized). For the verse's final mercy is its *fa-* (*a-fa-lam*, did-*then*-not): asked *then* (post-heaps-witnessed — multitudes-beheld-misled!) what *warns* now (heaps-visible — ruins, v. 31; headlines; history!). *Then*-asked (defendants-heaped) — *now*-asked (readers-warned: *see heaps? — understand? — flee?*). *See* (multitudes-misled — history-proved). *Understand* (difference-grasped — reason-used). *Flee* (heaps-exited — few-joined). *Understand!* — now. *Heaps* — fled. *Few* — crowned (*mukramīn*, v. 27; *fākihūn*, v. 55; *salām*, v. 58).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:63

> **This is the Hell that you were promised.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Hādhihi Jahannamu: "This Is Hell" — Presented**

*"This is the Hell"* — *hādhihi jahannamu* — *hādhihi* (this-feminine): Hell *demonstrated* (pointed-at, present — *"this!"*). *Jahannam* (Hell/Gehenna): Fire-proper (valley-named — Jerusalem's refuse-valley, fire-perpetual — Gehenna-archetype). The presentation-mode: defendants (*mujrimūn*-sorted, v. 59; covenant-charged, vv. 60–62) *shown* (sentence-displayed-pre-execution, v. 64's *burn*). The Qur'an's Hell-presentations: *"this is Hell which guilty deny"* (55:43 — denied-then-displayed); *"Hell shall be brought that Day"* (89:23 — *jīʾa*, brought — Fire-transported-to-court!); *"Hell made to appear for beholders"* (79:36 — *burrizat*, exposed — veils-stripped). *Hādhihi* (this) contrasts deniers' *hādhā-al-waʿd* (v. 48's "this-promise" — mocked-near) become *hādhihi-jahannam* (this-Hell — beheld-near!): mocked-*this* materialized. Presentation *tortures* pre-torment (sight-before-touch — dread-as-appetizer: *"they shall see it from afar, hearing rage-and-roar,"* 25:12 — audition-before-admission). *This!* — beheld. *Hell!* — named (*Jahannam* — proper-noun-terror). *Shown* — entered-next (*burn*, v. 64). *Beholding* — believers'-mercy (Hell-shown-*then*-averted — *"you shall see Hell... eye-of-certainty,"* 102:6–7 — sight-as-thanks-occasion: *spared-from-this!*). *Behold* now (verses-described) — spared then (sight-grateful, not sentenced). *This* — warned. *That-Day* — witnessed (all). *Spared* — believers (19:72's *nunajjī*, We-deliver-mindful).

**Allatī Kuntum Tūʿadūn: "That You Were Promised" — Foretold**

*"That you were promised"* — *allatī kuntum tūʿadūn* — *kuntum* (you-used-to): promise *habitual* (warned-repeatedly — v. 6's *tundhira*, warn; v. 11's warning-effective; vv. 20–25's plea; v. 45's counsel). *Tūʿadūn* (promised/threatened — *waʿd*: promise-includes-threat): Hell *promised* (as-threat — v. 48's *waʿd*-mocked — promise-kept!). *"These verses say essentially, 'This is the Fire the prophets warned you about, but you denied, so taste the punishment of the Fire that you used to deny!'"* (32:20; 34:42 — source commentary): warning-cited (prophets-named), denial-cited (*deny*-habitual), tasting-commanded (*taste!* — v. 64's *burn*). Promise-keeping *vindicates* warners (v. 52's *ṣadaqa-al-mursalūn*, messengers-true — Hell-displayed as truth-exhibit!) and *convicts* warned (promise-heard-yet-unheeded — *"we were warned but called-liars,"* 67:9 — Fire-inmates confessing!). *Tūʿadūn* (promised) also *consoles* (promise *includes* Garden — v. 11's *ajr-karīm*, generous-reward-promised; vv. 55–58's bliss-promised): Promise-Keeper *keeps-both* (threat-executed-here, promise-fulfilled-there — 39:20's *"God breaks not promise"*). Deniers *selected* (threat-portion — by denial); believers *selected* (promise-portion — by faith). *Promised* — both. *Received* — chosen (v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn*, what-you-did — choice-earned). *Hear* promise-now (vv. 55–64's both-portions) — *choose* wisely (faith-portion). *Promised* — certain. *This* — shown. *Choose* — now.

**Promise-to-Presentation: Warning's Fulfillment Arc**

The verse completes the sūrah's warning-arc: *promised* (v. 6: warn; v. 11: warn-followers; v. 48: *waʿd*, promise-mocked) → *previewed* (v. 29's town-Cry — Hell-foretaste; v. 49's world-Cry — Hell-rehearsal) → *presented* (here: *hādhihi*, this!). Arc's pedagogy: warnings *escalate* (words → previews → presentation): words-ignored (vv. 10, 46's turnings) → previews-dismissed (ruins-unconsidered, v. 31) → presentation-undeniable (*this!* — sight-compelled). Escalation *is* mercy (opportunities-multiplied pre-irreversible): each stage escapable (words-heeded? previews-pondered?) — only presentation-final (v. 64's *burn*-entered). The Qur'an's escalation-gallery: Noah (words-centuries → flood-preview-none → flood-final); Pharaoh (signs-nine → sea-parted-preview → sea-closed-final); town (messengers → runner → Cry, vv. 14–29); Quraysh (verses → Badr-preview → Hour-final). *Thy* stage? — *words*-now (this-verse-reading = promise-hearing!). Previews-available (graveyards, v. 31; Cries-described, vv. 29, 49). Presentation-pending (death-or-Hour). *Act* at words-stage (cheapest: repent-free, believe-open) — preview-stage costlier (terror-priced), presentation-stage *priceless* (no-purchase — 10:54's ransom-refused; 70:11–14's sons-offered-rejected). *Words* — heeded-now. *Previews* — pondered-now. *Presentation* — spared-then (19:72's mindful-delivered). *This* — promised. *This* — shown-then. *Escape-shown* — believed-now.

**Hell Described: Yā Sīn's Fire-Portrait in Context**

*Jahannam* (Hell) here (named-barely — *this-is-Hell-that-promised*) evokes the Qur'an's fuller Fire-portrait (believed-whole): *fuel* (*"whose fuel is men-and-stones,"* 2:24; 66:6 — deniers-as-kindling!); *heat* (*"Fire-of-God-kindled reaching-hearts,"* 104:6–7 — inward-burning); *drink* (*"pus-water, gulped-unswallowable,"* 14:16–17; *"boiling, cutting-bowels,"* 47:15); *food* (*zaqqūm*, 37:62–66 — *"food-of-sinners,"* 44:43–44); *garments* (*"cut from Fire,"* 22:19; *"pitch,"* 14:50); *shade* (*black-smoke*, 56:43 — *ẓill*-mockery); *keepers* (*nineteen-stern*, 74:30–31; 66:6's *"stern-severe, disobey-not"*); *cries* (*wailing-roaring*, 11:106's *zafīr-shahīq*; *"our Lord, remove — we-believe!"* 35:37 — refused); *eternity* (*"abiding eternally,"* 98:6 — no-exit, 35:36's *"neither-decided-death nor-lightened"*). The Prophet's ﷺ Fire-warnings: *"Fire is seventy-times hotter"* (al-Bukhārī 3265; Muslim 2843 — world-fire *cool*-comparatively!); *"lightest-punished: shoes-of-fire boiling-brain"* (al-Bukhārī 6561 — *lightest*!); *"shield with half-date"* (v. 47 — charity-as-shield). *This*-Hell (verse's *hādhihi*) *is* that-Hell (gallery's). *Shown*-then (defendants) — *described*-now (readers). *Description* spares (fear-born-obedience — v. 11's *khashyah*, fear-unseen). *Fear* now (wholesome-terror) — *spared* then (19:72). *This* — believed. *That* — escaped (God-willing).

**Fleeing "This" Now, Facing "That" Never**

The verse's *hādhihi* (this!) becomes a flight-order. *Flee Hell-fuel-acts*: *shirk* (association — unforgiven-unrepented, 4:48 — v. 23's idols-abandoned; v. 74's taken-gods-shed); *prayer-abandonment* (*"what led to Saqar? — we prayed-not,"* 74:42–43 — Hell-inmates' *first*-confession!); *poor-unfed* (74:44's second; v.  Funktion 47's withholders-warned); *vain-disputing* (74:45; v. 49's *yakhiṣṣimūn*); *Day-denying* (74:46; v. 48's mockers). *Flee* systematically (each-fuel quenched: testify-purely, pray-timely, feed-generously, dispute-minimally, believe-firmly). *Seek Hell-shields*: *tawḥīd* (fortress — *"whoever says lā-ilāha... sincerely enters Paradise,"* al-Bukhārī 99 — shields-foremost); *prayer* (*nūr*-shield, Muslim 223); *fasting* (*junnah*, shield — al-Bukhārī 1894); *charity* (half-date-shield, above); *tears* (*"eyes weeping-from-fear never touch Fire,"* al-Tirmidhī 1633 — v. 11's unseen-fearers); *duʿāʾ* (*"O God, I seek refuge from Hell"* — thrice-daily, al-Tirmidhī 2572 — Hell-itself-prays-for-seeker!). *Picture "this"*: Hell-visualization (lawful-fear — descriptions-pondered, *hādhihi*-imagined — dread-as-deterrent). For the verse's final mercy is its *kuntum* (you-*used-to*-be-promised): promised *habitually* (warnings-repeated — mercy-persistent!). *Habitual*-warning (prophets, vv. 14–17; runners, vv. 20–25; verses, vv. 6–64) — *habitual*-chance (repent-repeatedly!). *This* — promised-often. *Heed* — now (promise-believed). *Spared* — then (*hādhihi*-beheld-gratefully, 19:72-delivered, 102:7-thanked). *Flee-this* — now. *Face-that* — never (God-willing).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:64

> **Burn therein today for having disbelieved!**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Iṣlawhā al-Yawma: "Burn Therein Today" — Execution Ordered**

*"Burn therein today"* — *iṣlawhā al-yawma* — *iṣlaw* (burn/roast — *ṣalā*: entering-fire, enduring-blaze — *"they shall burn in Fire,"* 82:14–16): imperative of *execution* (sentence-enacted). *Hā* (therein): Hell (v. 63's *hādhihi-jahannam*, this-Hell). *Al-yawma* (today): *This-Day* (vv. 54–55's *al-yawma* — Garden-entered *today*, Fire-entered *today* — entries-simultaneous, eternally-divergent). The command's Speaker: God (Judge-as-Executioner-ordering — angels-executing, 66:6's stern-keepers; 74:30's nineteen). The mode: *iṣlaw* (burn!) — *endure*-Fire (not touch-and-exit — *abide*, 98:6; not die-from — *"neither death-decided nor lightened,"* 35:36). *"Taste the punishment of the Fire that you used to deny!"* (32:20; 34:42 — source commentary's gloss — *taste!* (*dhūqū*) — tasting-as-enduring). The Qur'an's burn-commands: *"burn therein — same whether patient-or-not"* (52:16 — patience-unavailing-then!); *"enter gates-Hell abiding"* (39:72 — *udkhulū*, enter! — v. 26's *udkhul*-Garden inverted!). *Iṣlaw* (burn) vs *udkhul* (v. 26's enter-Garden): two *entries* (bliss/torment), two imperatives (welcome/*"Enter!"* vs sentence/*"Burn!"*). *Today* — entered. *Eternally* — endured (*khālidūn*, abiding). *Ordered* — obeyed (compelled-marched, 55:41's forelocks-feet; 70:43's rushing-humbled). *Burn* — today. *Today* — forever (Day-unending). *Entered* — unexited (except-believers-purified — *shafāʿah*-hoped, 20:109's permitted-intercession).

**Bimā Kuntum Takfurūn: "For Having Disbelieved" — Cause Cited**

*"For having disbelieved"* — *bimā kuntum takfurūn* — *bi-mā* (for-what/because): causation (sentence-*grounded*). *Kuntum* (you-used-to): disbelief-*habitual* (lifestyle, v. 54's *kuntum-taʿmalūn* — used-to-do). *Takfurūn* (disbelieve — *kufr*: covering-truth, ingratitude — vv. 7's *lā-yuʾminūn*, believe-not; 35's unthankful; 47's withholders): crime-named. The causality *displays* justice (v. 54's *lā-tuẓlam*, no-wronging — *bi-mā*, for-what — punishment *earned*): burned *for* disbelieved (not arbitrarily — *fittingly*). The Qur'an's *bi-mā*-causations: *"taste for what you disbelieved"* (16:94-contexts); *"that is for what hands sent-forth — God never wrongs"* (3:182; 8:51; 22:10 — *bi-mā-qaddamat* + *laysa-bi-ẓallām*, never-unjust — causation-plus-justice-formula!). *Takfurūn* (disbelieve) encompasses (sūrah-catalogued): message-denied (v. 15's *takdhibūn*, liars-charged), warners-mocked (v. 30's *yastahziʾūn*), signs-fled (v. 46's *muʿriḍīn*), charity-scoffed (v. 47), promise-mocked (v. 48), Satan-worshiped (v. 60), Lord-abandoned (v. 61) — *kufr*-comprehensive (covering-all-truth). *Kuntum* (used-to) *excludes* lapses-repented (25:70's replaced; 39:53's forgiven-all) — *habitual*-unrepented alone *burns*. *For* — cited. *Disbelieved* — habitually. *Burned* — fittingly (*lā-tuẓlam* — unwronged!). *Cause* — announced (transparency: sentenced *know-why* — unlike tyrants' dungeons). *Why* — *takfurūn*. *Remedy*-now (believe! — v. 11). *Remedy*-then — none (v. 64-sealed).

**"Today" as Eternity's Dawn: Temporality of Burning**

*Al-yawma* (today/this-Day) ignites *eternity*: Day-unending (no-night, 76:13's neither-sun-nor-cold — Fire's *timeless*-torment; no-tomorrow, 35:36's unlightened). *"Burn therein today"* = *begin*-burning-Today (entry-dated) + *continue*-burning-eternally (exit-denied — 98:6's *khālidīn*; 4:169's *abiding-forever*). The Qur'an's eternity-emphases: *"abide eternally"* (dozens — Fire *and* Garden — symmetry-solemn); *"whenever skins matured, We replace for tasting"* (4:56 — renewal-torment — burning-unconsuming!); *"their torment unlightened, unrespited"* (2:86; 35:36 — intensity-constant). *Today*-contrasts: world-todays (pain-relieved — medicines, sleeps, deaths-ending; pleasures-fading — satiety, boredom, loss) versus Day (pain-unrelieved — 14:16's unswallowable; 35:36's undesired-death-denied!). *Al-yawma* also *individualizes* (thy-today: death-day as *yawma*-dawn — grave-preview, 40:46's morning-evening-exposed — then Day-full). The Prophet's ﷺ *yawma*-warnings: *"remember Fire"* (frequently — *"I warn you of Fire! I warn you!"* al-Bukhārī 1417-context); *"protected? — shields!"* (v. 63's shields-listed). *Today* — coming (*iqtarabat*, 54:1-near; v. 49's sudden). *Burning* — avoidable-now (shields-available, repentance-open) — unavoidable-then (*iṣlaw*-executed). *Today* — prepare (yesterday-passed, tomorrow-unpromised — *hadīth*-maxim). *Burn* — never (God-refuge-sought!).

**Fire Entered for Disbelief: Faith's Absolute Necessity**

*Bimā-kuntum-takfurūn* (for-disbelieved) establishes faith's *necessity*: disbelief-alone damns (however-moral-otherwise — charity-without-faith, kindness-without-*tawḥīd* — *"deeds-as-ashes"* wind-scattered, 14:18; *"mirage"* thirst-unslaked, 24:39). The Qur'an's faith-necessity: *"whoever seeks religion besides Islām — never accepted"* (3:85); *"those who disbelieve — deeds-vain"* (47:8–9; 18:103–105's *"greatest-losers — deeds-astray"*). Conversely, faith-alone saves-ultimately (sinners-purified-then-Gardened — *"whoever has atom's-faith exits Fire,"* al-Bukhārī 44; Muslim 193 — *kufr*-absence as salvation-floor!). The asymmetry (disbelief-damns-despite-deeds; faith-saves-despite-sins-ultimately) displays *tawḥīd*'s primacy (v. 22's Originator-right; v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*-covenant): worship-right *foundational* (all-deeds built-upon; foundation-absent — structure-vain). *Takfurūn* (disbelieve) as *kufrān* (ingratitude, v. 35): unthankfulness-damning (blessings-denied-Source — 16:112's ungrateful-town-punished!). *Kuntum* (habitual): persistence-damning (lapses-forgiven, 39:53 — *all*-sins; persistence-sealed, v. 7). *Believe*-now (v. 25's *āmantu* — testify!; v. 11's follow-fear — practice!). *Persist*-believing (steadfast-till-death — *"die not save submitting,"* 3:102). *Burn*-then — for-*takfurūn*. *Spared*-then — for-*tuʾminūn* (believing — 2:62's no-fear-no-grief). *Necessity* — absolute. *Faith* — all.

**Fleeing Burning Now, Entering Gardens Then**

The verse's *iṣlaw* (burn!) becomes a flight-siren. *Extinguish faithlessness*: testify (*shahādah* — sincerely, knowledgeably, actedly — *takfurūn*-reversed); learn (*tawḥīd* — creed-studied; *shirk*-forms-known — gross-and-subtle, v. 24's hidden-desire-ḥadīth); practice (*uʿbudūnī*, v. 61 — worship-habitual). *Extinguish Fire-acts*: major-sins abandoned (seven-destroyers — *shirk*, sorcery, murder, usury-consuming, orphan-wealth, battle-fleeing, chaste-slandering — al-Bukhārī 2766); minor-habits checked (tongue-guarded, gaze-lowered, anger-managed). *Ignite shields*: *taqwā* (foremost — 19:72's *nunajjī-alladhīna-ittaqaw*, We-deliver-mindful — *taqwā*-delivers!); *ṣadaqah* (extinguishes-sin-as-water-fire — al-Tirmidhī 614); *fasting* (shield, v. 63); *tears* (v. 11-fearers, v. 63-shields); *istighfār* (seekers-unpunished — *"God punishes-not while they seek-forgiveness,"* 8:33 — *mustaghfirīn*-spared!). *Beg refuge*: *istiʿādhah*-from-Fire (thrice-daily-duʿāʾ, v. 63; prayer-ending: *"O God, I seek refuge from Hell-torment...,"* Muslim 588 — Prophet's ﷺ constant). For the verse's final mercy is its *bi-mā* (for-what): causation *stated* (transparency!) — sentenced *know-why* (disbelieved). *Knowing-why*-now (this-verse!) *prevents*-then (believe-now!). *Why*-burn? — *takfurūn*. *Why*-spared? — *tuʾminūn* (believing-acting-persevering). *Burn-today* — warned. *Believe-today* — spared-tomorrow (*yawma*-welcomed, v. 58's *Salām!*). *Iṣlaw* — fled. *Udkhul* (v. 26) — entered (God-willing). *Today* — decide.

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:65

> **On that Day We shall seal their mouths. Their hands will speak to Us, and their feet will bear witness to that which they used to earn.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Nakhtimu ʿAlā Afwāhihim: Mouths Sealed**

*"On that Day We shall seal their mouths"* — *al-yawma nakhtimu ʿalā afwāhihim* — *al-yawma* (that-Day): judgment-Day (vv. 54, 59, 64's *yawma* — sentencing-Day). *Nakhtimu* (We-seal — *khatm*: sealing, stamping-shut — v. 7's Word-sealed-hearts echoed-bodily): divine *muzzling*. *ʿAlā-afwāhihim* (upon-their-mouths): speech-organs *locked*. *Why* sealed? — *"people will no longer be able to make excuses and hide sins with lies and duplicity"* (source commentary): mouths *lie* (world-excuses: *"we were weak,"* 4:97; *"leaders misled,"* 33:67; *"we associated — fathers did,"* 7:173 — excuse-repertoire!). Sealing *ends* perjury (courtroom-silenced-defendants — evidence-speaks-instead). The ḥadīth dramatizes: Anas ibn Mālik (d. ca. 91/709, Prophet's ﷺ servant-years) reports: *"We were with the Messenger ﷺ when he laughed: 'Dost thou know at what I laugh?' — 'God-and-Messenger know best.' — 'At what a servant says to his Lord. He says, "O Lord, wilt Thou not protect me from injustice?" — "But of course." — "I accept no witness against myself but myself." — God: "Today thy soul suffices as witness, as do noble scribes [angels, 80:15–16]." Then his mouth will be sealed and it will be said to limbs, "Speak!" — So they speak of deeds. Then permitted to speak, he says [to limbs], "Away! Be doomed! It was on your behalf I contended!"'"* (Muslim 2969; Ibn Kathīr, al-Qurṭubī — the *self*-witness- demander self-convicted-by-self!). Mouths-sealed (lying-ended), limbs-loosed (truth-begun). *Sealed* — silenced. *Silent* — sentenced (limbs-testifying).

**Wa Tukallimunā Aydīhim: Hands Speaking**

*"Their hands will speak to Us"* — *wa tukallimunā aydīhim* — *tukallimunā* (speak-to-Us): hands *address* God (direct-testimony — prosecution-witnesses!). *Aydī* (hands): deed-instruments (struck, stole, signed-false, withheld-charity — v. 47's ungiving-fists; typed-slanders, waved-mockingly, v. 30). Hands *speak* (articulate — *kalām*, speech — grammar-given-to-flesh!): *"I struck,"* *"I withheld,"* *"I forged,"* *"I touched-unlawfully."* The Qur'an's hand-testimony: *"their hands will speak"* (here); tongues-hands-feet *all*-testifying (24:24: *"tongues, hands, feet testify to what they did"* — tongues *included*-there, *sealed*-here — sequence: tongues-testify-then-sealed? or occasions-varied? — reconciliations-offered). Hands-as-witnesses *fit* (v. 8's shackled-hands — bound-then for evil-extended; v. 12's *qaddamū*, sent-forth-by-hands — hands'-deeds recorded; v. 35's *ʿamilat-aydīhim*, hands-worked — labour-witnessed). World-hands *concealed* (gloves, darkness, deniability — *"not my signature!"*); Day-hands *confess* (no-gloves, Light-unveiled, 24:35). *Tukallimunā* (to-Us): testimony *to-God* (Judge-direct — no-hearsay!). Hands *loyal* (to-Truth, not owner — owner-betrayed-by-owned!). *Own* hands — testifying. *Prepare* testimony-now (hands-действуя-righteously: giving, v. 47; helping; writing-truth; raised-in-duʿāʾ — witnesses-rehearsed!). *Hands* — speaking-then. *Speak* — righteously-now (hands-acting).

**Wa Tashhadu Arjuluhum: Feet Bearing Witness**

*"And their feet will bear witness"* — *wa tashhadu arjuluhum* — *tashhadu* (bear-witness — *shahādah*: testimony): feet *witness* (corroborate-hands — two-witnesses-rule, 2:282 — hands-speak, feet-confirm!). *Arjul* (feet): journey-instruments (walked-to-sin — taverns, backbiting-circles, v. 49's dispute-markets; fled-from-good — mosques-avoided, kin-unvisited, v. 12's footprints-unwalked!). Feet *witness* (destinations-listed: *"he walked me to..."* — itineraries-exposed!). *"To that which they used to earn"* (*bimā-kānū-yaksibūn* — *kānū*, used-to — habitual-earning; *yaksibūn*, earn — deeds-as-earnings, v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn*): testimony's *content* (lifestyle-ledger). The Qur'an's witness-choir expands: *"ears, eyes, skins bear witness"* (41:20–23 — *"they say to skins, 'why testify against us?' — 'God made us speak, Who makes all speak'"* — skins'-apologia!); earth (99:4–5: *"recounts tidings"* — venues-testifying); angels-scribes (82:11–12: *kirāman-kātibīn*, noble-recorders — books-matching-limbs!); prophets (4:41 — community-witnesses); God (*kafā-bi-Llāhi-shahīdan*, 13:43 — suffices!). Witnesses *converge* (limbs + earth + angels + prophets + God — unanimous!). Defendants *mute* (mouths-sealed — cross-examination-impossible!). Verdict *certain* (v. 54's *lā-tuẓlam*, unwronged — evidenced!). *Feet* — witnessing. *Walk* — righteously-now (mosque-ward, v. 12's recorded-footprints; kin-ward; need-ward — itineraries-Gardenworthy!).

**Self-Testimony as Perfect Justice**

Limbs-testifying is justice *perfected*: witnesses-unbribeable (hands unthreatened — *"speak!"* obeyed), evidence-undeniable (self-flesh — *"not-me!"* impossible — *thy*-hands!), judgment-transparent (defendants *hear*-own-conviction — ḥadīth's *"away! doomed!"* — self-cursing!). *"On Judgment Day people can no longer hide sins with lies"* (source commentary): world-strategies voided (denial — limbs-confirm; excuses — sealed-mouths-mute; blame-shifting — *"leaders misled"* answered-by-limbs' *"we-obeyed-willingly"*; concealment — 86:9's *"secrets tried"*). The ḥadīth's irony (demanding *self*-witness, receiving *selves'*-witness — mouth-demanded, limbs-delivered!) displays divine *wit*: request-granted-literally (self-testifies — *which*-self? — limbs'-self!). *"Today thy soul suffices"* (ḥadīth, echoing 17:14: *"read thy book — thy soul suffices as reckoner"*) — sufficiency *multipled* (soul + scribes + limbs + earth!). *Perfect* justice (*lā-tuẓlam*, v. 54 — unwronged-*proved*): punished *hear-why* (limbs-itemized — *bi-mā-kānū*, for-what-they-did); rewarded *hear-why* (limbs-praised — foreheads-prostrated, hands-gave, feet-mosqued!). *Thy* limbs — future-witnesses (rehearsing-now!). *Direct* testimony-now (limbs-acting-righteously — witnesses-prepared!). *Silence* mouth-lies-now (truth-habituated — sealed-then-unneeded!). *Justice* — self-evidenced. *Verdict* — self-witnessed. *Perfect.*

**Employing Witnesses Righteously Now**

The verse's testifying-limbs become an employment-manual. *Hands-manual*: employ-speaking-well (give-charity — v. 47's *anfiqū* — hands'-best-speech; help-weak — lift, carry, build; write-truth — beneficial-authorship, v. 12's *ʿilm-yuntafaʿu*, knowledge-beneficial; raise-duʿāʾ — palms-up Heavenward; shake-forgive — reconciliations). Unemploy-evil (strike-none, steal-none, forge-none, touch-unlawful-none, type-slander-none — each-evil a future-sentence-uttered-by-own-flesh!). *Feet-manual*: walk-witness-well (mosque-ward — v. 12's footprints-rewarded; kin-ward — joinings; need-ward — visit-sick, funeral-follow, counsel-run, v. 20's *yasʿā*!; knowledge-ward — study-circles).
