Unwalk-evil (taverns-unentered, backbiting-circles-unjoined, dispute-markets-avoided, v. 49; sin-trysts-unwalked). *Mouth-manual*: speak-seal-worthy (truth — *"speak good or silent,"* al-Bukhārī 6018; *dhikr* — Qur'an-recited; counsel — vv. 20–25-echoed; apology — wrongs-mended). Unmouth-evil (lies-unuttered — seal-anticipated; backbiting-unspoken — *"flesh-of-brother,"* 49:12; mockery-unvoiced, v. 30). *Skin-senses-manual* (41:20–23): eyes-lowered (24:30 — gaze-witnesses!), ears-guarded (backbiting-unheard, Qur'an-heard), skins-chaste (touch-lawful). For the verse's final mercy is its *al-yawma* (that-Day): *then*-sealed (mouths), *now*-loosed (repentance-utterable! apology-speakable! testimony-*āmantu*-proclaimable, v. 25!). *Now*-mouths (testify-faith, seek-forgiveness, counsel-truth, reconcile-feuds) — *then*-limbs (confirm-righteousness!). *Employ* — now. *Testify* — then (favourably!). *Sealed-mouths* — fearless (truth-habituated). *Speaking-hands* — hoped (charity-calloused!). *Witnessing-feet* — welcomed (mosque-worn!). *Limbs* — righteous-employees. *Testimony* — Gardenward (God-willing).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:66

> **And had We willed, We would have blotted out their eyes. Then they would race to the path; yet how would they see?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Law Nashāʾu La-Ṭamasnā: Blotting Eyes at Will**

*"And had We willed, We would have blotted out their eyes"* — *wa law nashāʾu la-ṭamasnā ʿalā aʿyunihim* — *law-nashāʾ* (had-We-willed): conditional-omnipotence (vv. 43's *in-nashaʾ-nughriq*, if-We-will-drown — will-suspended-judgments). *La-ṭamasnā* (We-would-blot/obliterate — *ṭams*: effacing, erasing — *"blotted"*): eye-obliteration (sockets-smoothed, sight-erased — 4:47's *"blot faces, turn backwards"* — *ṭams*-threatened!). *ʿAlā-aʿyunihim* (upon-their-eyes): vision-organs targeted. *"Blotted out their eyes indicates God will make them blind to guidance and the straight path"* (al-Qurṭubī, al-Ṭabarī — source commentary): blinding *spiritualized* (physical-metaphor, guidance-referent — vv. 8–9's veiled-see-not *actualized*!). The conditional (*law*, had) *displays* restraint (blinding-*possible*, blinding-*withheld* — mercy-suspending-wrath, v. 44's *raḥmah*-extended!). *Could*-blind (omnipotence) yet *didn't* (respite) — deniers seeing-yet-unseeing (vv. 8–9's veiled; v. 46's turning — eyes-open, guidance-blind!). The verse *warns*: withheld-≠-withdrawn (blinding *available* — persisted-defiance *triggers*? — 4:47's Sabbath-breakers-threatened; v. 9's veiled-*precedent*!). *Seeing*-now (eyes-plus-guidance-visible — vv. 33–44's gallery-beholdable!) — *use* (heed!) before *ṭams* (blot!). *Eyes* — loaned (*in-nashāʾ*, if-willed — recallable!). *Blot* — willed-if-persisted. *See* — now (guidedly!).

**Fa-Istabaqū al-Ṣirāṭa: Racing to Path — Blindly**

*"Then they would race to the path"* — *fa-istabaqū al-ṣirāṭa* — *istabaqū* (race, compete-speeding — *sabaq*: outstripping): frantic-running (blind-panic-scurry!). *Al-ṣirāṭ* (the-path): straight-path (vv. 4, 61's *ṣirāṭ-mustaqīm* — raced-to *after*-blinding = sought-too-late!). The scene: eyes-blotted (judgment) → path-sought (remorse) → racing (desperate) → ...? (*fa-annā-yubṣirūn*, how-see? — unseeing!). Parallels: Pharaoh's drowning-race (*"I believe!"* 10:90 — path-raced-too-late — *"now?!"* rejected, 10:91); Fire-inmates' plea-race (*"remove us — we-act-righteously!"* 35:37 — raced-refused: *"did We not grant-life-enough?!"*); grave-confessors (32:12's *"return us — we-act!"* — raced-denied). Racing *requires* sight (path-seen); blotted *precludes* (path-unseen) — effort-futile-without-faculty (remorse-without-opportunity!). The verse *inverts* v. 20's runner (*yasʿā*, running-*sighted* — truth-seen, path-run-early!): runner ran *seeing* (guidance-visible — arrived-Garden, v. 26); blotted race *blind* (guidance-veiled — arrive-nowhere!). *Race*-now (sighted — vv. 20, 51's running-praised!) — *race*-then (blinded — futile!). *Istabaqū* (race) *now*: *"hasten to forgiveness"* (3:133 — *sāriʿū*, hasten! — sighted-haste commanded); *"race to good"* (2:148's *istabiqū-al-khayrāt* — same-verb! — racing-commended-*now*!). *Race* — sighted-now. *Arrive* — Garden-then (v. 58's *salām*!).

**Fa-Annā Yubṣirūn: "Yet How Would They See?"**

*"Yet how would they see?"* — *fa-annā yubṣirūn* — *annā* (how?/whence?): impossibility-interrogative (seeing-*unpossible* — eyes-blotted!). *Yubṣirūn* (see/perceive — *baṣar*: sight + insight): vision *voided* (physical *and* spiritual — v. 9's *lā-yubṣirūn*, see-not — *echoed*!). The rhetorical-answer: *nowhere-how* (no-seeing — blotted-permanently). The verse's logic-chain: will (divine) → blot (eyes) → race (futile) → unseeing (permanent). *Annā* (how) *mocks* belated-effort (racing-without-eyes — running-without-route — remorse-without-result!). *"How-see?"* answers v. 48's *"when?"* (timing-mocked — *then*-unseeing!) and v. 9's *lā-yubṣirūn* (see-not *now* — veiled!) fulfilled *then* (see-not *ever* — blotted!). *Yubṣirūn* (see) contrasts believers' *then*-sight (75:23's *nāẓirah*, gazing-at-Lord — vision-crowned!; 50:22's *ḥadīd*, piercing-sight — veils-removed!). Two *then*-visions: believers' *enhanced* (Lord-beheld!) versus blotted's *erased* (path-unseen!). *Now*-visions decide: *baṣīrah*-cultivated (insight — signs-pondered, vv. 33–44; Reminder-followed, v. 11) → *then*-vision-crowned; *baṣar*-misused (signs-fled, v. 46; lust-gazed, 24:30-violated) → *then*-vision-blotted. *See* — now (guidedly — *baṣīrah*!). *See* — then (Lord!-75:23). *Unseeing*-now (heedless) → *unseeing*-then (*annā-yubṣirūn* — how-see? — *unhowed*!).

**Withheld-Blinding as Respite's Proof**

*Law-nashāʾ* (had-We-willed — *but-We-didn't*) *proves* respite (v. 44's *ilā-ḥīn*, enjoyment-while): blinding-withheld = mercy-extended (eyes-open *still*, path-visible *still*, racing-possible *still* — *sighted*!). The Qur'an's withheld-punishments gallery: *"were God to take for wrongdoing — not leave creature"* (16:61 — taking-withheld!); *"were He to hasten evil as good-hastened — term-decided"* (10:11 — hastening-withheld!); *"He defers to appointed-term"* (16:61; 35:45 — deferral!). Each-withholding a *niʿmah* (blessing — time-to-repent, 39:53; space-to-return, v. 61's path-open). Deniers *misread* withholdings (as impotence — *"where is punishment?"* 22:47's hastening-demanded; as approval — *"God loves us!"* wealth-misread, 89:15–16). The verse *corrects*: withholding = *law-nashāʾ* (will-suspended — *could*, choosing-not-*yet*). *Yet* (expiration: death-individual, v. 50's no-bequest; Cry-collective, v. 49; blinding-judicial, here). *Read* withholdings rightly (mercy, not immunity — v. 44's *raḥmah*-thanked, not presumed!). *Eyes*-open-now (withholding!) — *use* (read-verses! weep-fear! v. 11; behold-signs! vv. 33–44). *Blot*-then (persisted!) — *avoid* (heed-now!). *Withheld* — thanked. *Extended* — spent-well (*ilā-ḥīn*-budgeted, v. 44). *Blinding* — never (God-refuge!).

**Seeing While Sighted: Practices of the Unblotted**

The verse's eyes become a vision-regimen. *Guard eyes*: lower-from-unlawful (24:30–31 — gaze-guarded — *baṣar*-preserved for Lord-gazing, 75:23!); weep-from-awe (v. 11's unseen-fearers — tears-as-eye-*zakāh* — *"eyes-weeping-never-Fire,"* v. 63); read-revelation (Qur'an-gazed — verses-beheld — *baṣīrah*-fed). *Unguard*-consequences (lust-gazing — heart-blotted-pre-eyes; heedlessness-gazing — signs-unseen, v. 46 — *ṭams*-preludes!). *Hasten sighted*: *istabiqū-al-khayrāt* (2:148 — race-good-*now*!): dawn-mosque-raced (sleep-beaten), charity-raced (miserliness-outrun), forgiveness-raced (grudges-outpaced), knowledge-raced (ignorance-outstripped). *Racing*-now (sighted!) — arriving-then (Garden!). *Ponder blottedness*: visualize (*law-ṭamasnā* — had-blotted: darkness-perpetual, path-unseen, racing-futile — dread-as-deterrent); thank (*un*-blotted: eyes-opening-daily, v. 37's dawn-raising-sighted! — *ḥamd* for vision-spiritual-and-physical). *Beg vision*: *duʿāʾ*-for-sight (*"O God, show truth-as-truth..."* v. 9; *"delight of gazing-Face,"* v. 58's al-Nasāʾī-1305; *"light in heart, sight, hearing..."* — Prophet's ﷺ light-*duʿāʾ*, Muslim 763). For the verse's final mercy is its *law* (had — *unactualized*!): blotting *hypothetical* (warned!), sight *actual* (reading-this-verse *proves*-unblotted!). *Hypothetical*-then (persisted!) — *actual*-now (sighted!). *See* (this-verse!) — proof-mercy. *Heed* (this-warning!) — gratitude. *Race* (sighted!) — arrival (God-willing). *Ṭams* — never. *Yubṣirūn* — ever (Lord-gazing, 75:23!). *Eyes* — Garden-bound.

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:67

> **And had We willed, We would have transformed them in their places. Then they could neither advance nor go back.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Law Nashāʾu La-Masakhnāhum: Transformation at Will**

*"And had We willed, We would have transformed them"* — *wa law nashāʾu la-masakhnāhum* — *law-nashāʾ* (had-We-willed): second-conditional (v. 66's blinding + here transforming — twin-withhelds!). *La-masakhnāhum* (We-would-transform/metamorphose — *maskh*: transfiguration-into-lower-form — *"transformed into apes-and-pigs,"* 5:60; Sabbath-breakers-apes, 2:65; 7:166): shape-perverted (human-form revoked!). *"Seen by some as allusion to transforming into animals such as monkeys and pigs"* (al-Ṭabarsī — source commentary): *maskh*-precedents (Israelites'-Sabbath-violated → apes-despised, 2:65 — *"be apes, despised!"*; flatterers-threatened — *"transformed... "* ḥadīth-promised for music-usury-singers-ending, al-Bukhārī 5590's instruments-permitted-punished). *Hum* (them): deniers (vv. 66–67's persistent — blinding-/transforming-deserving!). *Maskh* (transformation) *fits* (inward-beastliness outwarded: swine-like-gluttons, ape-like-mockers — v. 30's *yastahziʾūn*! — forms-following-souls!). Withheld (*law*, had — *didn't*!): mercy (forms-honored, 17:70's *karramnā*, honored-Adamites — honour-*extended*!). *Could*-deform (omnipotence) yet *preserved*-form (respite, v. 44): faces-human *still* (mirrors-graced!), bodies-upright *still* (17:70's *faḍḍalnā*, favoured!). *Preserved* — thanked (*shukr*, v. 35 — form-gratitude!). *Threatened* (*maskh*-available!) — heeded (beastliness-shed-now — gluttony-mockery-cruelty-quit — forms-secured!). *Transformed* — never (God-refuge!). *Honoured*-form — kept (obediently!).

**ʿAlā Makānatihim: "In Their Places" — Immobilized Where Standing**

*"In their places"* — *ʿalā makānatihim* — *makānah* (place, station, dwelling — *"in their dwellings"* alternate, source commentary): transformation *localized* (where-standing/sitting/reclining — no-relocation-needed!). *"No matter where they may be, God can alter people's state instantly or destroy them if He wills"* (source commentary): omnipresence-of-power (palaces-unshielding — v. 43's *lā-ṣarīkha*, none-answering; fortresses-futile — *"wherever you are, death finds — though in towers,"* 4:78!). *ʿAlā-makānatihim* (in-places) also *freezes* (statuesque: transformed-*and*-rooted — next-clause's immobility!). Precedents: Sabbath-apes (in-villages — neighbours-beholding!); town-Cry (v. 29 — in-town — ash-localized!); Badr-dead (in-field — well-filled!). Localization *displays* (punishment-witnessed — survivors-warned — v. 31's *iʿtibār*, lesson-taking!). *Thy* makānah (place): office? home? mosque? market? (v. 49's disputing-places!) — *any*-place transformable (*law-nashāʾ* — had-willed!). Sanctify-places (worship-filled — mosques-frequented, homes-prayed — *makānah*-blessed); quit-sinful-places (taverns, backbiting-circles — *makānah*-cursed — transformable-*there*!). *Places* — witnessed (earth-testifies, 99:4 — venues-remember!). *Transformed-in-place* — dreaded (sin-spots!). *Honoured-in-place* — hoped (worship-spots — *"earth will testify: he prayed-here,"* 99:4-contexts!). *Makānah* — sanctified-now. *Maskh* — never-here (God-willing!).

**Fa-Mā Istaṭāʿū Muḍiyyan Wa Lā Yarjiʿūn: Neither Advance Nor Return**

*"Then they could neither advance nor go back"* — *fa-mā istaṭāʿū muḍiyyan wa lā yarjiʿūn* — *mā-istaṭāʿū* (could-not — power-stripped): paralysis (post-transformation). *Muḍiyyan* (advance/proceed — forward-motion): progress-impossible (path-unwalkable — v. 66's racing-futile echoed-bodily!). *Wa-lā-yarjiʿūn* (nor-return — *rajʿ*: going-back): retreat-impossible (repentance-unwalkable — return-road-blocked!). Double-immobility (forward-*and*-backward — frozen!): statuesque-damned (pillars-of-former-men — Lot's-wife-salt-pillar-echo, 11:81's *"turn-not"* violated!). The couplet *inverts* believers' mobility (advance-to-God, v. 51's *yansilūn*, rushing-Lordward; return-to-God, 89:28's *irjiʿī*, return-pleased!): damned *neither*-advance (good-unreachable) *nor*-return (repentance-closed!). *"See also 4:47c"* (source commentary — faces-blotted-turned — mobility-moral-voided!). Immobility *previews*: grave-confinement (*barzakh*-bounded, 23:100 — neither-world-nor-Hereafter-fully); Fire-fixity (shackled, 14:49–50 — *muqarranīn*, chained-together!). *Mobility*-now (advance-able! return-able! — v. 44's *ilā-ḥīn*, while-extended!): *advance* (good-steps — mosque-ward, v. 12; knowledge-ward; charity-ward); *return* (repentance-steps — sin-quit, rights-restored, 39:53's open-door!). *Advance-and-return*-now — *advance-and-return*-then (Garden-roaming, 39:73's *taḥiyyah*-welcomed; Lord-returning, 89:28!). *Frozen*-then (persisted!) — *fluid*-now (repentant!). *Move* — now!

**Twin Withhelds: Blindness and Transformation as Mercy-Pair**

Verses 66–67 form mercy's *pair*: blinding-withheld (sight-extended) + transforming-withheld (form-extended). Withholdings *prove* (v. 44's *raḥmah*, mercy-from-Us — evidenced!): *could*-blind (eyes-owed!) yet *didn't* (sight-gifted!); *could*-deform (forms-owed!) yet *didn't* (humanity-gifted!). Pair's logic: faculties (sight) + forms (bodies) *both*-loaned (*in-nashāʾ*, if-willed-recallable — v. 43's drowning-loans!). Gratitude's double-duty (eyes-thanked, v. 66; bodies-thanked, here — *"who shaped you perfectly?"* 82:7–8 — *sawwāka-fa-ʿadalak*, leveled-proportioned!). Misuse's double-peril (sight-misused → blotting-risked; form-misused → transforming-risked — beastly-acts beastly-ending!). The Qur'an's form-theology: honored-forms (17:70's *karramnā* — dignity-bestowed); proportioned-forms (95:4's *aḥsani-taqwīm*, finest-mould — beauty-entrusted!); regressing-forms (v. 68's *nunakkishu*, regress-in-creation — age-reminding!); resurrected-forms (v. 79's re-created — Knower-reassembling!). Forms *trajectory*: honoured (created) → used (tested) → regressed (aged, v. 68) → dissolved (died) → reassembled (raised, v. 51) → eternally-formed (Garden-beautified / Fire-deformed, 4:56's skins-renewed!). *Thy*-form: *where* trajectory? (youth-using? age-regressing? — v. 68!). *Use* honouredly (worship-postured — prostrations-beautifying; charity-handed; mosque-footed, v. 12!). *Withhelds* — thanked-doubly. *Pair* — preserved (sight-and-form-Garden-bound, God-willing!).

**Moving While Mobile: Practices of the Unfrozen**

The verse's immobility-threat becomes a mobility-charter. *Advance daily*: *muḍī* (proceeding) — forward-steps (knowledge-plus-verse-daily; charity-plus-coin-daily; character-plus-patience-daily; worship-plus-*rakʿah*-nightly, v. 37!). *Stagnation* (no-advance — days-identical — v. 6's *ghāfilūn*, heedless-routine!) preludes-freezing (*mā-istaṭāʿū-muḍiyyan* — advance-unable!). *Return readily*: *rajʿah* (returning) — backward-steps-to-God (sins-repented-immediately — *"follow-evil-with-good,"* v. 61; wrongs-righted — apologies-issued; lapses-recovered — prayers-made-up). *Unreturning* (persisting — v. 7's Word-due-path!) preludes-locking (*wa-lā-yarjiʿūn* — return-unable!). *Sanctify stations*: *makānah*-audit (places-frequented: mosques-increased, sin-spots-abandoned, homes-worshiped, workplaces-honested — venues-testifying-favourably, 99:4!). *Transfigure inwardly*: *maskh*-inverted (beastliness-to-beauty — gluttony-fasted, mockery-blessed, v. 30-resisted; cruelty-kinded — forms'-souls-humanized!). The Prophet's ﷺ transfiguration-program: *"I was sent to perfect character"* (Aḥmad 2:381; graded ṣaḥīḥ — *makārim*-perfecting as anti-*maskh*!). For the verse's final mercy is its *law* (had — *unactualized*, v. 66!): transforming *hypothetical* (warned!), mobility *actual* (advance-able! return-able! — *now*!). *Hypothetical*-then (persisted!) — *actual*-now (mobile!). *Advance* (proceeding-good!). *Return* (repenting-swift!). *Move* — now (fluid!). *Frozen* — never (God-refuge!). *Muḍī-and-rajʿah* — Garden-mobility-eternal (roaming-rejoicing, vv. 55–57; returning-pleased, 89:28!). *Move!*

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:68

> **And whomsoever We give long life, We cause him to regress in creation. Do they not understand?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Man Nuʿammirhu Nunakkishu Fī al-Khalq: Long Life, Regressed Creation**

*"And whomsoever We give long life, We cause him to regress in creation"* — *wa man nuʿammirhu nunakkishu fī al-khalq* — *nuʿammir* (give-long-life — *taʿmīr*: age-extending, life-prolonging): longevity *granted* (divine-gift — *"to some given-long-life,"* 22:5). *Nunakkishu* (cause-to-regress/reverse — *naks*: turning-upside-down, inverting — *"regress"*): aging-as-*inversion* (growth-reversed!). *Fī-al-khalq* (in-creation): created-form *regressed*. *"Refers to physical and mental decline in old age"* (source commentary): strength→weakness (muscles-wasted, bones-brittled, steps-shuffled); senses→dullness (eyes-dimmed, ears-deafened, taste-blunted); mind→forgetfulness (memories-faded — *"after knowledge, knows-naught,"* 16:70; 22:5 — learned-then-lost!); independence→dependence (fed-like-infants, carried-like-children — cradle-to-cradle!). The Qur'an's life-curve: *"God created you from weakness, then ordained strength after weakness, then ordained weakness-and-old-age after strength"* (30:54 — weakness→strength→weakness — bell-curve!); *"stages from conception to old-age as signs"* (22:5 — *nuṭfah*→*ʿalaqah*→*muḍghah*→infant→maturity→aged — embryology-to-gerontology!). *Nunakkis* (regress) *displays* (power-over-forms — v. 67's *maskh*-withheld yet *naks*-enacted! — transformation *slow* (aging) though sudden-withheld (metamorphosis)!). Long-life *paradox*: gift (years-added) + regress (powers-subtracted) — *more*-time, *less*-capacity. *Wisdom*: youth's illusion (strength-permanent!) *corrected* (strength-loaned!); eternity's hunger (forever-young!) *directed* (Garden-youth, 56:35–37's ageless!). *Regress* — witnessed (elders-beheld!). *Understand* — hence (next-clause!).

**"Do They Not Understand?": The Argument From Aging**

*"Do they not understand?"* — *a-fa-lā-yaʿqilūn* — *a-fa-lā* (do-then-not?): rebuke-question (vv. 62's *a-fa-lam-taʿqilūn*, did-not-understand — echoed!). *Yaʿqilūn* (understand/reason — *ʿaql*): intellect-invoked. *"Here meant as argument, as if to say, 'Does not the One who has power to do this have power to resurrect you?'"* (source commentary): aging→resurrection-inference (Regresser = Raiser!). Logic: He-who *inverts* (strength→weakness — form-remade-downward) *can* revert (dust→life — form-remade-upward!); He-who *regresses*-creation (*naks*) *can* resurrect-creation (*baʿth*, v. 52!). *A-fortiori*: regressing-living (harder — delicate-unmaking!) proves raising-dead (simpler — decreed-remaking, v. 79's Knower!). The argument *also* inverts youth's arrogance (v. 77's *"manifest-adversary"* — strong-man disputing-Resurrector! — strength *scheduled*-for-regress!): disputers-aging (bodies-betraying — knees-creaking mid-argument, memories-failing mid-dispute!). *Yaʿqilūn* (understand) *demands*: see-elderly (regressed!) → infer-Resurrector (powerful!) → believe-raising (certain, vv. 51–53!) → prepare (deeds, v. 54!). *Understand-not?* (*lā-yaʿqilūn* — v. 62's heap-members!) — *witness*-regress-yet-deny-raise (contradiction-lived!). *A-fa-lā* (do-not?): *behoves*-understanding (evidence-daily — mirrors-aging, parents-frailing!). *Understand!* — regress-proves-raise. *Raise* — believed. *Prepare* — now.

**Long Life as Trial, Not Trophy**

*Nuʿammir* (long-life-given) reframes longevity: *trial* (tested), not *trophy* (boasted). Long-life's *tests*: gratitude-in-decline (thanking-while-frailing — health-remembered, 14:7's increase-sought); patience-in-loss (powers-mourned-yet-borne — *"no fatigue, illness... but expiates,"* al-Bukhārī 5640 — regress-as-redemption!); worship-in-weakness (prayers-adapted — sitting-if-standing-unable, al-Bukhārī 1117's *"pray standing; if unable, sitting"* — concessions-honouring!); wisdom-in-age (counsel-offered — elders'-experience shared — *"blessing with elders,"* al-Mustadrak — gray-hairs-guided!). Long-life's *perils*: heedlessness-extended (v. 44's *ilā-ḥīn* misused — years-wasted!); sins-accumulated (habits-hardened — v. 8's shackles-tightened!); repentance-delayed (*"did We not grant-life-enough to remember?!"* 35:37 — longevity-prosecuting!); understanding-lost (16:70's *"knows-naught-after-knowledge"* — minds-regressed-past-repentance-capacity?!). The Prophet's ﷺ longevity-*duʿāʾ*: sought-refuge from *"miserable-old-age"* (*ardhal-al-ʿumur*, 16:70's vilest-age — al-Bukhārī 6376: *"I seek refuge... from miserable-old-age"* — senility-dreaded!) yet *praised* righteous-longevity (*"best are long-lived-good-deeded,"* al-Tirmidhī 2329 — years-plus-righteousness!). *Long*-life: *good* (if righteous-growing!) — *perilous* (if heedless-aging!). *Aged?* — intensify (deeds-concentrated — time-shortened, value-heightened!). *Young?* — anticipate (*nunakkis*-coming! — youth-invested — *"five-before-five: youth-before-old-age,"* v. 44!). *Life* — lengthened-for-goodness. *Goodness* — lengthened-life's purpose.

**Honouring the Regressed: Elders' Rights**

*Nunakkis* (regressed-creation) *commands* elder-honour (regressed-yet-revered!): weakness *increases* (not decreases) rights! The Qur'an's elder-charter: *"lower wing-of-humility out of mercy, pray: 'Lord, mercy them as they raised me small'"* (17:24 — *dhull*-humility + *duʿāʾ*!); *"say not 'uff' nor repel, speak noble-word"* (17:23 — irritation-forbidden, nobility-commanded!). The Prophet's ﷺ elder-sunnah: *"not of us who honours-not-elders"* (Abū Dāwūd 4943; al-Tirmidhī 1920 — graded ṣaḥīḥ — *belonging*-conditioned-on-honouring!); *"gray-hair-Muslim honoured"* (*"honouring-gray-haired-Muslim is glorifying-God,"* Abū Dāwūd 4843 — ḥasan — elder-honour = divine-glorification!); shortened-prayers-for-weak (*"whoever leads, let him lighten — among-them weak, sick, aged,"* al-Bukhārī 703). *Nunakkis*-witnessed (parents-frailing!) → *birr*-enacted (dutifulness: patience-with-repetition, gentleness-with-dependence, presence-with-loneliness, *duʿāʾ*-with-love!). Societies elder-warehousing (nursing-homes-as-abandonment!) violate *nunakkis*-ethic (regressed-*cherished*, not discarded!). *Thy*-regress-coming (*nuʿammirhu-nunakkis* — *thee*, if-aged!): honour-now (sowing!) → honoured-then (reaping! — *"as you treat, treated,"* v. 27's law!). *Elders* — regressed-in-creation, *advanced*-in-right. *Honour* — commanded. *Regress* — reverenced.

**Aging Wisely Now, Rising Youthfully Then**

The verse's *nunakkis* (regress) becomes a life-plan. *Invest youth*: strength-spent-Gardenward (worship-vigorous — night-prayers-while-able, v. 37; service-strenuous — ḥajj-while-strong, charity-while-earning, v. 47; knowledge-sought — memory-sharp-while-young!). *"Youth-before-old-age"* (v. 44's five!) — *now*! *Navigate middle-age*: peak-stewarded (powers-plateaued — leadership-just, wealth-purified, family-guided — *quwwah*-trusteeship!); decline-anticipated (wills-written, v. 50; habits-honed — worship-automatized-pre-frailty!). *Embrace elderliness*: regress-accepted (gracefully — complaints-minimized, gratitude-maximized — *"patience-beautiful,"* 12:83); worship-adapted (concessions-used-gratefully — sitting-prayers, shortened-fast-makeups — *rukhaṣ*-mercies!); wisdom-shared (counsel-generous — grandchildren-taught, communities-advised — *naks*-compensated-by-*ḥikmah*!); death-prepared (longing-meeting — *"loves-meeting,"* v. 51 — regress-as-departure-lounge!). *Hope resurrection-youth*: Garden-de-aging (*"age-thirty-three,"* v. 55's al-Tirmidhī-2545 — eternal-prime!; *"youths-immortal,"* 76:19 — serving-ageless!). Regress *reversed*-eternally (Raiseer unrestrained — v. 79!). For the verse's final mercy is its *man* (whomsoever): *nuʿammirhu*-conditional (long-life-*if*-granted — not-all-aged! — youth-deaths: martyrs-crowned-early, v. 26; children-spared-trials!). *Granted*-long (regress-witnessed — *understand*!). *Granted*-short (regress-spared — *thank*!). *Either* — Raiser-same (v. 79). *A-fa-lā-yaʿqilūn* (understand-not?): *understand!* (regress-observed → raise-believed → prepare-enacted!). *Regress* — tutor. *Raise* — certain. *Understand* — now.

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:69

> **And We have not taught him poetry; nor would it befit him. It is but a reminder and a clear Quran,**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Mā ʿAllamnāhu al-Shiʿr: Poetry Untaught**

*"And We have not taught him poetry"* — *wa mā ʿallamnāhu al-shiʿra* — *mā-ʿallamnā* (We-taught-not): divine *non*-teaching (curriculum-excluded!). *Hu* (him): Prophet Muḥammad ﷺ (v. 3's sent; v. 4's straight-pathed). *Al-shiʿr* (poetry: metered-rhymed Arabic-verse — *qaṣīdah*-culture, odes-competitive). *"Returns to nature-of-revelation from sūrah's beginning"* (source commentary — vv. 2–5's Book-theme resumed!): revelation-defended (against poet-charge, 26:224–27's *"poets-followed-by-straying"*; 37:36's *"mad-poet?"*; 52:30's *"poet-awaited-calamity"*). *"According to several accounts, the Prophet did not have facility with poetry, either rhyme or meter"* (Ibn Kathīr, al-Qurṭubī — source commentary): attempted-verses *broke* (meters-collapsed! — companion-witnessed: reciting-others'-lines *mis*-metered — unpoetic-tongue *proved*!). Untaught-poetry *proves* un-authored-Qur'an (illiterate-unpoetic producing *inimitable*-eloquence — 2:23's challenge-unmet!): *"thou didst not recite before any-Book; nor didst thou write with right-hand — then doubters would-doubt"* (29:48 — *ummī*-unlettered + unpoetic = miracle-compounded!). *Mā-ʿallamnā* (We-taught-not) *also* honours (omission-as-protection: poetry's *associations* — exaggeration, falsehood-licence, passion-excess — *unbefitting*-prophethood, next-clause!). *Untaught* — protected. *Unpoetic* — proved-true (*ṣadaqa*, v. 52!). *Poet?* — never. *Prophet* — ever (v. 3!).

**Wa Mā Yanbaghī Lah: "Nor Would It Befit Him"**

*"Nor would it befit him"* — *wa mā yanbaghī lah* — *mā-yanbaghī* (befits-not — v. 40's cosmic-*yanbaghī*, sun-befits-not-overtaking — propriety-vocabulary!): poetry *improper*-for-prophets. *Why* unbefitting? — poetry's *nature* (imagination-soaring-truth-bending — *"they say what they do-not,"* 26:226 — poets'-confessed-falsehood!; passion-inflaming — love/hate-excess; patron-flattering — praise-purchased!): prophethood's *opposite* (truth-uttering, 53:3–4's *"speaks-not-from-desire"*; sobriety-commanding; God-alone-praising!). Poets *followed-by-straying* (26:224 — *ghāwūn*-entourage!); prophets *followed-by-guided* (v. 21's *muhtadūn*!). *"Nor befit"* thus *elevates* (prophet-above-poet — heaven's-spokesman outranks imagination's-minstrel!). The charge's *irony* (Quraysh calling Qur'an-poetry): poetry *known*-to-them (markets-competed — ʿUkāẓ-fairs!); Qur'an *unlike*-it (challenged-unmatched — *"bring sūrah-like-it,"* 2:23 — poets-silent!). *"Befit"* also *protects-followers* (scholars-preachers — eloquence-suspected-if-poetic-excess?: truth-plain — v. 17's *balāgh-mubīn*, clear-proclamation — prioritized-over-rhyme!). *Yanbaghī*-ethic (befittingness — v. 40's orbits-proper!): each-station its-propriety (prophets-truth, poets-imagination — confusion-forbidden!). *Unbefitting* — unattempted. *Truth* — uttered (*ṣadaqa*, v. 52!). *Poetry* — left-to-poets. *Revelation* — kept-pure.

**In Huwa Illā Dhikrun: "But a Reminder"**

*"It is but a reminder"* — *in huwa illā dhikrun* — *in...illā* (naught...but): Qur'an *defined* (essence-captured!). *Dhikr* (reminder/remembrance — v. 11's *al-dhikr*, Reminder-followed!): revelation-as-*reminding* (not inventing — *re*-minding: covenant, 7:172; *fiṭrah*, 30:30; former-peoples, v. 31; death-judgment, vv. 49–54 — *known*-forgotten recalled!). *"But a reminder is repeated in several verses"* (6:69, 90; 12:104; 38:87; 68:52; 74:31; 81:27 — source commentary): reminder-refrain (Qur'an's self-title insistent!). *"To remind is function of all revelation; Torah called Reminder"* (40:54 — *dhikrā*; *"People-of-Book as people-of-Reminder,"* 16:43; 21:7 — *ahl-al-dhikr*!): reminders-chained (Torah → Gospel → Qur'an — *"continuation clarifying what forgotten,"* source commentary; 16:44: *"We sent Reminder to clarify what sent-down"* — Muḥammad-clarifying!). *Dhikr* (reminder) *also* remembrance (God-remembered — *"remembrance gives-hearts-rest,"* 13:28!): Book-reminding → hearts-remembering (v. 11's followers *dhākirūn*!). Reminder-vs-poetry: poetry *distracts* (imagination-wandering!); reminder *returns* (attention-homing!); poetry *invents* (novelties!); reminder *recalls* (verities!); poetry *entertains* (evenings!); reminder *saves* (eternities!). *Dhikr* — essence. *Reminded* — guided (v. 11!). *Forgetful* — perishing (v. 6's *ghāfilūn*!). *Remember!*

**Wa Qurʾānun Mubīn: "And a Clear Quran"**

*"And a clear Quran"* — *wa qurʾānun mubīn* — *qurʾān* (recitation/reading — *qaraʾa*: gathered-recited): revelation-as-*recital* (heard, memorized, 15:9's preserved!). *Mubīn* (clear — v. 12's *imām-mubīn*, clear-registry; v. 17's *balāgh-mubīn*, clear-proclamation; v. 24's *ḍalāl-mubīn*-inverted!): clarity-manifest (language-Arabic-clear, 26:195; guidance-unambiguous, 2:256's rectitude-distinct!). *"Clear"* refutes obscurity-charges ( *foreign-tongue?* — *"Arabic-clear,"* 16:103; *muddled-dreams?* — 21:5's *aḍghāth*-charged — *mubīn*-answered!). Clarity's *proofs*: illiterates-guided (Companions-transformed!); scholars-unexhausted (centuries-commentaries — Ṭabarī, Kathīr, Qurṭubī, Rāzī — depths-unplumbed!); challenge-unmet (2:23 — clarity-plus-inimitability!). *Qurʾān-mubīn* (clear-recital) *completes* v. 2's *qurʾān-ḥakīm* (wise-Qur'an): wise-*and*-clear (wisdom-accessible! — depth-clarified!). *"It is but reminder and clear-Qur'an"* — two-nouns: *function* (remind!) + *form* (recite-clearly!). Function-form-perfect: reminding-needed (heedlessness-epidemic, v. 6!) + form-fitting (clarity-memorable-recitable!). *Poetry?* — untaught-unbefitting. *Reminder?* — yes-essentially. *Clear-Qur'an?* — yes-formally. *Receive* as-reminder (heed!) + *recite* as-Qur'an (memorize-chant-convey!). *Dhikr-mubīn* — guidance-clarified. *Clarified* — followed (v. 11!). *Followed* — saved (v. 58's *salām*!).

**Receiving Reminder, Reciting Clearly**

The verse's *dhikr-mubīn* (clear-reminder) becomes a reception-manual. *Receive-as-reminder*: approach-Qur'an *re*-minded (covenant-remembered, 7:172 — *"am I not Lord? — Yea!"*; death-remembered, vv. 49–54; blessings-remembered, vv. 33–44 — readings-as-rememberings!). Forgetfulness-cured-sessionally (daily-portion — *hizb* — heedlessness-antidote, v. 6!). *Recite-as-Qur'an*: recite-clearly (*tartīl*, 73:4 — measured-melodious; *tajwīd*-studied — letters-honoured!); memorize-progressively (even-Yā-Sīn-full — Introduction's memorized-longest! — hearts-tableted!); convey-faithfully (teach — *"best learn-and-teach-Qur'an,"* al-Bukhārī 5027 — *qurʾān*-transmitted!). *Defend-as-neither-poetry*: answer poet-charges (ancient: 26:224–27 — poets-vs-prophets-contrasted; modern: "Muḥammad-authored" — *ummī*-unpoetic-inimitable-triple-proof, 29:48 + here + 2:23!). *Distinguish* truth-eloquence (Qur'an — clarity-serving-guidance!) from false-eloquence (poetic-excess, rhetorical-manipulation — beauty-serving-vanity!). For the verse's final mercy is its *in...illā* (naught-but): Qur'an *reduced* (to-reminder-clear — simplicity!) yet *inexhaustible* (reminder-layers-infinite — Ṭabarī-to-Ālūsī-centuries!). *Simple* (reminded!) — *profound* (oceans!). *Untaught-poetry* — miracle. *Unbefitting* — honour. *Reminder* — received. *Clear-Qur'an* — recited. *Dhikr* — remembered. *Mubīn* — followed-clearly. *Poet?* — never. *Prophet* — forever (v. 3's *mina-al-mursalīn*!).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:70

> **To warn whomsoever is alive, and so that the Word may come due for the disbelievers.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Li-Yundhira Man Kāna Ḥayyan: Warning the Living**

*"To warn whomsoever is alive"* — *li-yundhira man kāna ḥayyan* — *li-* (to/in-order): Qur'an's *purpose*-clause (v. 69's reminder-clear *aimed*!). *Yundhira* (warn — *indhār*: v. 6's *tundhira*, warn-people; v. 11's *tundhiru*, warn-followers): warning-as-mission (mercy-alarmed!). *Man-kāna-ḥayyan* (whomsoever-is/was-alive): life-qualified-audience. *"From one perspective, all humanity"* (6:19: *"I warn you and whomsoever it reaches"* — Ibn Kathīr — source commentary): *ḥayy* (alive) = living-breathing (corpse-excluded — warning-benefits-pre-grave!). *"From another, those whose hearts are alive"* (al-Qurṭubī — source commentary): *ḥayy* = spiritually-vivified (*"able to hear warning and soften unto remembrance,"* 39:23 — *talīnu*, softening-hearts!). Both-true: *universally*-offered (all-living warned!) + *effectively*-received (heart-living heed! — v. 11's *innamā-tundhiru*, only-warn-followers!). *"Alive"* contrasts v. 10's dead-hearts (warning-equals-silence — corpses-deaf!) and v. 12's *mawtā* (dead — God-revives-spiritually-or-physically!). The Qur'an's life-vocabulary: *"shall We grant life to one dead, making light to walk-by?"* (6:122 — believer-revived!); *"thou canst not make dead hear"* (30:52 — deniers-deaf!). *Ḥayy* (alive) *invites* self-diagnosis (heart-beating-spiritually?: verses-trembled-at? 8:2; warnings-softened-by? 39:23; counsel-welcomed? vv. 20–25!). *Alive?* — warned-beneficially (heed!). *Dead?* — revivable (v. 12's *nuḥyī-al-mawtā*, We-revive-dead — breathe-seeking!). *Warned* — living. *Living* — heeding. *Heeding* — saved (v. 11's *maghfirah-ajr*!).

**Wa Yaḥiqqa al-Qawlu ʿAlā al-Kāfirīn: Word Due Upon Deniers**

*"And so that the Word may come due for the disbelievers"* — *wa yaḥiqqa al-qawlu ʿalā al-kāfirīn* — *yaḥiqqa* (come-due/prove-true — *ḥaqqa*: v. 7's *ḥaqqa-al-qawlu*, Word-due!): sentence-falling. *Al-qawl* (the-Word): punishment-decree (v. 7's Hell-Word, 40:6; 32:13). *ʿAlā-al-kāfirīn* (upon-disbelievers — *kāfir*: coverers-truth, vv. 7, 47, 64's deniers): sentence-targeted. *"For meaning of Word coming due, see 36:7c"* (source commentary): v. 7's *akthar*-upon (most-due) *generalized*-here (*al-kāfirīn*, the-disbelievers — class-wide!). Purpose-duality (*li-yundhira... wa-yaḥiqqa*: warn-living *and* sentence-denying): revelation *saves-or-seals* (v. 7's *"proof for-or-against,"* Muslim-223 — Qur'an-double-edged!). Warned-living-responding → saved (vv. 55–58); warned-living-persisting → Word-due (vv. 59–64). *Yaḥiqqa* (due) *requires* warning-prior (17:15's *"never-punish-till-messenger"* — justice-sequenced: warn-*then*-due!). *Al-kāfirīn* (disbelievers) *defined* (persisters-post-warning — not uninformed — v. 6's warned-yet-heedless-become!). Word's *due*-mechanics: hearts-sealed (vv. 8–9 — persistent-refusal-congealed); mouths-sealed-then (v. 65 — excuses-ended); Fire-entered (v. 64's *iṣlaw* — sentence-executed). *Due* — justly (*lā-tuẓlam*, v. 54 — unwronged!). *Warned* — living. *Due* — denying-persistently. *Denying?* — repent-pre-due (39:53's door-open!). *Due-then* — irreversible (*ḥaqqa*-fallen!).

**The Living and the Word-Due: Revelation's Double Audience**

The verse *divides* humanity (warning's two-outcomes): *ḥayy*-heeders (living-hearing — saved!) vs *kāfir*-persisters (covering-despite-warning — Word-due!). Division *previews* v. 59's *wamtāzū* (stand-apart!): warned-sorted (heeders-Gardenward, persisters-Fireward). Division *explains* v. 10–11 (warning-void-for-sealed, warning-fruitful-for-followers): *ḥayy* (v. 11's) vs Word-due (v. 10's). Division *recurs*: parable-town (runner-*ḥayy*, vv. 20–27-saved; stoners-persisting, vv. 18–29-Cried); Noah (few-*ḥayy*, 11:40-boarded; multitudes-persisting-drowned); Quraysh (Companions-*ḥayy*, Badr-victors-eternal; chiefs-persisting, Badr-felled). *Ḥayy* (alive) *cultivable* (dead-revivable! — v. 12's Reviver; 6:122's dead enlivened!): lifelessness *treatable* (remembrance-resuscitates — *dhikr*-CPR!; company-revives — righteous-contagious!; tears-irrigate — v. 11's unseen-fear!). *Kāfir*-persisting *curable-pre-due* (repentance-pre-death — 39:53's *all*-sins; Word-*not-yet*-due-while-breathing!): due-ness *post*-mortem-finalized (v. 52's confessing-too-late!). *Audience*-now (readers-living!): *ḥayy*-potential (hearts-beating — heed!) — Word-due-avoidable (persist-not!). *Living?* — warned (heed-savingly!). *Persisting?* — Word-nearing (repent-urgently!). *Two*-audiences; *one*-warning; *two*-ends (vv. 55–64). *Choose* living-heeding. *Flee* persisting-dued.

**Warning as Mercy, Due-Word as Justice**

*Li-yundhira* (to-warn) = mercy (alarms-before-Cry, v. 49; counsel-before-Cry, v. 45; parables-before-Cry, vv. 13–30 — warnings-multiplied!); *wa-yaḥiqqa* (Word-due) = justice (sentence-after-warnings — v. 54's *lā-tuẓlam*!). Mercy-justice-paired (v. 5's *ʿAzīz-Raḥīm*, Mighty-Merciful — revelation-sourced!): warning-Merciful (Raḥīm-warning!), due-Mighty (ʿAzīz-sentencing!). Pair's *order* (warn-*then*-due): mercy-precedes (respite, v. 44; restraint, v. 28's host-un-sent; withholdings, vv. 66–67's *law-nashāʾ*!); justice-succeeds (Cry, v. 49; arraignment, vv. 32, 53; Fire, v. 64). Order *pastoral* (hope-before-fear — warnings-heeded-gratefully, not resentfully!) and *logical* (duty-before-desert — commanded-*then*-judged!). *Yundhira*-rejected (warnings-mocked, v. 30; fled, v. 46; disputed, v. 49) → *yaḥiqqa*-deserved (due-earned, v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn*!). *Mercy*-now (warning-heard — *this*-verse!): heed (repent! believe! follow, v. 11! run, v. 20!). *Justice*-then (Word-due — *that*-Day!): faced (prepared-or-punished!). *Warned-living* — mercy's-children. *Word-dued* — justice's-subjects. *Children*-now (heed!). *Subjects*-then-avoided (repent-now!). *Mercy* — embraced. *Justice* — escaped-through-mercy (39:53!).

**Staying Alive, Escaping Due-Word: Practices of the Warned-Living**

The verse's *ḥayy* (alive) becomes a vitality-regimen. *Vital-signs-monitored*: heart-alive? (verse-trembling, 8:2 — *wajal*-checked; tear-ready, v. 11 — weeping-capacity; counsel-welcoming, vv. 20–25 — advice-appetite; sin-pained — guilt-felt!). *Flatlining?* (dullness-to-verses, dryness-in-prayer, irritation-at-counsel — *morta*-signs!) — resuscitate (*dhikr*-intensive, company-righteous, solitude-reflective — v. 9's unveiling-sought!). *Warning-heeded*: each-warning-acted (sermons-applied — one-point-per-sermon-implemented!; verses-obeyed — commands-enacted; counsel-followed — advice-executed!). *Hearing-without-heeding* = dying-while-breathing (v. 10's warned-in-vain!). *Due-Word-dreaded*: *yaḥiqqa*-visualized (Word-falling — v. 7's Hell-decreed; vv. 59–64's sorted-sentenced-burned — dread-as-deterrent!); *kāfir*-traits-purged (covering-truth-habits — excuses-quit, v. 65-anticipated; ingratitude-cured — *shukr*, v. 35; persistence-broken — repentance-swift!). *Beg life-and-refuge*: *duʿāʾ*-for-heart-life (*"O Turner-of-hearts, establish mine,"* al-Tirmidhī 3522 — ṣaḥīḥ; *"renew faith,"* v. 39's ḥadīth!); *istiʿādhah*-from-due-Word (*"refuge from Hell,"* v. 63 — thrice-daily!). For the verse's final mercy is its *man-kāna-ḥayyan* (whomsoever-is-alive): *alive*-now (reading-breathing! — warned-beneficially!). *Alive* — warnable. *Warned* — savable. *Saved* — heeding (v. 11!). *Due-Word* — escapable-pre-death (39:53!). *Live* (heart-vivified!). *Heed* (warning-acted!). *Escape* (Word-due-avoided!). *Alive* — forever (Garden-*ḥayy*, death-tasted-once, 44:56!). *Warned-living* — rejoicing-then (v. 55's *fākihūn*!).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:71

> **Have they not considered that among that which Our Hands have wrought We created cattle for them, and that they are their masters,**

**Expanded Commentary**

**A-Wa-Lam Yaraw: Cattle as Consideration's Object**

*"Have they not considered"* — *a-wa-lam yaraw* — *a-lam-yaraw* (have-not-considered — v. 31's *a-lam-yaraw*, generations; here cattle!): consideration *redirected* (history → husbandry!). *"The final section returns to signs in created order"* (source commentary — vv. 71–81: cattle-provision → bones-resurrection → omnipotence-seal, vv. 82–83!). Cattle (*anʿām*: camels, oxen, sheep, goats — 6:142–144's eight-kinds!) as *āyah* (sign — v. 46's signs-fled-here-displayed!): provision-proofs (milk, meat, wool, transport — v. 73!) + power-proofs (subdued-mighty — v. 72's *dhallalnā*, subdued!). *"God created Adam as vicegerent (2:30), appointed humans vicegerents (6:165; 35:39)"* (source commentary): cattle-entrusted-to-vicegerents (stewardship — *"made subservient,"* 22:65!). *"But vicegerency requires remaining servant (ʿabd Allāh), recognizing benefits from God's Hands, thus thankful"* (source commentary — v. 73's *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn*, thanks-demanded!). *Yaraw* (consider/see): cattle-*beheld*-daily (markets, farms, plates!) yet *unconsidered* (v. 46's *muʿriḍīn*, turning!). *Consider*: who-created? (v. 71's *khalaqnā*!) who-subdued? (v. 72!) who-benefits? (v. 73!) — *arrive*: thankful-worshipers (v. 22's Originator-deserved!). *Unconsidered*-cattle (eaten-unthanked!) → *considered*-Creator (worshiped!). *Yaraw* — now!

**Mimmā ʿAmilat Aydīnā: "Among That Which Our Hands Have Wrought"**

*"Among that which Our Hands have wrought"* — *mimmā ʿamilat aydīnā* — *aydīnā* (Our-Hands — *yad*: hand — divine-attribute affirmed *bilā-kayf*, without-how — 38:75's *"I created with My Hands"* (Adam!); 48:10's *"God's Hand above"*; 51:47's *"built with Hands"* (heaven!)): Hands-as-power-care-craft. *ʿAmilat* (wrought/worked — divine-*ʿamal*, workmanship!): creation-as-*craft* (cattle-*handmade*!). *"From that which God's Hands have wrought"* (source commentary): provenance (cattle-divine-products, not accidents! — v. 42's *khalaqnā*, We-created-vehicles — craft-acknowledged!). Hands-plural (*aydī* — majesty!): cattle-majestically-made (udder-engineered! wool-designed! strength-tempered-to-subduable, v. 72!). *Mimmā* (among-that-which): cattle *subset* (of Hands'-works — heavens, 51:47; Adam, 38:75; all-bounties!). Hands-theology (Sunnī-balanced: affirmed-real (*ithbāt*) + modality-unspecified (*tafwīḍ*) + likeness-denied (*tanzīh*, 42:11's *"naught-like-Him"*)!): Hands *believed* (verses-affirmed!), *unimaged* (anthropomorphism-refused!), *thanked-through* (gifts-traced!). *Aydīnā*-wrought (Hands-made!) → *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn* (thanks-due, v. 73!). Hands-giving (cattle!) — hands-receiving (thanks! obedience! v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*!). *Wrought* — wonderfully. *Given* — generously. *Thanked* — duly!

**Anʿāman Fa-Hum Lahā Mālikūn: Cattle Created, Masters Made**

*"We created cattle for them, and that they are their masters"* — *anʿāman... fa-hum lahā mālikūn* — *khalaqnā* (We-created — v. 42's vehicles, v. 36's pairs, v. 79's every-creation — Creator-refrain!). *Anʿām* (cattle — *"usefulness for riding, transporting, clothing, furnishings, food, drink, even beauty, as signs"* — 6:142; 16:5, 66, 79; 23:21–22; 40:79; 43:12–13 — source commentary!): multi-bounty-beasts! *Lahum* (for-them): cattle-purposed-humanward (vicegerency-provisioned!). *Fa-hum-lahā-mālikūn* (they-are-their-masters — *mālik*: owner, possessor, controller): mastery-*granted* (ownership-delegated — true-Owner God, 2:107's *"God's is sovereignty"* — *mulk*!; men *mālikūn*-by-loan!). Mastery *displays* (vicegerency, 2:30: Adam-*khalīfah*!): weak-masters (toddlers-leading-camels! — strength-subdued, v. 72!) ruling strong-beasts (bulls-ton-weights!) — *inversion*-miraculous (mice-mastering-elephants-like!). Mastery *tests* (stewardship-audited: cruelty-forbidden — *"woman entered-Fire for cat-starved,"* al-Bukhārī 3318; mercy-commanded — *"mercy to earth's,"* v. 27!; thanks-required, v. 73!). *Mālikūn* (masters) *answering* Mālik-al-Mulk (Owner-of-Sovereignty, 3:26!): delegated-masters *audited*-by-Absolute (cattle-rights — fed? rested? un-overloaded? — *"fear God concerning beasts,"* Abū Dāwūd 2548!). *Masters* — entrusted. *Entrusted* — examined (v. 54's *mā-taʿmalūn*!). *Examined* — rewarded/punished (cattle-testifiers? — *"hornless-sheep's-right from horned,"* v. 54's Muslim-2582 — beast-justice-*then*!). *Master* — mercifully. *Owned* — gratefully. *Audited* — preparedly!

**Vicegerency's Terms: Mastership Conditioned on Servanthood**

*"Vicegerency requires remaining servant"* (source commentary): *mālikūn* (masters-of-cattle) *conditioned* on *ʿabīd* (servants-of-God!). Terms: *recognize-Source* (cattle-from-Hands — v. 71's *aydīnā*! — not self-earned! — Qārūn's *"given for knowledge,"* 28:78 — vicegerency-violated-proudly-punished-sunken!); *thank-Giver* (v. 73's *yashkurūn*! — tongue-praise, heart-attribution, limbs-sharing — meat-shared, 22:36's *"feed content-and-asking"* — *uḍḥiyah*-distributed!); *serve-justly* (cattle-rights — *"they have rights: fodder, water, rest,"* traditions!; creation-care — earth-un-corrupted, 2:205's *fasād*-forbidden!); *worship-exclusively* (v. 61's *uʿbudūnī* — mastership-never-master-worshiped! — cattle-undeified — Apis-calf-worship, 2:51–54's calf-doomed! — masters-worship-Master!). Violations: *arrogance* (mastership-as-ownership-absolute — tyranny-over-beasts-and-men!); *ingratitude* (cattle-consumed-unthanked — v. 35's *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn*!); *cruelty* (beasts-tortured — Fire-risked, cat-ḥadīth!); *idolatry* (cattle-deified — Hathor-Apis-cows! — v. 74's taken-gods!). *Mālikūn* (masters) *remembering* Mamlūk-ness (ownedness-by-God — *"ye are poor, God Rich,"* 35:15!): owned-masters (slaves-ruling-by-permission!). *Vicegerent* — servant-first (*ʿabd*), master-second (*mālik*). *Order*-kept (servanthood→mastership-legitimate!); *order*-inverted (mastership-without-servanthood → tyranny!). *Serve* — then-master. *Master* — as-serving.

**Mastering Mercifully, Thanking Duly**

The verse's cattle-mastership becomes a stewardship-code. *Receive cattle-gifts thankfully*: eat-meat gratefully (*tasmiyah*-slaughtered — *"mention God's name,"* 22:36 — Hands-remembered-at-table!; *ḥamd*-after — v. 35's liturgy!); wear-wool humbly (garments-gifted — *"He made garments,"* 16:81 — Hands-clothing!); ride-transport mindfully (v. 42's *mithlihi* — boarding-*duʿāʾ*, 43:13–14!). *Waste*-none (meat-honoured — *"waste not,"* 7:31 — Hands'-craft-respected!). *Exercise mastership mercifully*: feed-water-rest (rights-rendered!); workload-fair (overloading-forbidden — *"fear God concerning,"* above!); slaughter-kindly (sharpened-blade, calmed-beast — *"excellence in all, even slaughter,"* Muslim 1955 — *iḥsān*-to-throat!). *Extend* vicegerency (pets-kept-kindly; wildlife-spared — *"no bird killed-wantonly but questions,"* al-Nasāʾī 4445 — ṣaḥīḥ!; environment-guarded — *fasād*-shunned!). *Remember Mastership's Master*: *mulk*-attributed (ownership-confessed — *"to God belongs,"* 2:107 — *mālikūn*-by-loan!); *mulk*-returned (death-relinquished — *"to Us return,"* v. 83 — mastership-surrendered!); *Mālik*-worshiped (v. 61's *uʿbudūnī* — Mastership's-purpose!). For the verse's final mercy is its *lahum* (for-*them*): cattle-*for*-men (gift-purposed! — love-bestowed!). *For*-them-created (cared!). *For*-them-subdued (v. 72 — eased!). *For*-them-benefited (v. 73 — enriched!). *Them* — vicegerents-honoured (17:70!). *Honoured* — serving (servanthood!). *Serving* — thanking (v. 73!). *Thanking* — increased (14:7!). *Masters* — gratefully. *Servants* — firstly. *Cattle* — signs-considered (*a-lam-yaraw* — *have-considered* — now!).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:72

> **And that We have subdued these for them, such that some are a mount for them and of some they eat?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Dhallalnāhā Lahum: Subdued for Them**

*"And that We have subdued these for them"* — *wa dhallalnāhā lahum* — *dhallalnā* (We-subdued/tamed — *tadhīl*: making-docile, softening — *"subdued"*!): wildness-removed (divinely!). *Hā* (these): cattle (v. 71's *anʿām*!). *Lahum* (for-them): subdual-purposed-humanward (vicegerency-enabled!). Subdual's *marvel*: strength-disparity (camel-500kg led-by-child! ox-ton plowing-at-word! horse-gallop reined-by-girl!) — power-voluntarily-yielded (instinct-overridden — *by-Whom?* — *dhallalnā*, We-subdued!). Unsubdued-contrasts (lions-untamed! crocodiles-unridden! sharks-unmilked!): subdual-*selective* (cattle-yielded, predators-withheld — wisdom-distributed!). *"Humanity is told: 'Hast thou not considered that God has made whatsoever on earth subservient unto you?'"* (22:65 — source commentary — *sakhkhara*, subservience!). Subdual *daily-miraculous* (familiarity-blinding!: riders-unmarvelling! milkers-unwondering! — v. 46's signs-fled!). *Dhallalnā* (We-subdued) *attributes* (tameness-divine, not veterinary!: trainers-*use* subdual, never-*create*! — wild-caught-tamed-*occasionally*, species-subdued-*divinely*!). *Lahum* (for-them) *obligates* (subdual-thanked, v. 73! subdual-unabused — cruelty-forbidden! subdual-worship-directed — Subduer-worshiped, v. 61!). *Subdued* — marvelously. *Marveled* — rarely (*a-fa-lā-yashkurūn* — thanks-withheld?!). *Thanks* — now!

**Fa-Minhā Rakūbuhum: "Some a Mount" — Transport Tamed**

*"Such that some are a mount for them"* — *fa-minhā rakūbuhum* — *fa-minhā* (so-from-them): subdual-*fruited* (tameness-utilized!). *Rakūb* (mount/riding-beast — *rukūb*: v. 42's *yarkabūn*, ride!): transport-cattle (camels-primarily — desert-ships, v. 42!; horses — 16:8's *khayl*!; oxen-carts; donkeys-mules!). Mounts *enabled*-civilization (trade-caravans — Quraysh's Riḥlah, 106:2's winter-summer-journeys — camel-mounted!; warfare-cavalry — *"steeds-of-war,"* 8:60!; pilgrimage — *"come on-foot and lean-camel,"* 22:27 — ḥajj-mounted!; migration — Hijrah-camelled!). Mounts *humbled*-riders (boarding-*duʿāʾ* — *"glory to Subduer — we-could-not-match,"* 43:13 — *muqrinīn*-confessed-weakness! — riders-acknowledging-subdual-miracle-per-mounting!). *Rakūbhum* (their-mount): possession-personalized (mounts-owned — wealth-measured-camels! — Abū-Bakr's-fed-poor, v. 47 — charity-mounted?!). The Qur'an's mount-gallery: *"cattle wherein warmth, benefits, eating; beauty when bringing-home-and-pasturing"* (16:5–6 — *jamāl*, beauty! — mounts-admired!); *"carry loads to lands unreachable-save-hardship"* (16:7 — *athqāl*, heavy-loads — burdens-beast-borne!). *Rakūb* (mounts) *preview* (Garden-conveyances? — *"reclining,"* v. 56 — transported-effortlessly?!). *Ride* — thankfully (Subduer-praised-per-ride!). *Mounts* — mercifully-kept (rights!, v. 71!). *Transport* — worship-directed (mosque-ward, v. 12; ḥajj-ward; kin-ward!). *Rakūb* — gift-mounted. *Mounted* — grateful.

**Wa Minhā Yaʾkulūn: "And of Some They Eat" — Food Tamed**

*"And of some they eat"* — *wa minhā yaʾkulūn* — *yaʾkulūn* (eat — v. 33's grain-eaten, v. 35's fruit-eaten — *eating*-refrain!): cattle-consumed (meat-milk — v. 73's *mashārib*, drinks!). *"Of some they eat"* (others-ridden — *minhā/minhā*, some/some — division-of-labour-divine!: camels-ridden-and-eaten! sheep-eaten-and-wooled! oxen-plowing-then-eaten! — multi-purposed!). Eating-cattle *sanctioned* (lawful-slaughtered — *"lawful are grazing-livestock,"* 5:1 — *bahīmat-al-anʿām*!; *"mention God's name,"* 22:36 — slaughter-consecrated!): eating-as-worship (provision-thanked!). Eating-cattle *bounded* (carrion-blood-pork-forbidden, 2:173 — boundaries!; excess-forbidden, 7:31 — *"eat-drink-waste-not"*!; cruelty-slaughtered-avoided — *iḥsān*-slaughter, v. 71!). *Yaʾkulūn* (eat) *connects* (vv. 33–35's plant-eating + here animal-eating = diet-complete — vegetarian-and-carnivorous provisioned!): provision-comprehensive (grain-gardens-cattle — menu-divine!). *Minhā-yaʾkulūn* (of-them-eat) *also* warns (eaters-audited — *"questioned concerning bliss,"* 102:8 — meat-answered-for!; ingrates-sentenced — v. 35's *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn*!). *Eat* — lawfully (slaughtered-named!). *Eat* — moderately (waste-shunned!). *Eat* — thankfully (*ḥamd*-uttered!). *Eaten* — worship-fueled (strength-for-obedience, v. 61!). *Minhā* — Subduer's-table. *Yaʾkulūn* — guests-grateful.

**Subdual as Resurrection-Proof and Worship-Proof**

*Dhallalnā* (subdued) *argues* doubly: *resurrection* (Subduer-of-mighty = Raiser-of-dead! — power-subduing-camels *easily*-raising-corpses, v. 79! — v. 68's Regresser-Raiser-logic echoed!); *worship* (Subdual-benefiting-men → Subduer-deserving-worship! — v. 22's Originator-logic: benefited-therefore-worship! — *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn*, v. 73!). The town/idolaters (vv. 23, 74): gods-subdued-naught (idols-immobile! — *"cannot help,"* v. 75!) — Subduer-alone-God! The deniers (v. 78's bones-questioners): bones-unsubduable-by-idols, subduable-by-Subduer (*kun*, v. 82 — *Be!*-subduing-dust-to-life!). *Dhallalnā-lahum* (subdued-for-them) *also* convicts-unthankful (subdual-enjoyed-worship-withheld — v. 35's ingrates! — riders-praying-never! eaters-thanking-never!). Al-Rāzī's subdual-marvel (tamer-Tamed-by-None!): men-taming-beasts-yet-untamed-by-God (beasts-obeying-men, men-disobeying-Master — inversion-shameful!: camels-kneeling-at-word, deniers-kneeling-never-at-Word, v. 8's chin-up!). *Beasts* — subdued-outwardly. *Believers* — subdued-inwardly (*islām* = submission! — hearts-*dhallal*-to-Lord!). *Deniers* — unsubdued-inwardly (hearts-wild! — v. 62's Satan-led!). *Subdue-selves* (hearts-tamed — desires-reined! — *nafs*-trained!): *"best-jihād: self-struggle,"* traditions! — self-subdual *preceding* beast-subdual-enjoyed-rightly! *Subdued* — outwardly-beasts. *Subdued* — inwardly-believers (*muslimūn*!). *Unsubdued* — inwardly-deniers (*mujrimūn*, v. 59!).

**Riding and Eating Gratefully**

The verse's *rakūb/yaʾkulūn* (ride/eat) becomes a gratitude-duet. *Ride-rituals*: mount-*duʿāʾ* (43:13–14 — *every*-ride — car-ignition, horse-mounting, camel-boarding — Subduer-named!); ride-merciful (beasts-rights!, v. 71; vehicles-maintained — trusts!; driving-gentle — road-*isrāf*-shunned, v. 42!); ride-purposed (mosque-kin-knowledge-livelihood — *rakūb*-worship-directed!). *Eat-rituals*: slaughter-conscious (meat-sourced — *ḥalāl*-verified! — *tasmiyah*-honoured!); meal-bracketed (*tasmiyah*-before, *ḥamd*-after — v. 35's liturgy!); meat-shared (*uḍḥiyah*-thirds — family-poor-gifts, 22:36!; hospitality — guests-fed!). *Subdual-pondered*: marvel-regularly (child-leading-camel — witnessed!; milk-flowing — contemplated, 16:66's *"between excretions-and-blood, pure milk"* — marvel-anatomical!; eggs/milk/honey — subdual-galleries!). Teach-children (*dhallalnā*-catechism: *who-tamed? — God! who-thank? — God!* v. 73!). For the verse's final mercy is its *lahum* (for-*them* — twice-implied!): subdued-*for*, ridden-*by*, eaten-*by* — *them* (beloved-vicegerents!). *Them* — served-by-beasts. *Beasts* — serving-by-Subduer. *Subduer* — served-by-them? (*uʿbudūnī*, v. 61 — worship-answered!). *Serve* (Subduer!). *Ride* (thankfully!). *Eat* (gratefully!). *Subdued-for-them* — honoured. *Honoured-for-submission* — Garden-mounted? (*reclining*, v. 56 — conveyed-blissfully!). *Rakūb-yaʾkulūn* — now. *Fākihūn-muttakiʾūn* — then (vv. 55–56!).

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:73

> **And they have benefits and drinks therein. Will they not then give thanks?**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Lahum Fīhā Manāfiʿ: Benefits Therein**

*"And they have benefits therein"* — *wa lahum fīhā manāfiʿu* — *lahum* (for-them): benefits-owned (vicegerency-dividends!). *Fīhā* (therein): in-cattle (v. 71's *anʿām*!). *Manāfiʿ* (benefits — plural-sweeping!): *"usefulness for riding and transporting goods, for providing clothing and furnishings, food and drink, and even beauty"* (source commentary — 6:142; 16:5, 66, 79; 23:21–22; 40:79; 43:12–13!). Enumerate: *transport* (v. 72's *rakūb* — riding/carrying!); *clothing* (wool-leather-fur — *"garments,"* 16:81!); *furnishings* (tents-rugs — *"furniture,"* 16:80's *athāthan*!); *food* (v. 72's *yaʾkulūn* — meat!); *drink* (here *mashārib* — milk!); *beauty* (*jamāl*, 16:6 — *"beauty when bringing-home-and-pasturing"* — aesthetic-dividends!); *wealth* (herds-capital — zakāh-able! — livelihoods!); *worship* (*uḍḥiyah*-sacrificed, 22:36! — devotion-mediums!). *Manāfiʿ* (benefits) *plural-unbounded* (uses-multiplying — ancients: transport-clothing-food; moderns: dairy-industries, leather-goods, organic-fertilizers — benefits-extending!). *"Cf. 16:66; 23:21"* (source commentary — milk-wonders!): *"We give drink from bellies — between excretions-and-blood — pure milk, palatable"* (16:66 — marvel-located!). *Fīhā* (therein) *marvels* (benefits-*inside*-beasts — walking-warehouses! mobile-dairies! living-factories!): cattle-as-gift-packages (Subduer-wrapped, v. 72!). *Manāfiʿ* — counted? (*"count God's blessings — number-not,"* 14:34 — benefits-uncountable!). *Benefits* — enjoyed. *Enjoyed* — thanked? (next-clause!).

**Wa Mashārib: "And Drinks" — Milk and More**

*"And drinks"* — *wa mashāribu* — *mashārib* (drinks — plural! — or *"drinking places,"* source commentary — water-sources-accessed-via-cattle?!): milk-primarily (cattle-dairy! — *"milk,"* 16:66; 23:21 — *"We give drink of what is in bellies"* — *nusqīkum*!). Milk's *marvel* (16:66-anatomy: excretions/blood-neighboured, purity-issued — filtration-divine! — *"pure, palatable-to-drinkers"* — *sāʾighan*!): impurity-surrounded-yet-pure (like believer-among-corruption! — v. 20's runner-among-town!). Milk's *blessing* (Prophet's ﷺ favourite-drink — *"whoever is given milk, let him say: 'bless us in it, increase us'"* — Abū Dāwūd 3730; al-Tirmidhī 3455 — graded ḥasan — milk-*duʿāʾ*!; ascension-cup-chosen — milk-over-wine, al-Bukhārī 3394 — *fiṭrah*-chosen!). *"Drinking places"* (alternate — wells/springs, v. 34's *ʿuyūn* — cattle-leading-to-water?! — herds-watered-settlements-founded!): cattle-as-geography (pastures-sought, wells-dug — civilizations-moored-to-herds!). *Mashārib*-plural (drinks-*varied*: milk-fresh, yoghurt-cultured, ghee-clarified, whey-processed — dairy-arts!): one-source-multiplied (udder-versatility!). *Wa-mashārib* (and-drinks) *pairs* (v. 72's *yaʾkulūn*, eat-solids + here drink-liquids = diet-complete!): solids-and-fluids (cattle-catering-wholly!). *Drinks* — gifted. *Gifted* — thanked? (*a-fa-lā-yashkurūn* — coming!). *Milk* — pondered (16:66-marveled!). *Marveled* — worshiped-through (Subduer-praised, v. 72!).

**A-Fa-Lā Yashkurūn: "Will They Not Give Thanks?" — Refrain Repeated**

*"Will they not then give thanks?"* — *a-fa-lā yashkurūn* — *a-fa-lā* (will-then-not?): astonishment-interrogative (v. 35's *identical*-refrain! — grain-gardens-thanks-demanded-there, cattle-benefits-here!). *"For v. 73, cf. 16:66; 23:21"* (source commentary — milk-thanks-contexts!). Repetition *insists* (thanks-demanded-twice — plants *and* animals — diet-wholly-gifted-wholly-thanked!). *Yashkurūn* (give-thanks — *shukr*: v. 35's tripartite — heart-recognizing, tongue-praising, limbs-obeying/sharing!). Ingratitude *indicted* (blessings-heaped-thanks-withheld — v. 35's *most*-unthankful, 2:243!): cattle-enjoyed-Creator-ignored (riders-prayerless! eaters-*ḥamd*-less! herders-*zakāh*-less!). *Fa-* (then): thanks-*following*-benefits-logically (benefits-received → thanks-due — sequence-moral!). *Lā-yashkurūn* (thank-not) *diagnoses* disbelief's-root (v. 35: unthankfulness → unbowing → ungiving → unbelieving!): thanks-seed (watered → faith-tree!); thanks-withheld (seed-starved → *kufr*-weed, v. 64's *takfurūn*!). *A-fa-lā* (will-not?): *appeals* (reason: Benefactor-acknowledged?; conscience: gifts-answered?; prudence: Increase-sought? — *"if thankful, I-increase,"* 14:7!). *Yashkurūn* — now? (*shukr*-enacted: *ḥamd*-uttered, *zakāh*-rendered, meat-shared, beasts-mercied, Subduer-worshiped, v. 61!). *Thanks* — given. *Given* — increased (14:7!). *Increased* — eternally (Garden-*manāfiʿ*, vv. 55–57 — benefits-unending!).

**Benefits as Trusts: Vicegerency Audited**

*Manāfiʿ-mashārib* (benefits-drinks) *entrusted* (vicegerency-audit, v. 71!): benefits-*used* (righteously? — meat-shared? milk-given-needy? wealth-*zakāh*-purified? beasts-mercied?) versus benefits-*abused* (hoarded? wasted? cruelly-extracted? boasted — Qārūn's *"for-knowledge-given,"* 28:78?!). Audit-questions (*"questioned concerning bliss,"* 102:8 — *naʿīm*-interrogated!): cattle-bliss (meals-answered-for! rides-accounted! wealth-traced — *"wealth-source-and-spending,"* v. 53's five-questions, al-Tirmidhī-2417!). *Manāfiʿ*-stewardship (benefits-multiplied-for-good: herds-*zakāh*-paid — 2.5%-purified-growth!; dairy-shared — neighbours-gifted!; leather-wool-traded-fairly — measures-just, 83:1–3's *wayl*-to-cheaters-avoided!; beauty-appreciated — *jamāl*-pondered-Creator-praised, 16:6!). *Mashārib*-stewardship (drinks-distributed: milk-to-orphans — *"best-charity-water,"* v. 34 — milk-second?!; thirsts-quenched — *"whoever gives-drink..."* rewards!). Ingratitude's *penalty* (blessings-recalled — 16:112's ungrateful-town-hunger-clothed!; 34:15–17's Saba-gardens-exchanged-bitter!): *kufrān*-punished (unthankfulness-disastered!). Gratitude's *prize* (blessings-increased — 14:7!; blessings-eternalized — Garden-*mā-yaddaʿūn*, v. 57!). *Benefits* — trusted. *Trusted* — audited (v. 54!). *Audited* — passed-through-thanks (*yashkurūn*!). *Thanks* — now.

**Thanking Through Benefits: Practices of the Grateful Beneficiary**

The verse's *manāfiʿ-mashārib-yashkurūn* (benefits-drinks-thanks) becomes a gratitude-engine. *Inventory benefits*: list-cattle-blessings (meals-weekly — meat-milk-cheese — *manāfiʿ*-tallied!; garments — wool-leather — Subduer-clothed!; transport — rides-taken!; wealth — livelihood-linked!; beauty — herds-beheld, 16:6!). *Count* — thankfulness-triggered (14:34's uncountable-attempted!). *Discharge benefit-dues*: *zakāh*-on-livestock (herd-owners — niṣāb-calculated — *manāfiʿ*-purified!); *uḍḥiyah*-sacrificed (feast-shared — thirds-distributed, 22:36!); charity-milk/meals (hungry-fed — v. 47's *anfiqū*!); beast-kindness (rights!, v. 71 — *manāfiʿ*-sources-honoured!). *Vocalize thanks*: meal-*duʿās* (milk-*duʿāʾ*, above!; meat-*ḥamd*!; general: *"praise God who fed-gave-drink,"* v. 33!); herds-beheld-*tasbīḥ* (*subḥāna*, v. 36 — Subduer-glorified!). *Convert benefits-eternal*: *manāfiʿ*-now → *ajr*-then (benefits-spent-Godward — charity, hospitality, sacrifice — v. 12's *qaddamū*, sent-forth!; benefits-enjoyed-thankfully — *"eater-thankful as faster-patient,"* v. 33!). For the verse's final mercy is its *lahum* (for-*them*): benefits-*theirs* (gifted!), drinks-*theirs* (poured!), thanks-*theirs*-to-give (invited!). *Theirs* — received. *Thanks* — returned. *Returned* — increased (14:7!). *Increased* — eternally (vv. 55–57!). *Manāfiʿ* — enjoyed-thankfully. *Mashārib* — drunk-gratefully. *Yashkurūn* — *yes* (*naʿam! — we-thank!* — tongue-heart-limbs!). *A-fa-lā* — answered (*balā! — Yea, we-thank!*). *Thanks* — given. *Giver* — worshiped (v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*!).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:74

> **Yet they have taken gods other than God, that perhaps they might be helped.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa-Ittakhadhū Min Dūni Allāhi Ālihah: Gods Taken Instead**

*"Yet they have taken gods other than God"* — *wa-ittakhadhū min dūni Allāhi ālihatan* — *wa-* (yet/but): contrast (benefits-given, vv. 71–73 → gods-taken-here — *ingratitude*-embodied!: *a-fa-lā-yashkurūn*, v. 73-answered-*lā*!). *Ittakhadhū* (taken/adopted — *ittikhādh*: v. 23's *a-attakhidhu*, shall-I-take? — runner-refused, they-accepted!): gods-*adopted* (selected-crafted-appointed — artifice!, v. 23!). *Min-dūni-Allāh* (other-than/apart-from-God — *dūn*: below-beside-instead): God-bypassed (Subduer, v. 72; Benefactor, v. 73 — bypassed-for-bypassers!). *Ālihah* (gods — plural!): idols (town's, vv. 23–24; Quraysh's Lāt-ʿUzzā-Manāt, 53:19–20!; moderns': wealth-desires-powers-ideologies-selves!). *"Refers not only to polytheism and idolatry, but also to failing to acknowledge Creator-Sustainer, arrogating powers lent-by-God"* (source commentary): *shirk*-broad (gross: statues; subtle: selves-powers — *"perform works for other-than-God with hidden-desire"* — Prophet's ﷺ warning, Aḥmad-4:124, Shaddād — source commentary-cited! — *riyāʾ*, showing-off = hidden-*shirk*!). *Ittakhadhū* (took) *convicts* (makers-taking-made-as-makers — v. 23's carpenter-logic!): taken-gods (needing-taking!) vs True-God (taking-them! — originated, v. 22; returning, v. 83!). *Taken* — futilely (*lā-yastaṭīʿūn*, v. 75 — cannot-help!). *Instead* — fatally (*burn*, v. 64's *takfurūn*-earned!). *Yet* — warned-still (repent-open, 39:53!).

**Laʿallahum Yunṣarūn: "That Perhaps They Might Be Helped"**

*"That perhaps they might be helped"* — *laʿallahum yunṣarūn* — *laʿalla* (that-perhaps/maybe): *hoped*-help (speculative! — idolaters-*unsure*! — *"perhaps"* betrays-doubt!: true-helpers *certain*, false-*perhapsed*!). *Yunṣarūn* (be-helped — *naṣr*: victory-aid-rescue): help-sought (battles? famines? judgments? — crises-anticipated!). Help-expected-from-taken-gods (idols-defending-votaries! — *"they shall be hosts-testifying-against,"* v. 75-alternate?!): expectation-*inverted* (helpers-become-prosecutors! — 25:17–18's worshiped-disowning!; 28:62–64's partners-failing-called-unanswering!). *Laʿalla* (perhaps) *contrasts* believers' *certainty* (God-helps — *"God will help who-helps-Him,"* 22:40 — *la-yanṣuranna*, surely-helps!; *"victory from-God-near,"* 2:214 — *qarīb*!): perhaps-helpers (idols) vs Certain-Helper (*al-Nāṣir*, 8:40's *"excellent-Protector-Helper"*!). Quraysh's *naṣr*-seeking (idols-before-Badr! — Abū-Jahl-praying *"God, help-truer!"* — answered-*against*!): taken-gods *present*-at-Badr (idols-carried!) — *helped*-none (chiefs-felled! — *naṣr*-from-angels-to-believers, 8:9!). *"Perhaps-helped"* (idolaters-hoping!) → *"cannot-help"* (v. 75's verdict!) → *"host-ready-for-them"* (v. 75's twist — idolaters-serving-idols-instead!). *Hoped* — disappointed (*khāb*, 20:111's *"disappointed who-bears-wrong"*!). *Certain*-Helper — available (*Nāṣir*!). *Seek* — rightly (God-alone, v. 61!). *Helped* — surely (22:40!).

**Help-Seeking Misdirected: Theology of Istiʿānah**

*Yunṣarūn* (be-helped) invokes *istiʿānah* (help-seeking — worship-branch!: *"Thee-alone we-worship, Thee-alone we-ask-help,"* 1:5 — *iyyāka-nastaʿīn*!): help-sought-as-worship (ultimate-crises — drowning, v. 43; judgment, vv. 53–54; Fire, v. 64 — help-*only*-divine-sufficient!). Misdirected-*istiʿānah* (idols/saints/powerful-dead/gods-taken): *shirk*-in-help (worship-diverted!). *"When thou askest, ask-of-God; when seekest-help, seek-from-God"* (al-Tirmidhī-2516, Ibn-ʿAbbās — ṣaḥīḥ — v. 23's transfer-form!): help-monopolized-divinely. Lawful-means (men-helping-men — *"help one-another in righteousness,"* 5:2! — doctors-teachers-friends!) *versus* ultimate-reliance (hearts-trusting-God — means-*used*, God-*relied*: *"tie-camel-and-trust,"* al-Tirmidhī-2517 — *asbāb*-with-*tawakkul*!). Idolaters *confused* (means-as-saviours — idols-as-*naṣr*-sources!): taken-gods *cannot* (v. 75's *lā-yastaṭīʿūn* — power-voided!). Modern *ittikhādh* (taken-gods-for-help: wealth-as-security! — markets-crashing-unhelping!; status-as-shield! — scandals-unshielded!; powers-as-protectors! — patrons-perishing!; selves-as-saviours! — *"self-help"*-insufficient-at-grave!): *laʿalla-yunṣarūn* (perhaps-helped — *perhapsed*-still!). *Test* helpers (v. 23's stress-test: Compassionate-desiring-harm — *who*-rescues? — *none*-but-Him!). *Helpers*-tested — failed (all-but-God!). *Helper*-True — sought (*Nāṣir*!). *Helped* — believers (22:40! 47:7's *"if-you-help-God-He-helps-you"*!).

**Ingratitude-to-Idolatry Pipeline: Benefits-to-Gods-Taken**

*Wa-* (yet) *links* (vv. 71–73's benefits → v. 74's gods-taken): ingratitude-*becoming*-idolatry (pipeline!). Stages: benefits-received (cattle, vv. 71–73!) → thanks-withheld (*lā-yashkurūn*, v. 73-feared!) → Source-forgotten (Hands-unacknowledged, v. 71!) → powers-arrogated (*"my-wealth-my-might,"* Qārūn's-28:78!) → gods-taken (helpers-*other*-than-Giver, here!) → help-sought-elsewhere (*laʿalla-yunṣarūn*!) → abandoned-at-need (v. 75's *cannot*!) → burned-for-disbelief (v. 64!). Pipeline *reversed*-by-thanks (v. 73's *yashkurūn*!): benefits-thanked → Source-remembered → powers-attributed → God-alone-sought (*iyyāka-nastaʿīn*, 1:5!) → helped-certainly (22:40!) → Garden-welcomed (v. 58's *Salām*!). *"Failing to acknowledge Creator-Sustainer, arrogating powers lent"* (source commentary): arrogation-as-idolatry-root (Qārūn-archetype — wealth-self-attributed — sunken, 28:81!; Pharaoh — *"I am lord-most-high,"* 79:24 — drowned!; town — threats-as-divine-*ʿadhāb*, v. 18 — Cried, v. 29!). *Ittakhadhū* (took-gods) *is* arrogation-institutionalized (lent-powers-deified — wealth-worshiped! status-adored! selves-obeyed-absolutely!). *Subtle*-*shirk* (hidden-desire-works, ḥadīth!): deeds-for-applause (audience-deified!), charity-for-praise (reputation-worshiped!), worship-for-show (spectators-sought — *laʿalla-yunṣarūn*-by-crowds?!). *Pipeline* — recognized. *Reversed* (thanks!). *Taken-gods* — abandoned (smashed-inwardly!). *True-God* — sought (*Nāṣir*!).

**Abandoning Taken-Gods, Seeking True Help**

The verse's *ittakhadhū* (taken) becomes a purge-order. *Audit taken-gods*: list-trusted (wealth? status? patrons? appetites? anxieties? selves? ideologies? influencers? — *dūni-Allāh*-relied!); stress-test (v. 23: Compassionate-harming — *help?* — honest-answer: *none*!); dethrone (trust-withdrawn — reliance-transferred: *"ask-God, seek-God,"* above!). *Purge subtle-shirk*: intention-purified (deeds-for-God-alone — *"actions-by-intentions,"* al-Bukhārī-1!; *riyāʾ*-checked — applause-indifference-cultivated!; hidden-desires-exposed — v. 76's *"knows-hide-and-disclose"* — secrecy-purified!). *Seek True-Help*: *duʿāʾ*-for-*naṣr* (victory-prayers — *"O God, help-Islām-and-Muslims"*; personal: *"help-me-against-self-and-Satan"*); *means*-with-*tawakkul* (effort-plus-trust — tie-camel!); *help-God-to-be-helped* (47:7 — دين-served — truth-supported, v. 20's runner-aided!; poor-fed, v. 47!; prayer-established!). For the verse's final mercy is its *laʿalla* (perhaps): idolaters-*unsure* (doubt-admitted! — certainty-unclaimed!). *Unsure* — invit-able (doubters-dialogued — runner-reasoned, vv. 22–24!; warners-declared, vv. 14–17!). *Perhapsed*-helpers (idols!) → *Certain*-Helper (God!) — transfer-urged. *Taken* — abandoned (now!). *True* — taken (*wali*! — *"God is believers'-Protector,"* 3:68!). *Helped* — surely (*la-yanṣuranna*, 22:40!). *Laʿalla* — replaced (*inna*, truly-helped!). *Ālihah* — smashed (inwardly-outwardly!). *Allāh* — alone-sought (*uʿbudūnī*, v. 61! *nastaʿīn*, 1:5!).
