## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:75

> **They cannot help them, though they be a host made ready for them.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Lā Yastaṭīʿūna Naṣrahum: Helpless Helpers**

*"They cannot help them"* — *lā yastaṭīʿūna naṣrahum* — *lā-yastaṭīʿūn* (cannot — power-negated — v. 50's *lā-yastaṭīʿūn*, bequest-unable; v. 67's *mā-istaṭāʿū*, advance-unable — inability-refrain!): taken-gods (v. 74's *ālihah*!) powerless. *Naṣrahum* (help-them — *naṣr*: v. 74's *yunṣarūn*, hoped-help!): hoped-help *voided* (verdict: *cannot*!). *"They cannot help them"* (Ibn-Kathīr/Qurṭubī/Ṭabarī-consensus!): idols-impotent (stone-deaf! — v. 23's intercession-naught, rescue-none!; 25:3's *"possess-neither-harm-nor-benefit, neither-death-nor-life-nor-raising"* — powerlessness-total!). The Qur'an's helpless-gallery: *"those you-call-apart-own-not-pellicle-date-stone"* (35:13 — ownership-*zero*!); *"if you-call, they-hear-not; if-heard, answer-not; Day-they-deny-association"* (35:14 — deafness-denial-damnation-triple!); *"they create-naught, themselves-created"* (25:3 — creature-helpers?!). *Lā-yastaṭīʿūn* (cannot) *answers* v. 74's *laʿalla* (perhaps — doubt-resolved: *certainly-cannot*!). Cannot-*when*? (crises: battles — Badr-idols-felled-with-carriers!; storms — v. 43's *lā-ṣarīkha*, none-answering!; graves — 28:62–64's partners-called-unanswering!; judgment — 25:17–18's disowned!). *Cannot* — ever (*istaṭāʿa*-negated-absolutely!). *Hoped* (v. 74) — disappointed (20:111's *khāb*!). *Taken* — useless. *True*-Helper — available (*Nāṣir*, v. 74!). *Transfer* — now!

**Wa Hum Lahum Jundun Muḥḍarūn: Hosts Ready — For Idols!**

*"Though they be a host made ready for them"* — *wa hum lahum jundun muḥḍarūn* — *wa-hum* (though-they — idolaters!): subject-inverted! *Lahum* (for-them — idols!): service-directed-*to*-gods! *Jundun-muḥḍarūn* (host-made-ready — *jund*: army/troop!; *muḥḍar*: prepared/presented — v. 32's *muḥḍarūn*, arraigned-echoed!): idolaters-as-*armies* (mobilized-for-idols!). *"Reference to idolaters ready to fight on behalf of gods, though gods cannot fight for them"* (al-Ṭabarī — source commentary): protection-*reversed*! (worshipers-guarding-gods! — temple-guards!; idol-defenders! — *"defend-gods!"* mobs!; Quraysh-fighting-*for*-Lāt-ʿUzzā-at-Uḥud! — *"hubal-be-exalted!"* — gods-needing-human-defense!). Reversal-absurd (helpers-helped-by-helpless-seekers!: patients-nursing-physicians!; citizens-guarding-bodyguards!; clients-funding-patrons-for-protection-unrendered!). *"Some take as gods becoming host testifying-against at Reckoning"* (Ibn-Kathīr/Qurṭubī/Ṭabarī — source commentary — *"problematic, implying real-beings"*): idols-animated-testifying? (25:17–18's questioned-worshiped-answering! — *if*-idols-personified-prosecuting!). *"Verse implies hidden-desires as host of difficulties creating-obstacles till sincerity"* (source commentary — inward-reading!): taken-gods (desires!) as *jund* (troops-besieging! — appetites-garrisoned-against-soul! — *jihād-al-nafs*, self-struggle!). *Jundun-muḥḍarūn* (host-ready): readiness-*wasted* (armies-mobilized-for-impotence!) — or readiness-*witnessing* (hosts-assembled-for-prosecution, v. 53's *muḥḍarūn*?!). *Ready* — wrongly. *Hosts* — for-helpless. *Service* — inverted. *Correct* (hosts-for-God! — *"God's-party,"* 58:22!).

**Inversion's Irony: Worshipers Serving the Served**

The verse's *jundun-muḥḍarūn* (host-ready) *displays* idolatry's master-inversion: worshiped-needing-worshipers (gods-guarded-by-votaries!). Inversions-catalogued: *carried-carriers* (idols-borne-shoulder-high — processions! — Bearers-worshiping-borne! — *"they carry them,"* implied-absurd!); *fed-feeders* (offerings-placed — gods-"eating"-through-priests'-bellies! — feeders-worshiping-fed!); *housed-residents* (temples-built — gods-sheltered-by-mortals! — builders-worshiping-residents!); *defended-defenders* (wars-for-gods — *"fight for Hubal!"* — defended-worshiping-defenders!). True-God *uninverted* (carries-not-carried, v. 41's *ḥamalnā*!; feeds-not-fed, 6:14's *"He feeds, not-fed"*!; shelters-not-sheltered, 112:2's *Ṣamad*, Eternal-Refuge!; defends-not-defended, 22:38's *"God defends believers"*!). *Jundun* (hosts) *rightly* God's (*"hosts-of-heavens-and-earth,"* 48:4, 7 — angels-stars-winds-Cry, v. 29!): hosts-commanded-by-Omnipotent (Badr-angels, 8:9!; wind-at-Trench, 33:9!; birds-at-Elephant, 105:3–4!). Idolaters' *jundun* (hosts-for-idols): hosts-squandered (courage-for-coward-gods! wealth-for-wood! lives-for-lies — runner-stoners, v. 26-tradition — *jund*-for-falsehood!). *Enlist* rightly (God's-*jund* — truth-defended! poor-fed, v. 47! prayer-established!). *Discharge* wrongly (idols'-*jund* — resigned! — *laʿalla*-hopes-surrendered!). *Hosts* — God's. *Ready* — for-Truth (*muḥḍarūn*-righteously!).

**Helplessness as Proof, Hosts as Pathos**

*Lā-yastaṭīʿūn* (cannot) *proves* (tawḥīd-by-impotence: helpers-unhelping = gods-ungodly!): divinity-*requires*-efficacy (helping-power — *"is He-who-creates like who-creates-not?"* 16:17 — efficacy-compared!). *Jundun-muḥḍarūn* (host-ready) *pathoses* (devotion-wasted — tears-for-stone! blood-for-wood! lives-for-lies!): pathos-*evoking* (compassion-for-misguided — runner-weeping, v. 20's *yā-qawmi*!; Prophet-grieving, v. 76's *lā-yaḥzunka* — grief-felt!). Pathos *mobilizes* (invitation-urged — wasted-hosts-recruited-rightly!: stoners-becoming-runners? — ʿUmar-stoner-to-Fārūq!; Hind-mocker-to-Mother-of-believers?!; Khālid-persecutor-to-Sword-of-God!). *Cannot*-helpers (idols!) *contrasted* Can-Helper (God!): *"excellent-Protector, excellent-Helper"* (8:40 — *niʿma-al-mawlā-wa-niʿma-al-naṣīr*!); *"God suffices as Protector-and-Helper"* (4:45 — *kafā*!); *"if-God-helps, none-overcomes"* (3:160 — *naṣr*-guaranteed!). Helpers-compared (idols-*lā-yastaṭīʿūn* vs God-*ʿalā-kulli-shayʾin-qadīr*, Omnipotent!): comparison-terminating-delusion! *Helpless* — abandoned. *Pathos* — pitied-invited. *Helper* — embraced (*Nāṣir*!). *Hosts* — re-enlisted (God's-*jund*! — *"party-of-God-victorious,"* 5:56!). *Ready* — righteously (*muḥḍarūn*-for-truth!).

**Deserting Helpless Hosts, Enlisting in God's**

The verse's *jundun* (hosts) becomes an enlistment-office. *Desert idols'-hosts*: resign-commissions (wealth's-army — hoarding-campaigns-quit!; status's-troops — showing-off-battles-ended!; desire's-militias — lust-raids-ceased!; ideology's-brigades — falsehood-defenses-abandoned!). *Desertion*-courage (peer-pressure-defied — *"strangers"*-glad-tidings, v. 62's Muslim-145!; threats-endured — runner-stoned-yet-standing, vv. 20–25!). *Enlist God's-hosts*: volunteer-campaigns (truth-defended — v. 20's running!; poor-fed — v. 47's *anfiqū*!; prayer-established — mosque-built-attended!; knowledge-spread — v. 12's *ʿilm-yuntafaʿu*!; kin-joined — 47:22-warned!). *Enlistment*-terms (sincerity — hidden-desires-purged, v. 74's ḥadīth!; obedience — Commander-obeyed, v. 61!; patience — campaigns-long, Noah-950-years!). *Trust Helper*: *tawakkul*-in-*Nāṣir* (battles-fought-means-plus-trust — Badr-outnumbered-victorious, 8:9-angels!; crises-faced — drowning-prayed-sincerely, v. 43!; judgments-hoped — *shafāʿah*-sought-permittedly, 20:109!). For the verse's final mercy is its *wa-hum* (though-*they*): idolaters-*acting* (hosting!) — action-*redirectable*! (hosts-reassigned! — energies-repurposed! — zeal-sanctified!). *Acting*-wrongly (idols-served!) → *acting*-rightly (God-served!) — conversion-redirects-hosts! (ʿUmar's-zeal: persecuting → Fārūq-distinguishing!; Khālid's-generalship: Uḥud-against → Yarmūk-for!; Hind's-fierceness: mocking → Mothering!). *Hosts* — ready (energies-available!). *Ready* — rightly (God-enlisted!). *Cannot*-helpers — deserted. *Can*-Helper — served (*jund-Allāh*! — victorious, 5:56!). *Enlist!*

---
## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:76

> **So let not their speech grieve thee. Truly We know what they hide and what they disclose.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Fa-Lā Yaḥzunka Qawluhum: "Let Not Their Speech Grieve Thee"**

*"So let not their speech grieve thee"* — *fa-lā yaḥzunka qawluhum* — *fa-* (so/therefore): consolation-*following* (idols-exposed, vv. 74–75 — Prophet-comforted!). *Lā-yaḥzunka* (let-not-grieve-thee — *ḥuzn*: grief, sorrow — heart-heaviness!): grief-*forbidden* (not unfelt — *felt-yet-managed*!). *Qawluhum* (their-speech — *qawl*: sayings!): deniers'-utterances (poet! sorcerer! madman! liar! — 15:6; 37:36; 68:51; v. 15's *takdhibūn*!; threats, v. 18!; scoffs, vv. 47–48!; plots-secret, here *mā-yusirrūn*, what-they-hide!). *"Counsels Prophet not grieved by accusations or plotting, since God protects and they punished"* (source commentary — protection-promised, 15:95's *"We suffice thee against mockers"*!; punishment-scheduled, v. 49's Cry!). *"Indicates Prophet must not be concerned with continuing disbelief, once warned-fulfilled"* (source commentary — 3:176; 5:41, 68; 6:33; 10:65; 15:88; 16:127; 18:6; 26:3; 27:70; 31:23; 35:8 — consolation-constellation!): *"Thou art not tasked with guidance, but God guides"* (2:272; 28:56 — duty-delimited, v. 17's *balāgh*!). *Yaḥzunka* (grieve-thee) *acknowledges* (grief-real! — Prophet-wept, 6:33's *"We know it grieves thee"*; 18:6's *"destroying-thyself in grief"* — compassion-measured-by-grief!). *Lā-* (not!) *bounds* (grief-limited — felt-not-fatal! — *"let-not-soul-expended,"* 35:8!). *Qawluhum* (their-speech) *minimized* (mere-*speech* — wind! — v. 29's Cry-*real*; v. 58's *salām*-real — deniers'-*qawl* unreal!): speech-vs-Speech (theirs-passing, His-abiding, v. 82's *kun*!). *Grieved?* — comforted-here. *Comforted* — proceeding (warned-still, v. 6!). *Speech* — wind. *Speaker*-with-thee (next-clause!).

**Innā Naʿlamu Mā Yusirrūna: "We Know What They Hide"**

*"Truly We know what they hide"* — *innā naʿlamu mā yusirrūna* — *innā-naʿlamu* (truly-We-know — *ʿilm*: omniscience!): knowledge-asserted (consolation-grounded!). *Mā-yusirrūna* (what-they-hide/conceal-secretly — *isrār*: secrecy!): plots (assassination-night, v. 9's Hijrah-surrounders!; boycott-schemes!; slander-whispers!; heart-malice — envy-hatred-unvoiced!). *"We know"* (v. 12's registry-Knower! v. 76's plot-Knower!): secrets-surveilled (no-plot-hidden — *"three-conspiring... He-fourth,"* 58:7 — Conspirator-Present!). Knowledge *protects* (Prophet-guarded — *"God protects thee from men,"* 5:67 — *yaʿṣimuka*!; v. 9's veiling-enemies!; cave-guarded, 9:40's *"God-with-us"* — *maʿiyyah*!): plotters-outplotted (*"they plot, God plots — best,"* 3:54! — Badr-ambushed-plotters!; trench-winds-scattered-confederates, 33:9!). Knowledge *records* (v. 12's *naḥṣī*, counted! — secrets-registered!; v. 65's limbs-testifying-secrets!): hidden-prosecuted (*"secrets tried,"* 86:9!). *Yusirrūna* (hide) *futility-exposed* (hiding-from-Omnipresent?! — *"wherever you are, He-with-you,"* 57:4!): secrecy-illusion (whisperers-overheard! — *"He knows secret-and-more-hidden,"* 20:7!). *Hidden* — known. *Known* — countered (protection!) + recorded (prosecution!). *Prophet* — comforted (Plots-known = plots-foiled!). *Plotters* — warned (Secrets-known = secrets-sentenced, v. 54!). *Hide* — futilely. *Known* — fully (*Naʿlamu*!).

**Wa Mā Yuʿlinūn: "And What They Disclose" — Openness Surveilled**

*"And what they disclose"* — *wa mā yuʿlinūn* — *yuʿlinūn* (disclose-proclaim-openly — *iʿlān*: publicity!): open-hostilities (mockery-public, v. 30!; threats-uttered, v. 18!; boycott-posted-Kaʿbah!; armies-marched-Badr-Uḥud-Trench!; verses-parodied-attempted!). *"Hide-and-disclose"* pair (*sirr-wa-ʿalan*: secret-and-open — 13:10's *"hidden-by-night-and-appearing-by-day"* — *sārib*!; 16:19's *"knows-hide-and-proclaim"*; 2:77's *"knows-hide-and-proclaim"* — omniscience-formula!): totality (no-third-category! — all-speech either-hidden-or-open — *both*-known!). Openness *recorded-equally* (tongues-testifying, 24:24!; books-matching, 18:49!): publicity-unshielding (crowds-unprotecting — *"no kinship-then,"* 23:101!; popularity-unavailing — *jibillan*, v. 62-heaps-sentenced-together!). Openness *answered* (revelation-rebutting — poet-charges-refuted, v. 69!; timing-mocks-answered, vv. 48–49!; bones-questions-demolished, vv. 78–79!): *qawluhum*-open (mockery!) vs *Qawl*-Open (Qur'an! — *al-Qawl*, 23:68's *"the-Word"* — Speech-Outspeaking-speeches!). *Yuʿlinūn* (disclose) *also* comforts (openness-witnessed — sufferings-seen! — *"God knows"* = God-cares! — tears-counted, patience-recorded, v. 11's unseen-fear-*seen*!). *Hidden-and-open* — known. *Known* — judged-justly (v. 54's *lā-tuẓlam*!). *Prophet* — doubly-comforted (secret-plots-known-foiled! open-mocks-known-answered!). *Grieved-by-speech?* — *Known*-by-God (speech-and-suffering!). *Known* — sufficed (*Hasbunā*, 3:173!).

**Consolation's Logic: Grief Bounded by Knowledge and Duty-Delimitation**

The verse's *fa-lā-yaḥzunka... innā-naʿlamu* (grieve-not... We-know) *logics* consolation: grief-unnecessary-because-Known (suffering-seen — 93:3's *"Lord-has-not-forsaken"* — *mā-waddaʿaka*!; plots-known-foiled, above!; verdict-known-scheduled — vv. 49–54's Cry-court!). Grief-excessive-because-duty-delimited (v. 17's *balāgh*-only! — 88:21–22's *"reminder-not-warder"*! — results-God's, v. 7–11!): *"let-not-soul-expended,"* 35:8 — expenditure-capped! Grief *legitimate*-within-bounds (compassion-felt — Prophet-weeping-for-people, 6:33-acknowledged!; runner-loving, v. 20's *yā-qawmi*!): bounds-exceeded (despair? self-destruction, 18:6's *"destroying-thyself"*?! — forbidden!). The Qur'an's consolation-refrain (source-listed: 3:176's *"grieve-not over hastening-disbelievers"*; 5:41's *"grieve-not"*; 10:65's *"let-not-their-speech grieve — might-belongs-God"* — *twin*-verse!; 15:88's *"grieve-not, lower-wing-believers"*; 16:127's *"grieve-not, distress-not-plotting — God-with-mindful"*; 31:23's *"disbelief-grieve-not — return-to-Us"*!): grief-theme *major* (prophetic-psychology-tended!). *Qawluhum* (their-speech) *specifically* (words-wounding!): verbal-abuse-acknowledged (mockery-hurts! — Prophet-human! — *"strait-chest,"* 15:97's *"We-know breast-straitened"* — *yaḍīqu*!); verbal-abuse-remedied (*tasbīḥ*-prescribed, 15:98 — *"glorify-praise-prostrate"* — worship-as-wound-care!; 50:39's patience-plus-praise-pre-sunrise-sunset!). *Grieved* — legitimately. *Bounded* — mercifully (*lā-*!). *Remedied* — worshipfully (*tasbīḥ*!). *Known* — comfortingly (*Naʿlamu*!).

**Grieve-Not Practices for Speech-Wounded Believers**

The verse's *lā-yaḥzunka* (grieve-not!) becomes a resilience-manual. *Bound grief*: feel-acknowledged (wounds-named — mockery-insults-slanders *hurt* — Prophets-hurt, 6:33! — stoicism-uncommanded!); limit-set (grief-timed — mourn-then-move!; grief-proportioned — speech-vs-Speech-weighed — *qawluhum*-wind, *Qawl*-mountain!; despair-refused — 12:87's *"despair-not"* — Satanic-whisper-rejected!). *Trust Known*: secrets-entrusted (*yusirrūna*-plotters — God-knowing-foiling! — *"plot-best,"* 3:54-trusted!); openness-committed (*yuʿlinūn*-mockers — God-knowing-answering! — revelation-vindicating, v. 69!; history-vindicating — Badr! conquest!); sufferings-consigned (*"God-knows"* = God-compensates! — tears-bottled, 56:? — patience-paid-limitlessly, 39:10's *"without-reckoning"*!). *Remedy worshipfully*: *tasbīḥ*-at-wounding (15:98's prescription — glorify-when-mocked! — praise-when-insulted! — prostrate-when-slandered!); *ṣalāh*-as-refuge (*"coolness-of-eye,"* v. 56 — prayer-retreated!); *ṣabr*-beautiful (12:83's *ṣabrun-jamīl* — complaint-to-God-only, 12:86's *"complaint-to-God"*!). For the verse's final mercy is its *fa-* (so/therefore): consolation-*following*-idols'-exposure (vv. 74–75 — helpless-helpers-unmasked — *so*-grieve-not!: enemies-impotent! helpers-True, *Nāṣir*!). *So* — comforted (logically!). *Speech* — wind (idols-cannot! v. 75!). *Known* — fully (*Naʿlamu*!). *Grieve* — boundedly (*lā-*!). *Proceed* — warned-still (v. 6!). *Protected* — assuredly (5:67!). *Vindicated* — certainly (vv. 49–54!). *Qawluhum* — passing. *Qawluhu* — abiding (*kun!*, v. 82!). *Grieve-not!*

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:77

> **Has not man seen that We created him from a drop, and behold, he is a manifest adversary.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**A-Wa-Lam Yara al-Insānu: "Has Not Man Seen?"**

*"Has not man seen"* — *a-wa-lam yara al-insānu* — *a-lam-yara* (has-not-seen — v. 31's generations-unconsidered, v. 71's cattle-unconsidered — *seeing*-refrain!): sight-rebuked (evidences-visible — origins-observable!). *Al-insān* (man/human — species!): Everyman (disputer-archetype — v. 78's bones-questioner! — Ubayy-ibn-Khalaf-tradition, vv. 78–79!). *"Cf. 16:4"* (source commentary — *"created-man-from-drop — behold-manifest-adversary!"* — *twin*-verse!): disputation-after-lowliness (theme-paired!). *Yara* (see): seeing-as-knowing (origins-known! — *"from-drop"* — undeniable! — *"base-fluid,"* 32:8; 77:20's *"despised-fluid"* — *māʾ-mahīn*!). Man *sees* (knows-origin!) yet *disputes* (denies-destination! — resurrection!): sight-unreasoned (v. 62's *lā-yaʿqilūn*! — seeing-without-understanding!). The question *shames* (lowliness-known-yet-arrogance-acted!: drop-descended disputing-Ocean-Creator!): *"khaṣīm"*-absurdity (adversary-from-drop — gnats-challenging-giants!). *Al-insān* (man) *generalized* (Everyman-warned — thou?!): disputation-tendency (arguing-Creator! — *"most-contentious,"* 18:54's *akthara-shayʾin-jadalan*!). *Seen?* — origins (drop!). *Behold?* — adversary (manifest!). *Contrast* — convicting (*a-lam-yara... fa-idhā-khaṣīm*!). *See* — humbly (drop-remembered!). *Dispute* — never (Creator-acknowledged!). *Origins* — lowly. *Destinies* — Return (v. 83!). *Between* — worship (v. 61!), not wrangling!

**Annā Khalaqnāhu Min Nuṭfah: Created From a Drop**

*"That We created him from a drop"* — *annā khalaqnāhu min nuṭfatin* — *khalaqnāhu* (We-created-him — v. 71's cattle, v. 36's pairs, v. 79's every-creation — Creator-refrain-crowning!). *Min-nuṭfah* (from-drop — *nuṭfah*: sperm-drop — *"sperm,"* 53:46; 75:37; *"drop,"* 18:37; 22:5; 35:11; 40:67; 76:2; 80:19 — source-listed!): seminal-fluid (milliliters! — *"base-fluid-draught,"* 32:8; 77:20 — *mahīn*, despised-lowly!). *"Cited as testimony to God's ability to resurrect"* (source commentary — 32:7–11!): drop-to-man-proves dust-to-raised! (origination-*harder*-than-repetition — *"easier-for-Him,"* 30:27's *ahwan*!). Gestation-stages (22:5; 23:14 — *nuṭfah*→*ʿalaqah*-clot→*muḍghah*-lump→bones-clothed→*khalqan-ākhar*, other-creation!): drop-*programmed* (DNA-scripted! — *"We-know-every-creation,"* v. 79 — Knower-coded!). Drop's *lowliness* (emitted-unseen! — *"gushing-from-between-backbone-and-ribs,"* 86:6–7! — hidden-humble!) *contrasts* man's loftiness (erect-speaking-reasoning-ruling, 17:70's *karramnā*!): lowly-origin → lofty-endowed (gratitude-due, v. 35! — worship-due, v. 22's *faṭara*-logic!). *"In not recognizing nature-of-own-creation and its-witness, one is manifest-adversary to oneself"* (source commentary — self-adversity!: disputing-Resurrector = disputing-own-hope! — suicide-argumentative!). *Nuṭfah* — pondered (origins-meditated!). *Khalaqnā* — praised (Creator-thanked!). *Resurrection* — inferred (Originator-can-Repeat, v. 79!). *Drop* — destiny-teaching (*turāb*-to-*turāb*-to-raised!).

**Fa-Idhā Huwa Khaṣīmun Mubīn: "Behold, Manifest Adversary!"**

*"And behold, he is a manifest adversary"* — *fa-idhā huwa khaṣīmun mubīn* — *fa-idhā* (then-behold! — v. 29's ash-beheld, v. 37's darkness, v. 51's rising — *idhā*-disclosures!): *astonishment*-particle (contrast-shocking!: drop → disputer!). *Khaṣīm* (adversary/litigant — *khiṣām*: v. 49's *yakhiṣṣimūn*, disputing-seized!): contentious-opponent (arguing-God! — *"disputes concerning-God-without-knowledge,"* 22:8!). *Mubīn* (manifest — v. 24's *ḍalāl-mubīn*, v. 60's *ʿaduww-mubīn*!): adversity-*evident* (open-wrangling! — bones-brandished, v. 78-tradition!: Ubayy-crushing-bones *"can-God-revive?!"* — insolence-performed!). Adversity's *objects*: God (disputing-power! — *"who-revives?"* v. 78 — Omnipotence-questioned!); messengers (v. 15's *takdhibūn*, liars!; v. 30's mocked!); resurrection (v. 48's *matā*-mocked!; v. 78's bones-scoffed!); self (v. 77-source: adversary-to-*oneself* — hope-denied-self! mercy-refused-self! Garden-foregone-self!). *Khaṣīm-mubīn* (manifest-adversary) *inverts* v. 11's *khashyah*-believer (fearing-unseen!): fearing-vs-fighting (awe-vs-adversity!). *Mubīn* (manifest) *also* self-evidencing (adversity-displaying-its-own-absurdity!: drop-litigating-Ocean! — court-laughing!): *manifest*-to-all-reasonable (unreasonable-unseeing, vv. 8–9!). *Fa-idhā* (behold!) *summons-witnesses* (angels-beholding-absurdity! believers-marvelling-audacity! creation-testifying-against!). *Drop* — disputing. *Dust* — debating (*turāb*-talking-back-to-Fashioner, 38:75's Hands!). *Behold* — absurdity-manifest. *Repent* — humility-restored (*sujud*-for-*khiṣām*!).

**From Drop to Disputer: Pride's Biography**

The verse *biographs* pride: *nuṭfah* (drop — humble-origin!) → *insān* (man — honoured-endowed, 17:70!) → *khaṣīm* (adversary — honour-abused!). Pride's *mechanism* (blessings-forgotten-Source! — strength-attributed-self! — Qārūn's-28:78!; intellect-idolized! — *"I-am-better,"* Iblīs's-7:12 — pride-archetype!): endowed-then-estranged (gifts-severed-from-Giver!). Pride's *symptom* (disputation! — *"no Dispute-like-pride's"* — arguing-truth!): *khaṣīm*-litigating (God's-power! messengers'-truth! resurrection-certainty!). Pride's *precedents* (Iblīs — fire-clay-argued, 7:12 — *khaṣīm*-first!; Pharaoh — *"lord-most-high,"* 79:24 — drop-deified!; Qārūn — knowledge-boasted, 28:78 — wealth-worshiped!; Ubayy — bones-brandished, v. 78 — dust-defying!): *khaṣīm*-lineage (adversaries'-fraternity — v. 62's *jibillan*, heaps-joined!). Pride's *cure* (*nuṭfah*-remembered!: origins-meditated — *"created-from-what? — gushing-fluid,"* 86:5–6 — *fal-yanẓur*, let-consider!; ends-anticipated — corpse-decaying! — *"then dies-buried,"* 80:21!; accountability-faced — v. 53's *muḥḍarūn*!): drop-to-dust-meditation (humility-restored!). *Biography* — warned (*insān*-tending-*khaṣīm*!). *Rebiographied* (drop → *ʿabd*, servant! — *khaṣīm* → *khashīʿ*, humble! — 23:2's *khāshiʿūn*-praying!). *Drop* — servant-minded. *Servant* — Garden-bound (v. 58's *salām*!).

**Remembering Drops, Abandoning Disputes**

The verse's *nuṭfah-khaṣīm* (drop-adversary) becomes a humility-catechism. *Meditate origins*: *nuṭfah*-pondered (conception-contemplated — *"despised-fluid,"* 77:20 — pride-deflated!; gestation-marveled — 23:14's stages — Creator-praised!; birth-remembered — helplessness-recalled — *"knowing-naught,"* 16:78 — learning-gifted!). *Origins*-daily (mirrors: *nuṭfah*-descended!; strengths: *nuṭfah*-endowed!; disputes: *nuṭfah*-humbled!). *Abandon Creator-disputes*: *khiṣām*-quit (arguing-God's-commands! — *"hearing-obeying,"* 2:285 — submission-replacing-litigation!; questioning-decrees? — wisdom-trusted, 21:23's unquestioned!; doubting-promises? — *ṣadaqa*-confessed, v. 52-pre-Cry!). *Dispute*-rightly (truth-seeking-dialogue — *"wisest-disputation,"* 16:125's *jādil-billatī-aḥsan*!; learning-questions — scholars-asked!; sincere-doubts-studied — evidence-examined!). *Serve-as-drop-grateful*: worship-as-*nuṭfah*-honoured (prostrations — dust-to-Dust-Maker!; charity — wealth-as-*nuṭfah*-like-gift!; kindness — fellow-drops-cherished!). For the verse's final mercy is its *a-lam-yara* (has-not-*seen*?): *seeing*-invited (origins-visible! — biology-testifying!): *see* (drop!) — *infer* (Creator-Powerful! — v. 79's Repeater!). *Seen* — believed. *Believed* — worshiped (v. 61!). *Worshiped* — raised-honoured (vv. 55–58!). *Drop* — destiny's-teacher. *Adversary* — warned (*khaṣīm-mubīn*-renounced!). *Manifest* — humbled (*khashīʿ*!). *Seen* — saved (God-willing!).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:78

> **And he has set forth for Us a parable and forgotten his own creation, saying, "Who revives these bones, decayed as they are?"**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Wa Ḍaraba Lanā Mathalan: Striking Parables Against God**

*"And he has set forth for Us a parable"* — *wa ḍaraba lanā mathalan* — *ḍaraba* (struck/set-forth — *ḍarb*: v. 13's *iḍrib*, strike-parable!): parable-coining (man-minting!). *Lanā* (for/to-Us — God!): parable-*against*-God (audacity!: creature-illustrating-to-Creator! — student-lecturing-Teacher!). *Mathal* (parable/likeness!): bones-case (decayed- multivariable!): *"can-decayed-revived?"* (rhetorical-no-intended!). *"Express common objection to bodily-resurrection"* (13:5; 17:49, 98; 23:35, 82–83; 27:67; 37:16–17, 53; 50:3; 56:47–48; 79:11 — source-catalogue!): objection-*perennial* (every-prophet-heckled! — *"when we are dust...?!"* — dust-despairing!). Occasion (Ubayy-ibn-Khalaf — *"brought withered-bones, crushed-pieces, saying: 'Dost think God can revive after-decayed-rot?' — Prophet: 'Yes! And He will throw thee into Fire.' — Then verses revealed"* — Ibn-Kathīr/Jalālayn/Qurṭubī/Wāḥidī — source-cited!): bones-brandished (prop in-hand! — crumbled-dramatically! — dust-blown-insolently?!). *Ḍaraba-lanā* (struck-for-Us) *inverts* v. 13's *iḍrib-lahum* (strike-for-*them*!): God-strikes-parables-*for*-men (guidance!); man-strikes-parables-*at*-God (defiance!). *Parables*: God's-true (town, v. 13 — history-teaching!) vs man's-false (bones-unrevivable — ignorance-asserting!). *Struck* — refuted (v. 79's *qul*, Say! — counter-struck!). *Parable* — shattered (like-bones-crumbled — argument-dust!). *Audacity* — answered (*Nār*-promised, Prophet's-reply — Fire-for-scoffer!).

**Wa Nasiya Khalqah: "And Forgotten His Own Creation" — Amnesia Convicting**

*"And forgotten his own creation"* — *wa nasiya khalqah* — *nasiya* (forgot — *nisyān*: heedlessness-oblivion!): amnesia (origin-erased!). *Khalqah* (his-creation — *khalq*: createdness-origination! — v. 77's *nuṭfah*-made!): self-made-forgotten. *"Could also be rendered 'having forgotten his own creation'"* (source-grammar!): forgetting-*state* (arguing-while-amnesic!). Forgetting's *content* (drop-origin! — v. 77's *nuṭfah*!; gestation-stages! — 23:14!; birth-helplessness!; growth-to-disputer!): creation-*marvel*-unremembered. Forgetting's *function* (objection-enabling!: *remembered*-creation would-*refute*-objection — drop-to-man-proves dust-to-raised! — v. 79's logic!): amnesia-as-argument's-foundation (forget-to-deny!). The Qur'an's forgetting-indictments: *"they forgot God, so He-forgot-them"* (9:67 — reciprocal!; 45:34; 59:19!); *"thou-forgottest-Our-signs, so-today-forgotten"* (20:126 — *nasiya*-punished-by-*nisyān*!). *Nasiya-khalqah* (forgot-creation) *contrasts* v. 77's *a-lam-yara* (has-not-*seen* — seeing-commanded!): commanded-seeing vs chosen-forgetting (sight-refused!). *Khalqah* (his-creation) *also* his-*nature* (created-constitution — *fiṭrah*, 30:30!): forgetting-*self* (who-he-is! — God-made-for-worship, 51:56!): identity-amnesia (drop-denying-Ocean!). *Remembered*-creation (origins-pondered!) → *refuted*-objection (resurrection-inferred!). *Forgotten* — objected. *Objected* — refuted-anyway (v. 79's *qul*!). *Remember!* (*dhikr*, v. 69! — Reminder-cures-*nisyān*!).

**Man Yuḥyī al-ʿIẓāma Wa Hiya Ramīm: "Who Revives Bones, Decayed?"**

*"Who revives these bones, decayed as they are?"* — *man yuḥyī al-ʿiẓāma wa hiya ramīm* — *man* (who?): interrogative-denial (no-one-implied! — *"nobody-can!"*). *Yuḥyī* (revives/gives-life — *iḥyāʾ*: v. 12's *nuḥyī-al-mawtā*! v. 33's *aḥyaynahā*!): life-giving-questioned. *Al-ʿiẓām* (the-bones — *ʿaẓm*: skeleton!): structure-remains (flesh-rotted! — *"these"*-brandished, Ubayy's-prop!). *Wa-hiya-ramīm* (while-they-decayed/crumbled — *ramīm*: rotted-powdered-mouldered!): decomposition-emphasized (crumble-demonstrated! — Ubayy-*crushing*-as-speaking!). Question's *assumption* (decay-defeats-revival!): powder-unreconstitutable! (scattered-ungatherable! — *"when dust-and-bones...?!"* catalogue-above!). Assumption *refuted*-multiply: *earth*-precedent (v. 33's dead-earth-revived! — dust-reconstituted-annually-harvested!); *drop*-precedent (v. 77's *nuṭfah*-to-man! — fluid-to-person-proves powder-to-person!); *first-creation* (v. 79's *anshaʾahā-awwala-marrah*, originated-first-time! — inaugural-*harder*!); *knowledge* (v. 79's *ʿalīm-bi-kulli-khalq*, Knowing-every-creation! — blueprint-retained!); *fire-from-green* (v. 80's opposites-united! — wet-wood-flaming-proves dead-bones-living!); *heavens-earth* (v. 81's greater-creation! — cosmos-fashioning-proves corpse-fashioning!). *Man* (who?) *answered*: *He* (v. 79's *alladhī-anshaʾahā*! — Originator!). *Ramīm* (decayed) *irrelevant* (decay-degree-unlimiting-Omnipotence! — *"Be!"* v. 82 — powder-obeying!). *Question* — mocked-sincerity (Ubayy-scoffing!). *Answer* — silencing-certainty (*qul*! Say! — v. 79!).

**The Bones-Question Through History: Perennial Objection, Perennial Answer**

*Man-yuḥyī* (who-revives?) *echoes* (every-age!): ancients (Ubayy's-bones!; Quraysh's *"dust-and-bones...?!"* 23:82!; Ṣadducees'-resurrection-denied!); moderns ("consciousness-brain-bound!" — neurons-rotted-mind-ended?!; "thermodynamics-irreversible!" — entropy-unreversed?!; "persons-patterns-dissipated!" — information-scattered?!). *Forms*-change (bones → brains → bytes!), *fallacy*-same (decay-defeats-God?!): power-underestimated (Creator-confined-to-nature's-regularities?!); knowledge-underestimated (patterns-unremembered-by-Omniscient?! — v. 79's *ʿalīm*!); precedent-ignored (earth-drop-sleep-moon, vv. 33/77/37/39 — revivals-witnessed!). Perennial-*answer* (vv. 79–82!): *first-creation* (harder-done!), *knowledge* (complete!), *opposites*-power (fire-from-green, v. 80!), *greater*-creation (heavens-earth, v. 81!), *command* (*kun*, v. 82 — *Be!*). *Man-yuḥyī* (who?) — *Qul* (Say!: *He!*). Ubayy's-fate (Badr? — Uḥud-wounded-dying-captured-traditions — Fire-promised, Prophet's-reply!): scoffer-sentenced (bones-buried-him!). Modern-scoffers (bones-questions-published!): answered-identically (*qul*! — Say-He!). *Question* — perennial. *Answer* — eternal (vv. 79–82 — memorized-deployed!). *Brandished-bones* — crumbled-arguments. *Reviver* — coming (*nufikha*, v. 51 — trumpet-answering-questions-finally!).

**Answering Bones-Questions: Apologetics of Resurrection**

The verse's *man-yuḥyī* (who-revives?) becomes an apologetics-drill. *Learn refutation-sequence* (vv. 79–82-memorized!): first-creation (v. 79a — originated-first!), knowledge (v. 79b — Knows-every!), opposites (v. 80 — fire-green!), greater (v. 81 — heavens-earth!), command (v. 82 — *Be!*). *Deploy*-gently (sincere-doubters — evidence-presented! — 16:125's wisdom-beautiful-preaching!; scoffers-firmly — Prophet-to-Ubayy: *"Yes! And Fire-for-thee!"* — truth-unsoftened!). *Supplement* precedents (earth-spring, v. 33 — witnessed!; sleep-dawn, v. 37 — daily!; moon-crescent, v. 39 — monthly!; drop-person, v. 77 — biologically!; town-Cry-then-... — v. 29!): revivals-catalogued (doubters-surrounded!). *Expose amnesia* (*nasiya-khalqah* — forgotten-creation!: questioners-origins-quizzed — *"from-what-created?"* 86:5 — *nuṭfah*-confessed! — creation-remembered-objection-dissolved!). *Live resurrection-hope*: hope-embodied (griefs-comforted — *"dead-raised,"* v. 12's *nuḥyī*!; risks-taken — martyrdom-hoped, v. 26's *udkhul*!; generosity-freed — wealth-sent-forth, v. 12's *qaddamū*!). For the verse's final mercy is its *wa* (and): *wa-ḍaraba* (and-struck — objections-*recorded*!): scoffs-preserved (as-refuted-specimens! — Qur'an-immortalizing-Ubayy-as-warning!). *Recorded* — refuted (vv. 79–82!). *Refuted* — deployed-eternally (apologists-armed!). *Bones* — brandished-then. *Bones* — raised-then (*yansilūn*, v. 51 — rushing!). *Ramīm* — revived (*yuḥyī*! — Reviver-answering!). *Man?* — *He!* (*Qul!* — Say! — v. 79!).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:79

> **Say, "He will revive them Who brought them forth the first time, and He knows every creation,**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Qul Yuḥyīhā: "Say, He Will Revive Them" — Commanded Answer**

*"Say, 'He will revive them'"* — *qul yuḥyīhā alladhī* — *qul* (Say! — imperative!): Prophet-commanded-answering (Ubayy-answered! — v. 78's *man-yuḥyī*, who-revives? — *qul*-countered!). *"For passage similar, see 17:51"* (source commentary — *"Say: He-who-originated-first-time"* — *twin*-answer!). *Yuḥyīhā* (will-revive-them — *hā*: bones-feminine! — *iḥyāʾ*, life-giving!): future-certain (tense-assured! — *"will"*). *Alladhī* (Who — relative!): Reviver-identified (next-clause!). *Qul* (Say!) *commissions* (every-believer-apologist!: bones-questions-answered! — *"Say!"* echoing-through-ages — thou-*say*!): revelation-dialogical (objections-quoted, v. 78! — answers-dictated, here! — Qur'an-disputing-divinely!). *Yuḥyīhā* (revive-them) *matches* v. 78's *yuḥyī* (revive? — question-verb *affirmed*!): *"who-revives?"* — *"He-revives!"* (verb-returned!). *Hā* (them-bones!): *those*-crumbled (Ubayy's-powder! — *specifically*-revived! — no-bones-unrevived!). *Qul* — obeyed (Prophet-answered! — *"Yes!"* + Fire-warning, v. 78-tradition!). *Yuḥyīhā* — believed (certainty! — *"will"*). *Alladhī* — identified (Originator-Knower, next!). *Say!* — now (apologists-answering!). *Revived* — then (*nufikha*, v. 51!). *Bones* — living (*yansilūn*, rushing!).

**Alladhī Anshaʾahā Awwala Marrah: First-Creation Argument**

*"Who brought them forth the first time"* — *alladhī anshaʾahā awwala marrah* — *anshaʾa* (brought-forth/originated/inaugurated — *inshāʾ*: initiating-creation!): first-production (bones-*inaugurated*!). *Awwala-marrah* (first-time — inaugural-occasion!): precedence-cited. Argument: *inaugurating-harder-than-repeating*! (first-time-from-nothing vs second-from-remains! — *"easier-for-Him,"* 30:27's *ahwanu-ʿalayh* — repetition-*easier*!). *"As We began first-creation, so shall We bring-back — promise-binding"* (21:104 — *naʿīduhu*, bring-back!); *"He created you first-time, unto-Him-returned"* (41:21 — first→return!). Ubayy's-fallacy (second-impossible-despite-first-actual!): actuality-proving-possibility (first-bones-*exist*! — thine-own! — second-*possible*!). Analogy: author-writing-book-once (can-reprint! — manuscript-retained! — v. 79b's knowledge!); builder-building-once (can-rebuild! — blueprint-kept!); programmer-coding-once (can-rerun! — code-saved!). *Awwala-marrah* (first-time) *also* recalls *nuṭfah* (v. 77 — drop-to-bones-first-time! — *harder*-transformation!: fluid→skeleton vs dust→skeleton!): drop-precedent-cited-implicitly (*nasiya-khalqah*, v. 78 — forgotten-creation-*reminded*!). *Anshaʾa* (originated) *attributes* (bones-God-originated — not nature-accidented! — v. 71's *aydīnā*, Hands-wrought!). *First* — done. *Second* — promised (*waʿd*, v. 48!). *Promised* — kept (*ṣadaqa*, v. 52!). *Originated-first* — revived-second (*yuḥyīhā*!). *Argument* — unanswerable (*man-yuḥyī?* — *Anshaʾa-awwal!*).

**Wa Huwa Bi-Kulli Khalqin ʿAlīm: Knowing Every Creation**

*"And He knows every creation"* — *wa huwa bi-kulli khalqin ʿalīm* — *ʿalīm* (Knowing — superlative-omniscience!): knowledge-absolute. *Bi-kulli-khalq* (of-every-creation — *khalq*: created-thing/mode!): scope-total (every-bone! every-dust-mote! every-soul-body-pairing! — v. 36's *azwāj*, pairs-re-paired!). *"He knows"* answers scattering-objection (dust-dispersed-seas-winds-bellies! — *"how-gathered?"* — *Known!* — omniscience-as-reassembly-blueprint!: each-atom-tagged! each-soul-mapped!): *"not-atom-hidden,"* 34:3 — *"in-earth-or-heaven"* — hidden-none!; *"We know what-earth-diminishes-of-them — with-Us-protecting-Book"* (50:4 — *kitābun-ḥafīẓ*, guarding-Book — erosion-recorded!). *Kulli-khalq* (every-creation) *includes* (modes-of-creating!: *nuṭfah*-mode, v. 77!; earth-mode, v. 33!; fire-from-green-mode, v. 80!; heavens-mode, v. 81! — Creator-versatile!). *"ʿAlīm"* (Knowing) *paired* (v. 38's *ʿAzīz-ʿAlīm*, Mighty-Knowing — sun-decreed!; here bones-known!): Might-executes, Knowledge-guides (reassembly-directed!). Ubayy's-powder (crumbled-in-hand!): *Known* (particles-tracked! — hand-dust-wind-scattered — *all*-Known!). Modern-scattering (cremated-dissolved-consumed-dispersed!): *Known-equally* (modes-irrelevant-to-Omniscience!). *ʿAlīm* — comforted (believers'-dust-cherished-known!); *ʿAlīm* — warned (deniers'-evasion-impossible — *"wherever-you-are-He-with-you,"* 57:4!). *Knows-every* — reassembles-every (*yuḥyīhā*!). *Every* — none-lost (v. 12's *aḥṣaynā*, counted-all!).

**The Twin Pillars: Power-to-Repeat and Knowledge-to-Reassemble**

The verse's two-clauses (*anshaʾa-awwal*, originated-first + *ʿalīm-bi-kull*, Knows-all) *are* resurrection's twin-pillars: *power* (can-repeat — first-proves!) + *knowledge* (knows-how — omniscience-guides!). Pillars *jointly-sufficient* (power-without-knowledge = blundering!; knowledge-without-power = wishing! — *both*-possessed = raising-certain!). The Qur'an's pillar-pairing: *"Creator-Knowing"* (v. 81's *Khallāq-ʿAlīm* — Creator-*and*-Knower!); *"Mighty-Knowing"* (v. 38's sun-decree!); *"Powerful-over-all + Knowing-all"* (67:13–14's *"knows-created... Subtle-Aware"* — *a-lā-yaʿlamu-man-khalaq*, knows-not-Creator?!). *Anshaʾa* (originated) *also* promises *better*-repetition (*"other-creation,"* 23:14's *khalqan-ākhar* — gestation-crowned!; Garden-bodies-superior, v. 55's *fākihūn*-embodied-youthfully, 56:35–37!): second-*exceeding*-first (mortal → immortal! — *"no-death-after,"* 44:56!; frail → perfected!; ugly-to-some → beautified-all!). *ʿAlīm* (Knowing) *also* individualizes (every-*khalq*-known-distinctly — fingerprints-retained! — *"fingertips-reconstructed,"* 75:4 — *banān*, fingertips! — identity-preserved!). Twin-pillars *demolish* twin-doubts (*can-He?* — *anshaʾa-awwal*, originated-first-answers!; *how-scattered?* — *ʿalīm*, Knows-answers!). *Doubts* — demolished. *Pillars* — standing (*anshaʾa*-and-*ʿalīm*!). *Raised* — powerfully-knowingly (*yuḥyīhā*!). *Bones* — living-testimony (pillars-proved!).

**Proclaiming the Twin Pillars: Apologetics Deployed**

The verse's *qul* (Say!) *deploys* apologists. *Memorize-and-deploy*: vv. 79–82 *arsenal* (first-creation → knowledge → opposites → greater → command!): doubters-answered-sequentially (gentle-progression!); scoffers-silenced-cumulatively (evidences-heaped!). *Say* (personally!: *"He-will-revive — Who-originated-first — Knowing-every!"* — conviction-voiced!). *Teach* (children-catechized: *who-revives-bones? — First-Maker-Knowing-all!*; students-lectured: power-plus-knowledge-syllogism!; graveside-comforted: *beloved-known-reassembled!* — v. 12's *nuḥyī*!). *Trust* (dust-destined-bodies: *Known!* — particles-precious-tracked!; scattered-saints: *Known!* — martyrs'-fragments-gathered-honoured, v. 26's *mukramīn*!). *Hope* (better-bodies: *anshaʾa*-improved! — youth-beauty-strength-perfected, 56:35–37!; reunions: *Known*-paired! — families-joined, 52:21!). For the verse's final mercy is its *qul* (Say! — *commanded*!): answers-*provided* (apologists-unabandoned! — revelation-arguing-*with*!); answers-*sufficient* (pillars-bearing-weight! — doubts-crushed!); answers-*promised-true* (*ṣadaqa*, v. 52 — vindicated-then!). *Say!* — now (confidently!). *Revived* — then (*yuḥyīhā*!). *Originated-first* — remembered (*anshaʾa*!). *Known-every* — trusted (*ʿalīm*!). *Bones* — living (*ramīm*-reversed!). *Question* — answered (*man?* — *He!*). *Qul!*

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:80

> **Who made for you fire from the green tree, and, behold, you kindle from it."**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Alladhī Jaʿala Lakum: Who Made Fire for You**

*"Who made for you"* — *alladhī jaʿala lakumu* — *alladhī* (Who — relative-continuing v. 79's Reviver!): same-Agent (Originator-Knower-*and*-Fire-Maker!). *Jaʿala* (made/placed/appointed — *jaʿl*: v. 34's gardens-placed, v. 72's subdual — making-refrain!): fire-*instituted*. *Lakum* (for-you — benefit-dative!): fire-purposed-humanward (warmth-cooking-light-protection-industry-worship?!). Fire-from-green (next-phrase!) *displays* (opposites-united — wet-flaming!): *"Just as God makes fire emerge from wood wet-and-moist, or had-been, so too can He make life emerge from bones dead-decayed"* (al-Qurṭubī — source-cited!). Logic: opposites-reconciled-in-nature (*green*-burning!) → opposites-reconciled-in-resurrection (*dead*-living!). *"According to Ibn ʿAbbās, refers to Markh-and-ʿAfār trees growing in Hijaz; rubbing two green-branches together known to produce fire"* (Ibn-Kathīr — source-cited!): Bedouin-fire-drill (green-wood-friction-kindled! — witnesses-daily!). *Jaʿala-lakum* (made-for-you) *also* graces (fire-gifted! — *"enjoyment-for-travellers,"* 56:73's *matʿan-lil-muqwīn*!; *"reminder"* — 56:73's *tadhkirah* — Fire-reminding-Hellfire!). Fire-*made* (not evolved-worshiped! — Magi-fire-adored — Maker-not-made-adored, v. 61!). *Made* — oppositely (green-flaming!). *For-you* — graciously. *Fire* — signally (*tadhkirah*!). *Green* — burning (omnipotence-glowing!).

**Mina al-Shajari al-Akhḍari Nāran: Fire From Green Tree**

*"Fire from the green tree"* — *mina al-shajari al-akhḍari nāran* — *al-shajar* (the-tree — collectively!): wood-source. *Al-akhḍar* (the-green — verdant-moist-living!): greenness-emphasized (*green*-yet-burning! — moisture-yet-flaming!). *Nāran* (fire — *nār*: flame-energy!): product-paradoxical. Paradox *sharp*: green-opposes-fire (wet-quenches! living-unburnable!): opposites-coexisting-source (greenness-harbouring-flame-potential!). Markh-ʿAfār (Ibn-ʿAbbās's Hijazi-tinder-trees!): green-branches-rubbed → sparks → blaze (friction-liberating-latent!). *Science*-deepened (photosynthesis-storing-solar-fire-in-green! — hydrocarbons-awaiting-release! — green-as-bottled-sunlight!): revelation-anticipating (green-fire-potential *jaʿala*-instituted!). *"From-green-fire"* parallels *from-dead-life* (v. 79's bones! — decay-harbouring-revival-potential-by-Omnipotence!): latencies-divinely-planted (fire-in-green, life-in-dust, guidance-in-hearts, v. 12's revivable-dead!). *Al-shajar-al-akhḍar* (green-tree) *also* evokes (gardens, v. 34's *jannāt*! — palms-vines-fire-bearing?!): provision-trees *and* pyre-trees (nourishing-and-warming!). *Nāran* (fire) *also* warns (Hellfire, vv. 63–64 — *nār*-named! — world-fire *tadhkirah*, reminder, 56:73!). *Green* — burning. *Burning* — reminding (Hell!). *Reminding* — returning (repentance!). *Tree* — sign. *Fire* — proof (*ramīm*-revivable!).

**Fa-Idhā Antum Minhu Tūqidūn: "Behold, You Kindle From It"**

*"And, behold, you kindle from it"* — *fa-idhā antum minhu tūqidūn* — *fa-idhā* (then-behold! — v. 77's adversary-beheld, v. 29's ash — *idhā*-astonishments!): wonder-summoned. *Antum* (you — second-person!): deniers-addressed (bones-questioners! — *you*-kindlers!). *Minhu* (from-it — green-tree!): sourcing (fire-from-green-*by-you*!). *Tūqidūn* (kindle/ignite — *īqād*: fire-starting!): kindling-practiced (Bedouin-daily! — friction-drilling! flint-striking! hearth-tending!). *"Behold-you-kindle"* convicts-experientially (*you*-do-it!: objectors-kindling-green-while-denying-dead-revived — practice-refuting-theory!: hands-kindling-what-mouths-deny!). *Tūqidūn* (kindle) *daily* (cooking-warming-lighting!): miracle-routine (green-fire-tamed-domestically!): routine-blinding (v. 46's signs-fled — hearth-miracles-unpondered!). *Fa-idhā* (behold!) *commands-pondering* (kindling-contemplated!: green-flaming-in-hands → bones-living-by-Omnipotence!): hearth-as-apologetics-lab (kitchens-catechizing! campfires-converting!). *Antum* (you!) *personalizes* (Ubayy-kindled! — bones-brandisher fire-user! — self-refuted-by-hearth!). *Minhu-tūqidūn* (from-it-kindle) *also* graces (kindling-ability-gifted! — *"made-for-you,"* above! — fingers-skill, wood-provided, sparks-permitted!): ability-thanked (v. 35's *yashkurūn*!). *Behold* — kindling. *Kindling* — proving (*yuḥyīhā*, v. 79!). *Proving* — believed. *Fire-from-green* — daily. *Life-from-dust* — promised (*waʿd*!). *Both* — *jaʿala* (Made!).

**Opposites United: Omnipotence's Signature**

Fire-from-green *signatures* Omnipotence (opposites-mastered!): green/fire (here!), wet/flame (Markh-moist-burning!), dead/living (v. 79's bones!), night/day (v. 37's flaying!), sweet/salt-seas (25:53's *barzakh*-parted-yet-adjacent!), male/female (v. 36's *azwāj*!). *"Opposites-united"* displays (power-unbounded-by-contrariety!: natures-subdued — v. 72's *dhallalnā*! — fire-obeying-green-containment-then-release!). Theologians' *jamʿ-al-aḍdād* (gathering-opposites — divine-prerogative!): men-combining-opposites-artificially (alloys! circuits!) yet never-creating-opposites-union-naturally (green-fire-growing?!). *Jaʿala* (Made!) *instituted* (latent-fire-in-green *designed*!): design-argued (tinder-trees-placed-Hijaz-travelers'-need! — providence-located!). Ubayy's-objection (decay-vs-life-opposed!): opposites-precedented (green-vs-fire-opposed-yet-united!): *ramīm*-vs-*ḥayy* (decayed-vs-living!) *like* *akhḍar*-vs-*nār* (green-vs-fire!) — united-by-*Jaʿala*! *"Behold-you-kindle"* generalizes (opposites-experienced → opposites-believed!): kindlers-believing-revivers (practice-to-creed!). *Opposites* — mastered (*Jaʿala*!). *Mastered* — trusted (resurrection-hoped!). *Trusted* — worshiped (v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*!). *Green-fire* — glowing-proof. *Dead-life* — coming (*nufikha*, v. 51!).

**Kindling Contemplatively, Hoping Resurrectedly**

The verse's fire-green becomes a hearth-theology. *Ponder kindling*: fire-observed (hearths-campfires-stoves-lighters — green-origin-traced! — wood-paper-fuel-plant-derived!): *jaʿala*-marveled (*subḥāna*, v. 36 — Fire-Maker-glorified!); resurrection-inferred (green-flaming → dust-living! — *man-yuḥyī?* — *Jaʿala-fire-from-green-He!*). Teach-children (fireside-catechism: *who-put-fire-in-green? — God! who-raises-bones? — Same-One!* vv. 79–80!). *Use fire-thankfully*: warmth-grateful (winters-thanked!); cooking-praised (*tasmiyah*-before, *ḥamd*-after — fire-services-thanked!); light-valued (electricity-fire-derived — *nūr*-gifted!). *Fear Fire-remindedly* (*tadhkirah*, 56:73 — world-fire-Hell-reminding!: heat-felt → Hell-imagined → sins-abandoned!; *"Fire-seventy-times-hotter,"* v. 63 — dread-calibrated!). *Hope opposites-united*: dead-hopes (dreams-decayed! — *ramīm*-like!): Reviver-hoped (*yuḥyī*! — green-fire-precedented!); dead-hearts (v. 10-fearing!): Reviver-sought (v. 12's *nuḥyī-al-mawtā*!); dead-limbs (sins-paralyzed!): Reviver-trusted (repentance-enlivening, 39:53!). For the verse's final mercy is its *lakum* (for-*you*): fire-*gifted* (warmed! fed-lighted!), proof-*gifted* (resurrection-evidenced!), warning-*gifted* (*tadhkirah*!). *For-you* — kindled. *Kindled* — pondered. *Pondered* — believed (*yuḥyīhā*, v. 79!). *Believed* — raised (*nufikha*, v. 51!). *Green* — flaming-daily. *Bones* — living-then. *Jaʿala* — praised (*subḥāna*!). *Tūqidūn* — testifying (*man-yuḥyī? — Jaʿala-fire-He!*).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:81

> **Is not He Who created the heavens and the earth able to create the like thereof? Yea, indeed, He is the knowing Creator.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**A-Wa-Laysa Alladhī Khalaqa: "Is Not the Creator-Able?"**

*"Is not He Who created the heavens and the earth able to create the like thereof?"* — *a-wa-laysa alladhī khalaqa al-samāwāti wa-al-arḍa bi-qādirin ʿalā an yakhluqa mithlahum* — *a-wa-laysa* (is-not?): interrogative-affirming (rhetorical-yes-demanded! — *"isn't-He?!"*). *Alladhī-khalaqa* (Who-created): Creator-identified (heavens-earth-Fashioner!). *Al-samāwāt-wa-al-arḍ* (heavens-and-earth — cosmos!): creation-supreme (vastness-unimaginable! — *"greater-than-men's-creation,"* 40:57's *akbaru-min-khalqi-al-nās*!). *Bi-qādirin* (able/capable — *qudrah*: power!): ability-questioned-affirmed. *An-yakhluqa-mithlahum* (to-create-like-them — *mithl*: like/equivalent — humans-recreated!): re-creation-posited. *"Several passages maintain first-creation as evidence of Ability to re-create"* (41:21; 21:104; 50:1; 79:27 — source-cited!): *"As began-first, so bring-back"* (21:104!); *"He created-you-first-time, unto-Him-returned"* (41:21!). *A-fortiori* (greater-to-lesser!: heavens-earth-*vaster*-than-men — 40:57!; 79:27's *"are-you-harder-or-heaven-built?!"* — heaven-harder-confessed!): vaster-done → smaller-certain! (cosmos-fashioning *proves* corpse-fashioning!). Ubayy's-bones (v. 78!): heavens-weighing-against (cosmos-vs-crumbs!): crumbs-doubted-after-cosmos-actual?! — absurdity-exposed (*a-wa-laysa?!*). *Is-not?* — *Yes!* (*balā*! — next-clause!). *Created-heavens-earth* — witnessed (nightly-daily!, vv. 37–40!). *Able-like-them* — inferred (necessarily!). *Like-them* — recreated (*mithl*! — counterparts-restored — identity-preserved, 75:4's fingertips!). *Question* — convicting. *Answer* — coming (*Balā!*).

**Balā: "Yea, Indeed!" — Affirmation Thundered**

*"Yea, indeed"* — *balā* — *balā* (yea/oh-yes! — affirmative-to-negative-question!): thundered-confirmation (God-answering-Own-question!: heavens-Creator-*able*!). *Balā* (yes!) *echoes* (7:172's *balā-shahidnā*, yea-we-testified! — covenant-confirmed!): creation-confessed-then (*balā*-to-Lordship!) → re-creation-confessed-now (*balā*-to-Raising!). *Balā* (yea!) *demands*-echo (readers-answering!: *balā! — Yea-He-is-able!* — faith-voiced!): reciters-confessing (each-reading *balā*-renewed!). *"Yea-indeed"* also *refutes*-Ubayy (*man-yuḥyī?* — who-revives? — *Balā-He!*): interrogative-denial (*man*, who=none!) crushed-by-affirmative (*balā*, yea!=He!). *Balā* (yea!) *seals*-argument (vv. 77–81's disputation-closed!: drop-cited, v. 77!; bones-questioned, v. 78!; first-creation + knowledge, v. 79!; fire-green, v. 80!; heavens-earth, here! — *Balā*: case-closed!). *Balā* — believed. *Believed* — proclaimed (*qul*, v. 79's Say!-echoed!). *Proclaimed* — vindicated-then (v. 52's *ṣadaqa*!). *Yea* — He-can. *Indeed* — He-will (*waʿd*, v. 48!). *Can-and-will* — raised (*nufikha*, v. 51!). *Balā!*

**Wa Huwa al-Khallāqu al-ʿAlīm: The Knowing Creator**

*"He is the knowing Creator"* — *wa huwa al-khallāqu al-ʿalīm* — *al-Khallāq* (the-Creator — intensive-perpetual-Creating!: *khallāq*-form — creating-repeatedly-continually!): Creator-constantly (creations-ongoing! — *"every-day-He-is-upon-affair,"* 55:29!). *Al-ʿAlīm* (the-Knowing — v. 79's *ʿalīm-bi-kull*!): Knower-completely. Pair (*Khallāq-ʿAlīm*): power-creating + knowledge-guiding (v. 79's twin-pillars! — *anshaʾa*-power + *ʿalīm*-knowledge — *named-together*!). *"Knowing-Creator"* answers *both*-doubts (*can-He?* — *Khallāq*, Creator-perpetual-answers!; *how-scattered?* — *ʿAlīm*, Knower-answers!). *Khallāq* (Creator-intensive) *also* promises-variety (re-creations-diversified!: Garden-bodies-glorified! — 56:35's *inshāʾ*, special-formation!; Fire-bodies-torment-fitted, 4:56's skins-renewed!): creations-tailored (ends-matched!). *ʿAlīm* (Knowing) *also* individualizes (every-*khalq*-known!, v. 79!). Names-paired-elsewhere (*Khallāq-ʿAlīm*, 15:86 — *"thy-Lord-is-Knowing-Creator"* — Lordship-linked!). *Huwa* (He — pronoun-emphatic!): *He*-alone (exclusive! — idols-uncreating, 25:3! — *"create-naught"*!). *Al-Khallāq-al-ʿAlīm* — invoked (*duʿāʾ*: Creator-creating-needs! Knower-knowing-needs!). *Invoked* — answered (*Qarīb*, Near, 2:186!). *Creator* — worshiped (v. 61!). *Knower* — feared-unseen (v. 11!). *Knowing-Creator* — trusted-raising (*Balā*!).

**Greater-to-Lesser: The A-Fortiori That Fells Doubt**

The verse's *samāwāt-arḍ → mithlahum* (heavens-earth → like-men) *is* *a-fortiori* (greater-proving-lesser!): logic-formalized (premise: greater-*done*; conclusion: lesser-*doable*!): heavens-earth (magnitude-cosmic! complexity-astronomical! — galaxies! orbits, v. 40! — *done*!) → men-like (magnitude-minute! — bones-motes! — *doable*!). *"Are-you-harder-creation-or-heaven?"* (79:27 — *a-antum-ashaddu-khalqan?!* — heaven-harder-admitted!); *"Creation-of-heavens-earth greater-than-men's, but most-know-not"* (40:57 — *akbar*! — majority-ignorant!). Ubayy-inverted (lesser-doubted-despite-greater-admitted!: heavens-confessed-Creator's! — *"who-created-heavens-earth? — God!"* 31:25 — pagans-confessing! — bones-denied-Same-Creator's?!): inconsistency-exposed (cosmos-yielded, corpses-grudged!). *Mithlahum* (like-them) *nuances* (re-creation-*like*! — counterparts!): identity-preserved-yet-renewed (same-persons! — 75:4's fingertips!; renewed-bodies! — Garden-glorified!). *Bi-qādirin* (able!) *absolute* (power-unlimited! — *"powerful-over-all,"* 67:1's *ʿalā-kulli-shayʾin-qadīr*!). *A-fortiori* — deployed (doubters-argued!: cosmos-admitted? — corpses-conceded!). *Greater* — beheld-nightly (vv. 37–40's sky-gazing!). *Lesser* — believed-firmly (*Balā*!). *Doubt* — felled (*a-wa-laysa?!*). *Faith* — raised (*Khallāq-ʿAlīm*-trusted!).

**Beholding Greater, Believing Lesser: Cosmic Contemplation**

The verse's heavens-earth becomes an observatory-order. *Contemplate greater*: sky-gaze-regularly (stars-numbered-attempted! — *"adorned-nearest-heaven-with-lamps,"* 67:5!; vastness-pondered — light-years! — *subḥāna*, v. 36!; orbits-watched, v. 40 — punctuality-praised!). Earth-walked-reflectively (mountains-rooted! — *"pegs,"* 78:7!; seas-parted! — 25:53!; valleys-cleft! — *"brought-forth-pasture,"* 79:31!). *Greater*-beheld → Creator-magnified (*Allāhu-Akbar*! — God-Greater — *takbīr*-cosmic!). *Infer lesser*: bones-to-cosmos-compared (crumbs-to-cathedrals!): cosmos-Created-admitted → corpses-recreated-conceded (*Balā*!). Doubts-dissolved-comparatively (resurrection-"difficulty" *vs* heavens-actuality!: difficulty-dwarfed!). *Invoke Knowing-Creator*: *yā-Khallāq* (O-Creator! — needs-created!: provision-fashioned!; healing-formed!; guidance-shaped!); *yā-ʿAlīm* (O-Knower! — states-known!: secrets-confessed-comforted, v. 76!; needs-wordless-understood!). *Pair*-invoked (creating-*and*-knowing!: *"create-for-me-good-and-know-it-best"* — *istikhārah*-spirit!). For the verse's final mercy is its *balā* (yea!): affirmation-*gifted* (doubters-answered-divinely! — apologists-echoing!). *Balā* — believed (He-able!). *Able* — trusted (*Qādir*!). *Trusted* — worshiped (*Khallāq*!). *Known* — feared-loved (*ʿAlīm*! — unseen, v. 11!). *Greater* — beheld (*samāwāt-arḍ*!). *Lesser* — believed (*mithlahum*!). *Heavens-earth* — Created. *Like-them* — re-created (*Balā*!). *Knowing-Creator* — praised (*subḥāna-al-Khallāq-al-ʿAlīm*!).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:82

> **His Command when He desires a thing is only to say to it, "Be!" and it is.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Innamā Amruhu: His Command — Only**

*"His Command when He desires a thing is only"* — *innamā amruhu idhā arāda shayʾan an yaqūla lah* — *innamā* (only/merely — *ḥaṣr*: restriction!): Command-*confined* (to-saying! — v. 11's *innamā-tundhiru*, only-warn — restriction-echoed!). *Amruhu* (His-Command — *amr*: order/matter/affair!): divine-fiat (decree-executive!). *Idhā-arāda* (when-He-desires — *irādah*: will!): willing-antecedent (desire-preceding-command!). *Shayʾan* (a-thing — *anything*!): scope-unlimited (cosmos-or-crumb! heavens, v. 81! bones, v. 78! Garden, vv. 55–58! Fire, v. 64!). *"One of most-frequently-cited verses"* (2:117; 3:47; 6:73; 16:40; 19:35; 40:68 — source-catalogue!): *kun*-constellation (command-verses-clustered!). *"Points to fact God's creative-act dependent-upon-no-other and like blinking-eye"* (54:50 — source-cited! — *"Our-command-but-one-as-twinkling-sight"*!; 2:117c!): independence + instantaneity (aseity-and-agility!). *Innamā* (only!) *excludes* (instruments! assistants! delays! deliberations! materials!): Command-unmediated (no-means-needed!); Command-unresisted (*"no-repeller,"* 13:11!); Command-undelayed (utterance-execution-simultaneous!). *Amruhu* (His-Command) *contrasted* human-commands (uttered-disobeyed! — kings-defied!; uttered-delayed! — bureaucracies!; uttered-impotent! — wishes-unfunded!): divine-*amr* (uttered-obeyed-instantly-totally!). *Arāda* (desires) *paired* (*amr*-follows-*irādah*!): willing-then-wording (desire-decreed!). *Shayʾan* (thing!) *universally* (all-existents-commanded!: *"Be!"*-uttered-to-each!). *Only* — saying. *Saying* — sufficing. *Command* — creation (*kun*!).

**Kun Fa-Yakūn: "Be!" And It Is — The Creative Dyad**

*"To say to it, 'Be!' and it is"* — *an yaqūla lahū kun fa-yakūn* — *yaqūla-lah* (say-to-it): address-to-thing (nonexistent-addressed-as-present! — *"to-it"* — thing-*summoned*!). *Kun* (Be! — imperative-exist!): existence-commanded (one-word-creation!). *Fa-yakūn* (and-it-is — *fa*: immediate-consequence!): being-following-instantly (utterance-presence-simultaneous!). *"Be! and it is (kun fa-yakūn) has become aphorism in Arabic and Islamic languages used often in daily discourse"* (source-noted!): *kun*-proverbial (difficulties-dismissed — *"kun-fa-yakūn-for-God!"*; hopes-expressed — *"God-says-Be!"*). *Kun-fa-yakūn* *displays*: power-absolute (*Be!*-obeyed-by-nothingness!: nonexistence-listening! — void-complying!); ease-absolute (creation-effortless! — *"not-wearied,"* 50:38's *mā-massanā-lughūb*!; *"heavens-earth-in-six-days-unwearied"* — effort-negated!); speed-absolute (*fa*-immediate! — 16:77's blinking! 54:50's twinkling!). *"Dependent-upon-no-other"* (source!): aseity (self-sufficient-commanding! — *"God-Sufficient-Praised,"* 14:8!; *"Samad,"* 112:2 — Besought-needing-naught!). *Lah* (to-it) *marvels* (addressing-nonexistent!: things-*called*-before-being! — *"called-and-responded,"* implied-obedience-pre-existence?!): existence-obedient (being-*hears*!). *Kun* (Be!) *contrasted* men's-making (labouring-delayed-defective! — years-building! — *kun*-instant-perfect!). *Fa-yakūn* (it-is!) *guaranteed* (no-*kun*-failing!: uttered-accomplished! — *"His-promise-true,"* 4:122!). *Be!* — uttered. *Is!* — instantly. *Kun-fa-yakūn* — omnipotence-formula. *Formula* — believed (*Balā*, v. 81!). *Believed* — hoped (needs-*kun*-ed!).

**Kun as Resurrection's Seal and Creation's Key**

*Kun-fa-yakūn* (Be!-is!) *seals* resurrection-argument (vv. 77–82!): drop-cited (v. 77!) → bones-questioned (v. 78!) → first-knowledge-answered (v. 79!) → fire-green-precedented (v. 80!) → heavens-greater-argued (v. 81!) → *kun*-crowned (here!): bones-*kun*-ed! (*"Be!"*-to-dust — dust-living! — *fa-yakūn*!). Trumpet-*kun*-like (v. 51's *nufikha* — blown! — *kun*-instrumental!; v. 53's Cry — *kun*-voiced!): raisings-commanded (graves-addressed — *"come-forth!"* — *kun*-echoed!). *Kun* *also* keys-creation (heavens-earth-*kun*-ed! — 2:117's *"when-decrees-matter-says-Be!"*; Jesus-*kun*-ed — 3:47's *"says-Be-and-is"* — fatherless-created!; Adam-*kun*-ed — 3:59's *"dust-said-Be!"*): creations-*kun*-originated (all-beings-*kun*-descended!). *Kun* *also* governs-providence (needs-*kun*-met!: wombs-*kun*-opened!; clouds-*kun*-gathered!; hearts-*kun*-turned! — *"Turner-of-hearts,"* v. 70's *duʿāʾ*!): affairs-commanded (daily-*kun*s! — *"every-day-upon-affair,"* 55:29 — *kun*-perpetual!). *Kun* *also* warns-judgment (*kun*-sentencing!: *"Be-apes!"* 2:65 — *kūnū*-transforming!; *"burn!"* v. 64's *iṣlaw* — *kun*-executing!): sentences-commanded. *Kun-fa-yakūn* — universal-key (creation-providence-resurrection-judgment-unlocked!). *Key* — held-by-One (*amruhu*, His-Command!). *Held* — exclusively (*innamā*!). *Exclusive* — worshiped-alone (v. 61's *uʿbudūnī*!). *Be!* — believed. *Is!* — awaited (*waʿd*!). *Kun* — coming-to-bones (*yuḥyīhā*, v. 79!).

**Desiring-and-Commanding: Will-and-Word United**

*Idhā-arāda... an-yaqūla* (when-desires... to-say) *unites* will-and-word: *irādah* (desire/will!) → *qawl* (saying!) → *kawn* (being!): volition-verbalized-actualized (desire-decreed-done!). Unity *contrasts* creatures' disunity (desiring-unable! — wishes-wanting-power!; saying-undone! — commands-disobeyed!; doing-undesired! — acts-regretted!): divine-coherence (desires-accomplished! — *"effecter-of-what-He-wills,"* 85:16's *faʿʿālun-limā-yurīd*!; says-actualized! — *kun-fa-yakūn*!; acts-wisely! — *Ḥakīm*, v. 2!). *Arāda-shayʾan* (desires-a-thing!) *unlimited* (things-all-willed-possible! — *"wills"* unbound-except-wisdom! — evil-unwilled-legislatively though-permitted-cosmically, v. 47's two-wills!). *Amruhu* (His-Command!) *comprehensive* (*amr*-as-decree! *amr*-as-revelation! — *"Spirit-from-My-Lord's-command,"* 17:85!; *amr*-as-affair! — 65:12's affairs-descending!): commands-multiform (creating-commanding-revealing-ruling!). *Innamā* (only!) *also* comforts (needs-*only*-His-saying!: healing-*kun*-away!; provision-*kun*-near!; guidance-*kun*-uttered!; forgiveness-*kun*-granted — *"Be-forgiven!"* — 39:53's *all*-sins!). Desires-creation's-best (believers'-good-willed! — *"God-wills-ease,"* 2:185!): *kun*-kindness (commands-merciful! — v. 58's *salām*-commanded!; Garden-*kun*-ed — *"Be-Gardened!"*). *Desires* — wisely (*Ḥakīm*!). *Commands* — effectively (*Qadīr*!). *Says-Be* — lovingly (*Raḥīm*, v. 5!). *Is* — gratefully-received (*yashkurūn*, vv. 35, 73!).

**Living Under Kun: Trusting the Commander**

The verse's *kun-fa-yakūn* (Be!-is!) becomes a trust-charter. *Trust Commander*: needs-entrusted (*kun*-capable!: illnesses-*kun*-healed! — *"when-sick-He-cures,"* 26:80!; debts-*kun*-relieved!; fears-*kun*-calmed!; dead-hopes-*kun*-revived — *yuḥyī*!): *tawakkul*-in-*kun* (means-taken, Commander-trusted — tie-camel, v. 74!). *Despair*-banished (*kun*-near!: impossibilities-men's — *"difficult-for-men-easy-God"* — *kun*-dissolving!; Ubayy-impossibilities — bones-*kun*-ed, v. 79!). *Ask Commander*: *duʿāʾ*-as-*kun*-seeking (*"ask-given,"* 40:60! — requests-*kun*-candidates!: *kun*-asked-humbly-persistently!; *kun*-answered-wisely — given-delayed-substituted, v. 57's *duʿāʾ*-economics!). *Obey Commander*: commands-heeded (*amr*-as-legislation!: orders-obeyed — *uʿbudūnī*, v. 61!; prohibitions-shunned — *lā-taʿbudū-shayṭān*, v. 60!): *kun*-of-legislation (*"establish-prayer!"* — *kun*-prayed!; *"give-charity!"* — *kun*-given!). *Hope kun-raising*: bones-*kun*-awaited (*yuḥyīhā*! — *kun*-to-dust — dust-persons!); Garden-*kun*-longed (*udkhul*! — *kun*-welcomed — *salām*!). For the verse's final mercy is its *shayʾan* (a-*thing* — *anything*!): *kun*-scope-unlimited (needs-all-*kun*-able!: greatest-cosmic — heavens-*kun*-ed, v. 81!; smallest-personal — tears-*kun*-dried!): *anything*-asked (*duʿāʾ*-boundless!); *anything*-hoped (*rajaʾ*-unlimited!); *anything*-repented (*dhunūb*-*kun*-forgiven, 39:53!). *Shayʾan* — entrusted. *Kun* — trusted. *Fa-yakūn* — awaited (*waʿd*!). *Amruhu* — obeyed-loved-feared-hoped. *Be!* — believed. *Is!* — coming (*nufikha*! v. 51!). *Kun-fa-yakūn* — sūrah's-crown (creation-key, resurrection-seal!). *Crown* — worn-humbly (*subḥāna*! — v. 83-coming!).

## Sūrah Yā Sīn 36:83

> **So glory be to Him in Whose Hand lies the dominion of all things, and unto Whom you shall be returned.**

**Expanded Commentary**

**Fa-Subḥāna: "So Glory Be" — The Sūrah's Seal**

*"So glory be"* — *fa-subḥāna* — *fa-* (so/then/therefore!): conclusion-particle (all-argued → glorified!): creation-cited (vv. 33–44, 71–73!) + parables-struck (vv. 13–30!) + resurrection-proved (vv. 77–82!) + judgment-displayed (vv. 49–65!) → *therefore*-glorified! *Subḥāna* (glory-transcendent-flawless-far-removed! — v. 36's *subḥāna*!): glorification-sealing (sūrah-opened-mystery, v. 1's *Yā-Sīn*! — sūrah-closed-majesty, here!). *"Glory be indicates both God's being beyond all idolaters associate and amazement at what they say"* (source commentary — transcendence + wonder!): beyond-idols (vv. 23, 74's taken-gods-transcended!) + beyond-objections (Ubayy's-bones-marveled-at-audacity! — *subḥāna*-from-such!). *Fa-subḥāna* (so-glory!) *commands*-readers (glorify-*consequently*!: arguments-heard → awe-rendered!; proofs-pondered → praise-uttered!; sūrah-finished → *subḥāna*-sealed!). The Prophet's ﷺ *subḥāna*-habits (post-prayer-33×! — Muslim-597!; dawn/dusk-100× — *"sins-forgiven-though-foam,"* al-Bukhārī-6405!; creation-beheld — *subḥāna*!): glorification-lifestyle (sūrah-sealing-habitually!). *Fa-* (so!) *also* answers-denial (bones-questions-asked, v. 78! — *so*-glorified-anyway!: objections-unanswered-by-silence-but-praise!): truth-praised-despite-scoffs (v. 48's *matā*-mocked — *subḥāna*-anyway!). *So* — glorified. *Glory* — sealed-sūrah. *Sealed* — believed-lived-proclaimed (*Yā-Sīn*-completed!).

**Alladhī Bi-Yadihi Malakūtu Kulli Shayʾ: Dominion in His Hand**

*"To Him in Whose Hand lies the dominion of all things"* — *alladhī bi-yadihi malakūtu kulli shayʾ* — *bi-yadihi* (in-His-Hand — *yad*: Hand — v. 71's *aydīnā*, Our-Hands! — affirmed-*bilā-kayf*!): possession-grasped (dominion-*held*!). *Malakūt* (dominion/sovereignty-inner-kingdom! — *"closely related to mulk, sovereignty — 'Unto God belongs sovereignty over heavens-and-earth' (2:107; 3:189; 5:17...)"* — source-traced! — root *m-l-k*: possess-control-rule-reign!): kingship-absolute. *"According to some, malakūt-and-mulk same"* (Ibn-Kathīr!); *"others: malakūt-Lordship-unseen, mulk-visible"* (source-layered! — cosmologies: *mulk*-this-world, *malakūt*-above, *jabarūt*-archangelic-above!): dominion-stratified (visible-and-invisible-ruled!). *Kulli-shayʾ* (of-all-things — *every*-thing!: atoms-angels! — v. 82's *shayʾan*, thing-*kun*-ed! — *kulli*-commanded!): totality (exception-none!: idols-possessed-*by*-Hand! — v. 75's helpers-*held*!; deniers-grasped! — v. 53's *muḥḍarūn*-produced-from-Hand!; believers-kept! — v. 58's *salām*-greeted-from-Hand!). *"Answer to 23:88: 'Who in-Whose-Hand dominion-of-everything, protecting-unprotected-against?'"* (source-linked! — *man-bi-yadihi?!* — rhetorical-God!): question-answered-here (*alladhī-bi-yadihi* — He!). Hand-*holding*-dominion (grasp-firm!: *"heavens-folded-in-Right-Hand,"* 39:67 — *maṭwiyyātun-bi-yamīnih*!; *"earth-grasp-Day,"* 39:67 — *qabḍatuhu*!): cosmos-handled (planets-palm-held!). *Bi-yadihi* (in-Hand!) *also* pledges-allegiance (hands-given — 48:10's *"Hand-above-hands"* — *bayʿah*-sealed!): hands-pledged-to-Hand! *Malakūt* — His. *His* — worshiped-alone (v. 61!). *Alone* — glorified (*subḥāna*!).

**Wa Ilayhi Turjaʿūn: "And Unto Whom You Shall Be Returned" — The Final Return**

*"And unto Whom you shall be returned"* — *wa ilayhi turjaʿūn* — *ilayhi* (unto-Him — direction-goal!): destination-divine (v. 22's *ilayhi-turjaʿūn*, runner's-you-returned! — *echoed-closing*!; v. 51's *ilā-rabbihim*, Lordward-rushing!). *Turjaʿūn* (shall-be-returned — passive!): return-compelled (no-choice!: *"humans have no choice regarding return,"* source-passive-noted!). *"Could also be rendered 'are being returned,' emphasizing ongoing-process experienced-by-all"* (source-continuous!): returning-*now* (lives-flowing-Godward! — breaths-numbered-returning! — *"every-day-nearer,"* implied!). Return's *certainty* (*turjaʿūn*, shall!): promise-sealed (*waʿd*, v. 48 — mocked-then, certain-now!; *ṣadaqa*, v. 52 — confessed-then!). Return's *universality* (*turjaʿūn*, you-plural-all!): deniers-returned (judged!, vv. 59–64!) + believers-returned (welcomed!, vv. 55–58!) + cosmos-returned (*"to-God-are-matters-returned,"* 2:210!). *"From one perspective answer to 23:88"* (source!): Hand-holding-dominion *and* Return-receiving-subjects (possession-*and*-destination!). *Ilayhi* (unto-Him!) *also* comforts-homecoming (exiles-returning! — *"return-pleased-pleasing,"* 89:28! — *irjiʿī*!): Return-as-reunion (beloveds-met!, v. 58's *salām*!; vision-granted!, 75:23!). *Turjaʿūn* (returned!) *ends*-sūrah (final-word!): sūrah-opened-addressing-Prophet (vv. 1–5!) — sūrah-closed-addressing-all (*turjaʿūn*, you-all!): universalized (message-for-mankind!, 34:28's *kāffah*!). *Unto-Him* — returned. *Returned* — judged-welcomed-or-sentenced (vv. 55–64!). *Shall* — certainly (*ḥaqq*!). *You* — all (*jamīʿ*, v. 53!). *Return!*

**The Sūrah's Circle: From Yā-Sīn to Return**

*Turjaʿūn* (returned!) *completes* Yā-Sīn's circle: opened (*Yā-Sīn*, v. 1 — mystery-address!) → oath (Wise-Qur'an, v. 2!) → Prophet-confirmed (vv. 3–5!) → peoples-warned (vv. 6–12!) → town-parabled (vv. 13–30!) → generations-cited (vv. 31–32!) → earth-signed (vv. 33–36!) → cosmos-signed (vv. 37–40!) → arks-signed (vv. 41–44!) → counsel-scoffs-Cries (vv. 45–54!) → Garden-Fire (vv. 55–64!) → limbs-orbits-aging (vv. 65–68!) → revelation-defended (vv. 69–70!) → cattle-signed (vv. 71–73!) → idols-exposed (vv. 74–75!) → Prophet-consoled (v. 76!) → bones-raised (vv. 77–82!) → *glorified-returned* (here!). Circle-*thematic* (heart-of-Qur'an, Introduction!: *tawḥīd* — Oneness-argued!, *nubuwwah* — messengers-vindicated!, *maʿād* — Return-proved-sealed-*turjaʿūn*!). Circle-*pastoral* (warned → signed → Cried → sorted → welcomed/sentenced → consoled → convinced → glorified-returning!). *Fa-subḥāna... turjaʿūn* (so-glory... returned!) *pairs* worship-and-destiny (glorify-*now*, return-*then*!): duties-crowned (praise!); destinies-declared (Return!). *"Ongoing-process"* (*are-being-returned*, source-alternate!): sūrah-unfinished-in-life (returning-daily! — readers-mid-return!). *Yā-Sīn* (v. 1!) → *turjaʿūn* (here!): mystery-to-destiny (letters-to-Return!). *Read* — wholly. *Believed* — fully. *Lived* — straightly (v. 4's *ṣirāṭ*!). *Returned* — pleasingly-pleased (89:28!). *Circle* — completed. *Heart* — beating (*Qalb-al-Qurʾān*!).

**Glorifying and Returning: The Reader's Final Practices**

The verse's *subḥāna-turjaʿūn* (glory-return!) becomes a life-seal. *Glorify consequently* (*fa-subḥāna*!): arguments-to-awe (proofs-pondered-praise-uttered!); sūrah-completions-sealed (*subḥāna-rabbika*, 37:180 — *"glory-to-Lord-of-might-above-attributes!"* — reciters'-custom!); daily-rhythms-glorified (morning-evening-*tasbīḥ*! — post-prayer! — creation-beheld! — v. 36!). *Entrust dominion* (*bi-yadihi-malakūt*!): affairs-consigned (Hand-holding-all! — anxieties-surrendered! — *"affair-to-God,"* 40:44's *ufawwiḍu-amrī*!); powers-relativized (tyrants'-hands-trembling-in-Hand! — 39:67's grasp!; own-hands-weakness-confessed — strength-borrowed!); provision-trusted (*malakūt*-owned-provided! — *"in-heaven-provision,"* 51:22!). *Prepare Return* (*ilayhi-turjaʿūn*!): returning-consciously (daily-returns — prayers-five-mini-returns!; nightly-returns — sleep-death-practice, v. 37!; yearly-returns — Ramaḍān-intensified!); returning-beautifully (*irjiʿī-rāḍiyatan-marḍiyyah*, 89:28 — pleased-pleasing! — deeds-beautified!); returning-longed (*shawq* — meeting-loved, v. 51! — *"delight-of-gazing,"* v. 58!). For the verse's final mercy is its *finality* (sūrah-*ends*!): ends-with-*glory* (*subḥāna* — praise-lasting-impression!) + ends-with-*Return* (*turjaʿūn* — purpose-lasting-direction!). *Endings*-remembered (sūrah-recited-over-dying, Introduction! — *turjaʿūn*-whispered-at-deathbeds! — return-eased!). *Beginnings*-from-endings (sūrah-completed → life-commenced-anew! — *ṣirāṭ*-walked! — v. 4!). *Glory* — be-His (*subḥāna*!). *Dominion* — in-Hand (*malakūt*!). *Return* — unto-Him (*turjaʿūn*!). *Yā-Sīn* — completed (83-verses! — heart-pumping!). *Reader* — returning (ongoingly!). *Returned* — welcomed (*Salām!*, v. 58 — God-willing!). *Fa-subḥāna-alladhī-bi-yadihi-malakūtu-kulli-shayʾin-wa-ilayhi-turjaʿūn.* *Āmīn.*
